# TREVOR Hub Dashboard — System State

> **Last rebuilt: 2026-05-16.** CC's current-state reference for the Hub — read top-to-bottom in Phase 0 of every Hub prompt. Hard ceiling: under 300 lines.
> Hub-specific only. Bot-side engineering rules, the feature registry, and recurring bugs live in `/home/trevor/trevor/BEHAVIOR_RULES.md` + `/home/trevor/trevor/CLAUDE.md` — read those; this file does not restate them.
> Prior session history is archived to `docs/SESSION_HISTORY.md` (historical, not active documentation).

---

## Two-Box Topology

> Current-state operating layout (post-migration). TREVOR runs on **two boxes / two Termius tabs**. Some older dated entries below predate the migration and describe the Hub co-residing with the bot on the VM — those are historical records, not the current layout.

- **This box = TrevorHub (the "WSL Hub").** Ghost's PC — `ghost@Ghost`, hostname `Ghost`, tailnet `100.125.247.52`. **One machine, two names:** also reachable as **`trevorhub-wsl`** via Tailscale MagicDNS (same box). Runs the **Hub dashboard** (Next.js 15) at `/home/ghost/projects/trevor-dashboard`. This is Ghost's default cockpit — monitoring, audits, recon, dashboard work.
- **The other box = the VM.** `trevor@trevor-prime`, **GCP e2-standard-2** (8 GB RAM, 2 vCPU), tailnet `100.93.113.117`, domain **`trevor-prime.com`**. Runs the **live bot** (`trevor.service`), the exit engine, signals, and the live **`trevor.db`**. All bot code lives here — bot repo `/home/trevor/trevor`, branch `main`.
- **The pipe (`ssh vm`).** From this WSL tab, **`ssh vm`** reaches the VM (login user `ghost`, passwordless sudo, trevor-group). Use it **read-only** — "run a command on the VM, read the result": audits, status, recon. **NOT** for editing VM files over the pipe; bot-file edits run in the **VM tab** directly.
- **The live `trevor.db` lives on the VM — never on this PC.** Network SQLite = corruption + latency risk. Dashboard reads reach it through the **gateway/pipe**, not a local DB copy.

---

## Tech Stack

- **Next.js 15.3.3** (App Router) · **React 19.0.0** · **TypeScript 5.9.3** · **Tailwind CSS 4** (CSS-first; design tokens live in `globals.css @theme`).
- Repo: `/home/trevor/trevor-dashboard/`, git branch **`master`**.
- Served by a custom **`server.js`** — `npm start` runs `node server.js` (*not* `next start`) on port **3333**, bound to `127.0.0.1` only. Dev server: `npm run dev` (`next dev -H 127.0.0.1 -p 3333`).
- **nginx** reverse proxy (ports 80/443, Let's Encrypt SSL) → **https://trevor-prime.com**. `middleware.ts` 301-redirects direct-IP hits to the domain.
- systemd unit: **`trevor-dashboard.service`**.
- Auth: a single cookie `trevor_session` — **HMAC-SHA256-signed, expiring token** (`base64url(JSON{u,iat,exp}) . base64url(HMAC)`, key = **env-only `SESSION_SALT`**, 7-day `exp`), verified server-side (signature + expiry, constant-time compare) in `middleware.ts` + `/api/auth` via the shared `src/lib/session-token.ts` — no network call. **Fail-closed** if `SESSION_SALT` is unset/short (no default). (W-D-P1, 2026-06-07 — replaced the legacy reversible `user:pass:salt` token + the hardcoded `trevor-mc-2025` fallback.)
- `layout.tsx` wraps every page in `<ThemeProvider>` + `<AppShell>`. (The Hub chat UI + `/api/chat/*` routes were removed — B2 2026-06-28 + DEADCODE-B1 2026-06-29; the Hub has no chat surface.)
- The Hub reads the bot's SQLite **read-only** at `/home/trevor/trevor/trevor.db` (the litestream replica, overwritten atomically every tailsync cycle — never write there). Local file state lives in `data/` (`chat-sessions/`, `watchlist-meta.json`). **Hub-owned writable state now also lives in `data/hub.db`** (its first own DB, WAL, gitignored like the replica): created by `scripts/db/apply_hub_migration.py` from numbered `migrations/*.sql`, tracked in a `_migrations` table. First table: **`cost_snapshots`** (C1, 2026-06-22) — caches daily GCP cost data (per `snapshot_date`/`service`/`sku`, UNIQUE-keyed for upsert) so the cost-tracker card's history graph reads local cache, never BigQuery on page load; populated by B4. **WSL has no `sqlite3` CLI — all DB ops use python3 stdlib `sqlite3`.**
- Shared code: `src/lib/` (`api-helpers.ts` Python bridge, `feature-flags.ts`, `fetch.ts`, `format.ts`, `navigation.ts`, `a11y.ts`, `anthropic-key.ts`, `utils.ts`) and `src/hooks/` (`useLongPress`, `usePullToRefresh`, `useScrollDirection`).
- The Hub never calls the bot over HTTP — every bot fact comes from reading `trevor.db` and brain files on the shared filesystem.
- Request flow: `middleware.ts` (auth gate) → page or `api/*/route.ts` → `runPython()` → a root-level `*.py` script → `trevor.db`.

## Route Structure

App Router pages under `src/app/`, verified against the filesystem:

| Path | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `/autotrader` | AutoTrader — 3-tab read-only board: **Dashboard** (default) · **Recent** · **Activity**. **Hub landing** — `/` redirects here (Wave B1). Was a 5-tab shell (Wave C1, 2026-05-27); the **Config + Control** sub-tabs + their write components were removed in B1 (2026-06-28, Hub Read-Only Lockdown) — bot config/control is CC-prompt-only now. Killswitch lives in the header/scalper-header/health, not here. |
| `/stocks` | Stock signals + DCA reminders — Stock / DCA sub-tabs (was `/manual`, Wave C2) |
| `/stocks/scout` | SCOUT discovery feed |
| `/intel` | Notes / Shadow / Lessons / Journal (Lessons + Journal moved back from `/docs` 2026-05-19; Similar + Calibration removed and Notes added as default 2026-05-20 — Notes is a client-side notepad, localStorage `trevor-hub-notes`, no API) |
| `/docs` | Downloads file browser with category tabs — single-view zone since Lessons / Journal moved back to `/intel` 2026-05-19; gated `HUB_REDESIGN_DOCS` (ON) |
| `/memory` | Brain / Memory / ChromaDB / Aggressive (System Health sub-tab removed B1, 2026-06-20) |
| `/health` | **Health home** (B4, 2026-06-19) — top-level promotion of `<HealthSection>`: Killswitch + Data-Freshness/Drift + Reconcile + Heartbeat + Sentinels. **The single health home** — the old `/memory?tab=health` deep link now 308-redirects here via `middleware.ts` (B1, 2026-06-20). **B7 (2026-06-22) redesign hierarchy:** `<HealthRollup>` glance pill at the very top → `<AiFindingsPanel>` AI hero directly under it → the system/infra cards below. Two sub-tabs (`health` default, `docs`). |
| `/login` | Cookie auth |
| `/design-system` | A4 primitive showcase (internal) |

- App-level files in `src/app/`: `layout.tsx` (shell), `error.tsx` (error boundary), `not-found.tsx` (404), `globals.css`.
- Every page except `/login` is auth-gated by `middleware.ts`; an unauthenticated page request 302-redirects to `/login?from=<path>`, and unauthenticated `/api/*` calls get a 401.
- Redirect-only pages: `/` → `/autotrader`; `/brain` → `/control` (chains through to `/memory`).
- Legacy redirects in `middleware.ts` (308): `/trading`, `/scalp` & `/manual` → `/stocks` (Wave C2 — direct single hop, no chaining), `/command` → `/memory`, `/intelligence` → `/intel`, `/dashboard` → `/autotrader` (HOME page retired, Wave B1).
- Legacy redirects in `next.config.ts` (308): `/trades` `/holdings` `/signals` `/research` `/training` `/control` `/ghost` `/dev-tasks` `/reminders` → a retired zone path + `?tab=` (then re-chained by `middleware.ts`); plus 3 `/api/auto-trader/*` → `/api/auto/*`.
- Sub-tabs are `?tab=` query params only — no nested routes except `/stocks/scout`. Per zone: autotrader → `dashboard`/`recent`/`activity` (`dashboard` default; `config`+`control` removed in B1 2026-06-28); stocks → `stock`/`dca`; intel → `notes`/`shadow`/`lessons`/`journal` (`notes` default); memory → `brain`/`memory`/`chroma`/`aggressive` (B1 removed the duplicate `health` sub-tab → `/memory?tab=health` 308-redirects to `/health`). (docs is single-view — no zone-level sub-tabs; docs has its own internal category tab strip inside the Downloads section from Wave B1.)

## API Routes

84 `route.ts` files under `src/app/api/`, grouped by zone (Wave E2 deleted 7 manual-scalp routes; Wave RM-AH-E2 deleted 3 duplicate/dead routes; B3 added `auto/exit-controls` 2026-05-27; DEADCODE-B1 2026-06-29 deleted the 4 dead `chat/*` routes):

- **Dashboard** — `status`, `live`, `dashboard/calibration`, `stats/daily-pnl`, `heartbeat`, `prices`, `nav-badges`, `hub/drift-state` (B4 — READ-ONLY live-heartbeat-vs-replica open-count drift + replica age; both reads error-handled, never 500). (Wave B1 dropped the `/dashboard` page and its `active`/`pnl`/`edge`/`quick-stats` API routes. `dashboard/calibration` is kept but now orphaned — its sole consumer, the SCALP calibration section, was deleted in Wave C2; retained pending a cleanup wave.)
- **AutoTrader** — `auto/{state,config,trades,exit-controls,profit-risk,leverage-regime}`, `capital`, `circuit-breaker`, `entry-preflight`, `exit-signals`, `analytics/{confidence-tiers,duration,regime-performance}`, `time-slots`, `trade-stats`. (`auto/profit-risk` — S1-P06, READ-ONLY: per open-trade exit posture [breakeven armed?, ratchet floor R, partials taken + realized partial P&L, intended risk $/%] + consolidated circuit-breaker state [entries_allowed?, active breakers, each breaker's reading vs limit]. Backed by `query_profit_risk.py` which reads `auto_trades` (sqlite RO) + `circuit_breaker.CircuitBreakerSystem().get_status()`; 10s in-route cache, fail-safe shape. Never mutates / sends bot commands.)
- **Manual / Trades** — `signals/unread-count`, `trades` + `trades/{close,close-status,command-status,edit-entry,flip}`, `watchlist`, `reminders`, `dca`. (Wave E2 deleted the `signals` parent route, `live-board` + `live-board/{enter,ticker}`, and `trades/{add-position,partial-close,tranches}` with the manual-scalp helper teardown — `signals/unread-count` kept, it backs the nav unread badges.)
- **Intel** — `intel/{lessons,journal,shadow,downloads}`, `journal`, `quality`, `training`. (Wave 2026-05-20 deleted `intel/similar/[source]/[id]` and `intel/calibration`; Wave 2026-05-26 deleted `intel/optuna` with the Optuna A/B removal; Notes is client-side and has no API.)
- **Docs** — `docs/categories` (GET list + POST create), `docs/categories/[id]` (PUT rename / DELETE), `docs/categories/reorder` (PUT), `docs/downloads/[filename]/move` (PUT). Downloads category/folder system — all route through `query_docs_categories.py` → bot-side `download_manager` (backend, 2026-05-19; frontend lands in B1).
- **Memory** — `memory/{brain,chroma,daily,health,journal,aggressive,autotrader-toggle}`, `brain`, `system-health`, `aggressive`.
- **Control / Admin** — `killswitch`, `admin/{current-state,reset-capital,reset-pnl-stats}`, `feature-flags`, `commits`, `auth`, `health`.

Conventions:
- Handlers call `runPython()`, parse its stdout as JSON, and return that. Live-data routes set `export const dynamic = "force-dynamic"` to bypass caching; several wrap the call in try/catch and return a fail-safe JSON shape instead of throwing.
- About 30 of the 70 accept `POST` (writes/actions); the rest are GET-only reads.
- `admin/*` routes are destructive resets (capital / P&L stats / history) — POST-gated, Ghost-only. `commits` reads recent git history for the in-app deploy widget.
- High-traffic reads: `live` (full dashboard payload), `status` (service state + signal counts), `nav-badges` (bottom-nav unread counts), `heartbeat` (Observatory pulse).
- Dynamic segments: `intel/journal/[source]/[id]`, `memory/brain/[name]`, `memory/daily/[date]`, `quality/[id]`, `intel/downloads/[filename]`, `docs/categories/[id]`, `docs/downloads/[filename]/move`.

## Python Helper Scripts

The Hub has no direct SQLite binding — every DB read/write goes through Python scripts at the repo root. Verified counts:

- **`query_*.py` × 41** (was 43; QUAL-05 2026-06-03 deleted the orphan `query_similar_trades.py` + `query_calibration_deep.py`, also untracked from the bot's `TRACKED_HELPERS`) — MOSTLY read-only (`trevor.db` opened `mode=ro`); Wave E2 deleted 6 manual-scalp query helpers; S2-P05 added `query_leverage_regime.py`. Notable: `query_brain.py`, `query_training.py`, `query_feature_flags.py` (the last backs `/api/feature-flags`). **⚠️ NOT all `query_*.py` are read-only** (QUAL-04, 2026-06-03): **9 carry WRITE paths** (open a *separate* rw `sqlite3.connect(db_path)` without `mode=ro`, or delegate to a bot writer) for legitimate action endpoints — `query_trades.py` (DELETE `active_trades`/`trade_outcomes`/`training_learned_outcomes` + UPDATE `training_status` — the trade-delete + training-status feature), `query_close.py` (INSERT `close_requests`), `query_edit_entry.py` (UPDATE `active_trades`), `query_hub_commands.py` (INSERT `hub_commands`), `query_admin_reset.py` (INSERT `capital_resets` + REPLACE `trevor_config`), `query_capital.py` (REPLACE `trevor_config`), `query_quality.py` (UPDATE `quality_patterns`), `query_journal_narrative.py` (INSERT/DELETE `trade_journal` + UPDATE `auto_config`), `query_chat_budget.py` (UPDATE/INSERT `auto_config`). Don't assume a `query_*.py` is read-only — check.
- **`set_*.py` × 5** — WRITE: `set_killswitch.py`, `set_aggressive.py`, `set_autotrader_enabled.py`, `set_dca.py`, `set_reminders.py`.
- **`write_*.py` × 2** — WRITE: `write_brain_file.py`, `write_chat_log.py`.
- **`manage_*.py` / `chat_*.py`** — `manage_{brain,portfolio,schedule,watchlist}.py`, `chat_bridge.py`, `chat_ai.py`.

Invoked from `src/lib/api-helpers.ts`:
- **`runPython(script, args)`** — `spawnSync` with an argv array: **no shell, no string interpolation**, so user input is injection-safe (it never reaches a shell). Synchronous — **never `await` it**. Default timeout 15 s, `maxBuffer` 10 MB; runs with `cwd=/home/trevor/trevor` and `HOME=/home/trevor`.
- **`runPythonInline(code)`** — runs a snippet via `python3 -` (stdin); untrusted values must be passed through the env map, never interpolated into the code string.
- A non-zero exit becomes a thrown `Error` carrying the first 500 chars of stderr; scripts must print one JSON object to stdout. Python binary: `/home/trevor/trevor/venv/bin/python3`.

## Design System

- A4 tokens live in `globals.css` under `@theme inline` — a parallel namespace. **Locked** to extension by surgical A4-revision prompts only (most recent: A4 v1.1 Hub Design Foundation, 2026-05-21).
- A4 v1 token families: `--color-*`, `--shadow-glow-*`, `--radius-*` (incl. `--radius-pill`), `--transition-duration-*` (instant 80 ms / fast 160 ms / medium 240 ms / slow 400 ms), `--breakpoint-*` (`xs` 375 px, `mm` 430 px), `--animate-*`.
- **A4 v1.1 refined family (additive, 2026-05-21).** Parallel to v1 — both render concurrently. Refined accents `--color-accent-{cyan-soft, plum, mint, gold}` with `*-strong` / `*-subtle` siblings (red kept; only `-strong`/`-subtle` added). Subtle glow utilities `--shadow-glow-{subtle, active, focus}` + per-color subtle/active glows (~30-40% of v1 neon intensity). Geist Sans loaded via `next/font/google` in `layout.tsx` — `--font-sans` token → Tailwind `font-sans` utility. Opt-in type utilities `.text-{label,heading,h1,h2,prose,caption}-ui`. Tab primitive (`tab-bar-v2.tsx`) carries a 28px mask-image edge-fade on overflow + refined active-tab visual. New card utility classes `.card-{base,elevated,active,warn}`. `<Pill>` primitive gains `intent` prop (`blue-chip` / `mid-cap` / `meme` / `live` / `active` / `running` / `warn` / `error`). **One-page reference: `src/styles/design-tokens.md`.** Revamp complete end-to-end as of 2026-05-21 (A1 foundation → B1 Auto+Stocks+Docs → B2 Intel+Memory+Chat+Header → C1 login+password+nav+cross-page+mobile). Body font default stays mono (sans is opt-in per surface).
- **Standing aesthetic preference (confirmed 2026-05-21 C1).** Hub aesthetic is **refined dark cyber** — desaturated accents (~50% saturation), subtle glows (~30-40% of original neon intensity), mono for data (prices, P&L, timestamps, tickers, hashes), sans-serif for UI labels (nav, tabs, buttons, headings, prose, form labels), professional polish over arcade neon. Refined cyan-soft is the default UI accent; refined mint = financial green / LIVE / ACTIVE / RUNNING; refined plum substitutes magenta for restrained mid-cap / meme tiers; refined gold replaces electric amber for non-critical warnings; red stays unchanged (critical attention only). Future Hub work follows this aesthetic by default — surfaces should opt into refined tokens, not legacy v1 electric tokens.
- Legacy tokens stay under `:root` (`--background`, `--card`, `--neon-*`, `--glow-*`, `--sidebar`) and still drive pre-A4 components; all namespaces coexist deliberately.
- Cyberpunk palette (A4 v1, still live): primary/brand **cyan `#00f0ff`**; neon accents — green `#00ff88`, magenta `#ff00ff`, amber `#ffaa00`, red `#ff3366`, violet `#b478ff`. Backgrounds `#0a0a0f` / `#12121a`. The `html.oled` class swaps to true-black.
- Fonts: **JetBrains Mono** (`@font-face` from gstatic, weights 400/700, `--font-mono`) — body default; **Geist Sans** (`next/font/google`, variable, `--font-sans`) — opt-in for UI labels/headings/prose.
- **19 `@keyframes`** in `globals.css` — legacy (`shake`, `pulseLive`, `scanline`, `ticker-scroll`, `quickJumpIn`, …) and A4 (`pulse-cyan/amber/green/magenta`, `shimmer-ds`, `slide-up`, `fade-in`, `slide-up-spring`, `slide-down-spring`, `scrim-fade-in`).
- **20 primitives** in `src/components/ui/`, including trading-domain ones — `direction-badge`, `money-text`, `confidence-bar`, `killswitch-pill`, `live-pulse`. `tw-animate-css` is imported; dark styling uses the `@custom-variant dark` selector.
- `/design-system` renders every `ui/` primitive live — review it before building a new one. Mobile-interaction hooks: `useLongPress`, `usePullToRefresh`, `useScrollDirection`.
- The "live data" affordance is `live-pulse` + the `pulse-*` keyframes — reuse it rather than rolling a new indicator.
- Mobile-first — verify every layout at **375 px** (the `xs` breakpoint) before calling it done.
- Bottom nav: 4 zones — Auto, Intel, Docs, Memory (Wave B3 dropped the HOME slot; Wave C2 renamed Manual→Stocks; Wave D3 added the Docs slot; **Stocks zone removed 2026-06-19, Stock+DCA removal** → 5→4 zones). Chat is a floating action button, not a tab.

## Feature Flags

`HUB_REDESIGN_*` flags are rows in the `auto_config` DB table. The Hub reads them via `/api/feature-flags` → `query_feature_flags.py` (fail-safe: any error → all-off → old layout). A flag flip is a direct `auto_config` write — picked up within seconds on the next read, no restart needed. The `hub_redesign_override=` cookie lets Ghost preview a flag per-session without flipping the DB (format: `hub_redesign_override=HUB_REDESIGN_CHAT=true`, comma-separate multiple).

Live values, verified from `auto_config` on 2026-05-16:

| Flag | Value | Gates |
|---|---|---|
| `HUB_REDESIGN_MODE` | `false` | master switch for the whole redesign |
| `HUB_REDESIGN_NAV` | `true` | redesigned nav shell (sidebar + bottom bar) |
| `HUB_REDESIGN_DASHBOARD` | `true` | rebuilt `/dashboard` |
| `HUB_REDESIGN_AUTO` | `true` | AutoTrader v2 (`autotrader-v2/`) |
| `HUB_REDESIGN_AUTO_API` | `true` | consolidated `/api/auto/*` endpoints |
| `HUB_REDESIGN_SCALP` | `true` | gates the `/stocks` page — legacy flag name kept (Wave C2 renamed the zone, not the inert `auto_config` key; additive-DB rule) |
| `HUB_REDESIGN_INTEL` | `true` | rebuilt `/intel` sections |
| `HUB_REDESIGN_MEMORY` | `true` | rebuilt `/memory` sections |
| `HUB_REDESIGN_CHAT` | `false` | new chat surface |
| `HUB_REDESIGN_DOCS` | `true` | gates the `/docs` page — Downloads / Lessons / Journal live (Wave D2) |
| `SCOUT_V3_FEED` | `true` | SCOUT discovery feed v3 |

Eight wave flags are live; `MODE` (master) and `CHAT` remain off. Note: `feature-flags.ts:readFlag()` is a leftover stub that always returns `false` — `/api/feature-flags` is the real source.

## Deploy Checklist

Commit first — explicit `git add <files>` (never `-A`) on branch `master` — then:

1. `npm run build` (`next build`) — must succeed.
2. `npx tsc --noEmit` — must pass.
3. `sudo systemctl restart trevor-dashboard.service` — **required after every build.** The running Next.js server caches its build manifest in memory; rebuilding without a restart leaves it serving HTML that references deleted fingerprinted CSS/JS → 400/404 on every asset, pages render unstyled. After the restart, check `journalctl -u trevor-dashboard.service -n 30` for boot errors before moving on.
4. `bash verify_deploy.sh` — ~50 smoke checks: service health, page routes, API GETs, the brain-write 423 gate, the unauth 401 gate, chat-budget gate, recurring-bug canaries, litestream. It exits non-zero if any check fails, and still probes the retired `ghost-qa.service`, so expect one pre-existing FAIL — judge the run on the rest.
5. Confirm the change live at https://trevor-prime.com.

Ad-hoc route test (auth is a cookie):
```bash
curl -s -c /tmp/c -X POST localhost:3333/api/auth -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  -d "{\"action\":\"login\",\"username\":\"trevor\",\"password\":\"$(grep ^DASHBOARD_PASS= .env.local|cut -d= -f2-)\"}"
curl -s -b /tmp/c localhost:3333/api/status
```

No service restarts mid-task — restart only at step 3, and only with Ghost approval.

## Known Issues

- `feature-flags.ts:readFlag()` is a stub that always returns `false`; the real flag source is `/api/feature-flags`. Components must not call `readFlag()` directly.
- `src/app/api/system-health/route.ts` killswitch readout reads `auto_config.EMERGENCY_KILLSWITCH` (canonical state). The historical dead `.kill_switch` flat-file read was removed by RM-AH-P03 (commit `28daed0`) and again hardened by QUAL-01 (2026-06-03) to **fail SAFE** — on a DB read error it reports `{"active": null, "status": "unknown"}`, never a false `active: false` all-clear. (Prior note here claimed `route.ts:81` still read the flat file — that was stale; the flat file no longer exists and nothing reads it.) Note: no Hub component currently consumes this route's `kill_switch` field — the `/memory?tab=health` killswitch tile is sourced from `/api/killswitch` (`<KillswitchControlCard>`) + `/api/heartbeat` (`<HeartbeatView>`).
- `next.config.ts` legacy redirects target retired zone names (`/trading`, `/intelligence`, `/command`) and only resolve because `middleware.ts` re-chains them — editing either file alone can silently break legacy links.
- `verify_deploy.sh` still probes `ghost-qa.service` (retired) and the pre-rename `/scalp?tab=*` paths — the service check is a pre-existing FAIL; the page checks pass only because the 308 redirect is accepted.
- `verify_deploy.sh` briefly force-mutates two `auto_config` rows (`HUB_BRAIN_EDIT_ENABLED`, `ANTHROPIC_API_DAILY_TOKENS_USED`) during its gate tests and restores them — don't run it alongside a real config edit.
- `middleware.ts` hardcodes the VM IP `34.28.231.36` for the direct-IP→domain redirect; a VM IP change silently breaks it.
- `/chat` injects a page-scoped `<style>` block overriding the global theme (Termius-blue) — a deliberate zone-local exception to the design tokens.
- `change-password-modal.tsx` is present but historically buggy (field-name mismatch) — verify before relying on it.
- `tsconfig.tsbuildinfo` and `.env.local` are perpetually modified in `git status`; the repo root also carries stale `*.bak` files — never stage any of them in a selective commit.

## Hub-Specific Rules

- **`trevor.db` belongs to the bot; the Hub reads it `mode=ro` but DOES write a defined set of tables** through dedicated action helpers (the `set_*.py`/`write_*.py`/`manage_*.py` writers + the 9 writing `query_*.py` above + one inline TS writer in `trades/route.ts`). The reconciled real **write surface = ~18 `trevor.db` tables** (QUAL-04, 2026-06-03 — supersedes the old "writes happen only through ~30 POST routes / treat everything else read-only" framing, which understated it): **`auto_config`** (flags/killswitch via the bot module/aggressive/toggles/budgets) · **`autotrader_state_audit`** · **`change_log`** (audit envelope via `audit_logger`) · **`aggressive_mode_history`** · **`hub_commands`** · **`active_trades`** (margin edit / entry edit / delete-closed) · **`close_requests`** · **`trade_outcomes`** · **`training_learned_outcomes`** · **`trade_journal`** · **`capital_resets`** · **`trevor_config`** · **`quality_patterns`** · **`portfolio_positions`** · **`watchlist`** · **`brain_edit_audit`** · **`chat_sessions`** · **`chat_messages`** — plus `dca_schedule` (via `DCAManager`) + reminders (via `set_reminders`). All are legitimate user/action features; **no illegitimate write exists** (the DELETEs are row-level delete-feature ops, NOT destructive *schema* edits, so the additive-schema rule below still holds). Every OTHER route stays read-only; new writes need a dedicated helper + this list updated.
- **WAL ownership:** litestream/writer owns checkpointing; the Hub **never** runs `wal_checkpoint` and opens all *reads* `mode=ro` (its writes use short-lived rw connections through the helpers above — "read-only on `trevor.db`" here means **checkpoint-ownership + reads**, not "no writes"). WAL bloat / TRUNCATE-blocked cadence is a writer/litestream concern (bot repo, RM-SYS) — not a Hub fix.
- **Read-replica is SELF-REFRESHING, never frozen (W-H-P2-HUB, 2026-06-09).** `/home/ghost/trevor-replica/trevor.db` is **continuously restored from GCS**: `trevor-restore.timer` → `trevor-restore.service` (oneshot, `deploy/scripts/trevor-restore.sh`) runs `litestream restore` from `gcs://trevor-prime-backups/litestream/trevor.db` every ~15 min (`OnUnitInactiveSec`; a full restore ≈8–14 min, so effective freshness ~20–30 min), publishing a self-contained **DELETE-mode** file via atomic `mv` — the Hub's per-request `mode=ro` opens pick it up with **no restart**. **One-way, pull-only** (GCS→WSL); it NEVER pushes. The old `litestream.service` is a *replicate*/PUSH unit — now **disabled + masked**, and `litestream.yml` renamed `…DISABLED-replicate-DANGER`, so nothing can fork the VM's live GCS generation. The script's `wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)` runs **only on the throwaway staging copy** (to publish one clean file) — it does NOT touch the live read path or the VM/GCS chain, so the "Hub never checkpoints" rule above still holds. Efficiency win (shrink each restore to seconds) = shorten the **VM-side** litestream `snapshot-interval` — a separate, VM-side follow-up, NOT done here. Runbook: `docs/hub_replica_continuous_restore_2026_06_09.md`.
- **GCS backup retention TRIMMED 30d→7d (B2, 2026-06-22).** `gs://trevor-prime-backups` now keeps **7 days** of DR backups (was 30d). Two GCS-side changes via `ssh vm gsutil` (NOT litestream config): (1) bucket **retention policy** lowered 30d→7d (was UNLOCKED; it had been forcing 30d on *everything* incl. litestream, which is why litestream sat at 10.5 GiB despite its own 7d config); (2) **lifecycle** = `Delete @ age 7d` **scoped to `daily/`+`nightly/` only** via `matchesPrefix`, plus a 3d noncurrent-version cleanup — the **`litestream/` prefix is deliberately EXCLUDED** from the lifecycle (litestream self-manages its 168h/7d retention; putting a raw-age bucket lifecycle on the replica path risks severing the restore chain). JSON artifact: `deploy/gcs/lifecycle-7d.json` (apply: `cat … | ssh vm 'cat > /tmp/lc.json && gsutil lifecycle set /tmp/lc.json gs://trevor-prime-backups'`). The read/restore path (bullet above) is **unaffected** — restore reads the latest litestream generation (6h snapshots), always within the 7d window; `litestream.yml` was **not touched**. **Rollback to 30d** (forward-only): `ssh vm 'gsutil retention set 30d gs://trevor-prime-backups'` + re-set lifecycle `age:30` — rollback restores retention *going forward only*; backups already aged out cannot be recovered. **⚠ COST CAVEAT:** NEARLINE has a **30-day minimum storage duration**, so objects deleted at 7d are still billed the full 30d → storage-$ savings from this trim ≈ **near-zero**; it shrinks the DR *window/footprint*, not the bill. The real cost lever is a **NEARLINE→STANDARD class change** (no min-duration penalty) — **DEFERRED to a separate prompt**.
- **Equity "STALE" badge is an AGE check, not the SWR flag (W-H-P2-HUB, 2026-06-09).** `/api/auto/state` `equity_stale` = `Date.now() - equity_ts > 60s` (was the per-call SWR `stale` flag, which was permanently `true` because the equity cache TTL 10s < client poll 15s). Clears under the normal 15s poll (value stays <60s old); still flags when the heartbeat is genuinely stale >60s, showing the last-known value. `src/app/api/auto/state/route.ts`.
- **The W-H-P2 badge fix was committed but NEVER DEPLOYED — re-fixed by W-H-P3-HUB (2026-06-09).** Why the badge "survived Fix 2": the running Hub served a `.next` build from **13:14:45**, but the age-fix commit `ad6eaad` landed **13:23:07** (8 min later) and nobody ran `npm run build` + restarted. The deployed artifact still had the always-true per-call flag (the source was already correct). **Durable lesson: a Hub *source* fix is inert until `npm run build` AND `sudo systemctl restart trevor-dashboard.service` — verify against the LIVE endpoint at 15s cadence, never just `tsc`/a synthetic check (the P2 synthetic check passed while the bug shipped).** P3 rebuilt+restarted; the badge is now proven `equity_stale:false` at cadence on the live Hub (`ts` advancing), and the genuine-stale path (>60s un-refreshed → `true`) was observed firing live.
- **Floating PnL now mirrors real HL via the heartbeat (W-H-P3-HUB).** The Hub has no HL creds, so `query_auto_state.py:_fetch_hl()` always returns 0 unrealized → the "floating" sub-line on `capital-hero.tsx` showed **$0 even with an open position**. `route.ts:resolveRealHlEquity()` now also reads `categories.autotrader.unrealized_pnl_usd` from the SAME heartbeat (best-effort; absent/flat ⇒ null) and the response computes `unrealized_usd = equity.unrealized ?? value.unrealized_usd`. Same pattern/feed as `account_value_usd`. **Equity-value cadence caveat:** the account value itself is the latest `equity_snapshots.equity` (VM monitor-loop, **~hourly** — see observatory `collect_account_value()`), so the displayed value mirrors real HL only to ~1h granularity; the badge's "fresh" means "the Hub is reading the heartbeat fresh", NOT "tick-by-tick HL". Real-time equity = a separate VM-side follow-up. **Every Hub number was audited against live HL `user_state` + VM `trevor.db` on 2026-06-09 (W-H-P3-HUB) — all match source-of-truth within the published replica (≤15min) and equity-snapshot (~1h) cadences.**
- **Live/open Hub numbers source from the heartbeat; closed history from the replica (W-H-P4-HUB, 2026-06-09).** Cleared the remaining read-side defects from the full HL-mirror audit (display-accuracy only; money path untouched):
  - **Margin utilization (`/api/auto/leverage-regime`) is derived from the heartbeat, not a live HL call.** The Hub has no `hyperliquid` module, so `query_leverage_regime.py:fetch_margin()`'s `info.user_state` read ALWAYS threw `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'hyperliquid'` → the panel was pinned to "margin read degraded". `fetch_margin()` is now a no-op sentinel (no HL import); `route.ts:resolveHeartbeatMargin()` rebuilds the block from `account_value_usd` + per-open-position `notional_usd`/`leverage`: `total_ntl_pos = Σ notional`, `margin_used = Σ(notional/leverage)` (isolated initial margin — the bot's mode), `utilization_pct = margin_used/account_value`. Flat ⇒ 0% / $acct. Live-proven `available:true`, 0% flat. **Do NOT install `hyperliquid` on WSL — the heartbeat is the source.**
  - **`/api/prices` repointed off the dead `:3335` WS.** W-E-P2b moved the Observatory to `:8443`, which serves `/api/heartbeat` but NOT prices/allMids (verified 404), so the `tryWsPrices`/`:3335` path was dead — REMOVED. The 30s HL-REST→CG SWR cache is now the sole/primary source (tracks live HL within ~0.01–0.04%). Per-ticker `stale` is now **age-based** (`Date.now()-ts > PRICE_STALE_AGE_MS = 90s`), not the SWR per-call flag — kills the false-stale blip that fired on the one poll per 30s window landing on the TTL boundary. Live-proven 0/20 polls stale across a boundary crossing. (The audit's "stale:true ALWAYS" was an isolated-sampling artifact; under the real 2s cadence it was already mostly false — the real defects were the dead path + the boundary blip.)
  - **Open-count / open-positions-count / deployed (`open_exposure_usd`) now come from the heartbeat** in `/api/auto/state` (live `categories.autotrader.open_count` + `Σ open_positions[].notional_usd`), NOT the ≤15min litestream replica `query_auto_state.py` reads — kills the transient phantom open (#100444 / $4.33 deployed) that lingered for one restore window after a close. `resolveRealHlEquity()` was extended to carry the open numbers from the SAME heartbeat fetch (one call). **Closed history stays on the replica** (`realized`/`realized_count`/`realized_pct`/`realized_unknown_count`/`trades_total` — fine at 15min). Live-proven 0/0/$0, matching the heartbeat.
  - **`trades_today` = closed-LIVE trades, ET-calendar** (the dashboard canonical = `query_auto_state.py` `realized_count.today`) — this was ALREADY ET-correct (no boundary bug on the dashboard). The memory/health page (`heartbeat-view.tsx`) showed the heartbeat's broader `trades_today` (ALL modes, **UTC day**, incl null-pnl) under the same "Trades today" label → looked like a contradiction (Hub 11/12 vs heartbeat 22/23). Fixed by **relabeling** the heartbeat count to "Closed today · all modes (UTC)" so it reads as a distinct diagnostic, not the same metric. The collector (VM) is untouched.
  - **DURABLE PREFERENCE: Hub LIVE/OPEN numbers (equity, floating, margin, open-count, deployed) source from the Observatory heartbeat; CLOSED history + realized totals stay on the litestream replica.** The heartbeat is live (the VM collector reads the real `trevor.db` each collect); the replica lags ≤15min. Never read an open/live number off the replica (phantom risk); never expect tick-by-tick freshness from the replica.
  - Files: `src/app/api/auto/leverage-regime/route.ts`, `query_leverage_regime.py`, `src/app/api/prices/route.ts`, `src/app/api/auto/state/route.ts`, `src/components/memory/heartbeat-view.tsx`. Rebuilt + restarted `trevor-dashboard.service` (BUILD_ID `NY4mg6y0yKd1dN0HObgPu`). Full HL-mirror audit **closed 2026-06-09**.
- DB changes are **additive** — new rows / new `auto_config` keys, never destructive schema edits. (The only new table in flight is **`gateway_idempotency`** — created `IF NOT EXISTS` on the VM by the W-C-P2a VM gateway.)
- **Dangerous writes go through the two-hop write-gateway (W-C-P2a), not the replica.** The 18 financial/destructive/state routes call `src/lib/gateway-client.ts` → local gateway (`:3939`) → Tailscale → VM gateway → bot helper → live `trevor.db`. The client sends an **op name** (allowlist in `gateway/ops.js`) + validated args, never SQL or a helper path. Flag check + mandatory `change_log` audit + idempotency are enforced **VM-side**. Fail modes: 423 locked · 502 VM unreachable · 504 timeout — **no route returns a raw readonly-DB 500 anymore**. Until the VM gateway is deployed, these writes fail CLOSED at 502/504. Transport is **Tailscale-only** (no nginx/public/non-tailnet fallback). New write surfaces must add an op (both gateways) + keep route-level validation.
- **Gateway health probes (2026-06-07):** unauthenticated **`GET /healthz`** → `200 {"status":"ok"}` (liveness only, no token, reveals nothing else); authed **`GET /gateway/health`** still reports `write_route`/`ops`/`writes_enabled` (bearer required).
- **Benign writes (W-C-P2b, 2026-06-07) — Hub half wired, VM op-map PENDING.** The last 4 benign routes now route writes through the gateway instead of the read-only replica: `/api/trades` PUT→`trades.annotate` (trade_outcomes notes), `/api/reminders`→`reminders.set`, `/api/chat/stream`→`chat.log_user`/`chat.log_assistant`, `/api/intel/journal`→`journal.generate`. Journal **GET is cache-read-only** (generation is POST-only); `query_chat_budget.py` is now **pure-read** (the daily-rollover reset is VM-owned + reaches the replica via litestream — the Hub must never write it). All 5 ops ride **`HUB_BENIGN_WRITE_ENABLED`** (default OFF). **VM follow-up REQUIRED:** add the 5 ops + op→helper map to `vm_gateway.js`, create the flag row, widen the VM forward timeout for `journal.generate` (Haiku call > the 8s `VM_FORWARD_TIMEOUT_MS`). Until it lands these routes return 400/502 (explicit-fail — was silent replica-write fail).
- Surgical edits only — change what the prompt asks and nothing more; diagnose before you touch.
- Verify after every change — build, restart, hit the route — before reporting it done.
- `HUB_REDESIGN_*` and other `auto_config` flags are Ghost-controlled rollout switches — never flip one without explicit direction.
- **Write-gateway enable flags (W-C-P2a, 2026-06-06) are `auto_config` rows too, default OFF (absent or != 'true'):** `HUB_CAPITAL_WRITE_ENABLED` (capital + pnl-stats writes) · `HUB_TRADE_EDIT_ENABLED` (margin/entry edits, flip/close/delete, dca) · `HUB_LIST_WRITE_ENABLED` (quality + watchlist) · **`HUB_BENIGN_WRITE_ENABLED`** (W-C-P2b benign writes: trade notes, reminders, chat logs, journal gen — Hub-wired 2026-06-07, VM op-map pending). The **VM gateway** enforces them authoritatively (`SELECT value FROM auto_config`); a 423 means "locked — flip the row to enable". `killswitch` is ungated (emergency stop). Same governance: never flip without explicit direction.
- **Brain-file edits** go through `/api/memory/brain/[name]` → `write_brain_file.py`, gated by the `HUB_BRAIN_EDIT_ENABLED` flag; the bot's protected brain documents are rejected with HTTP 423.
- **Secrets**: `.env.local` (perms 600) holds `DASHBOARD_USER` / `DASHBOARD_PASS`; `.env` holds `DISCORD_BOT_TOKEN`. Append-only — never overwrite an existing key. `/api/auth` re-reads `.env.local` on every POST, so a password rotation needs no restart.
- **Deliver a file to #downloads from WSL — NON-NEGOTIABLE (use this — never desktop, never ask/guess/scout, never local-only fallback; authoritative contract: `BEHAVIOR_RULES.md` Section 1 → Rule — REPORT DELIVERY CONTRACT).** Canonical entry point: `scripts/deliver_report.py` → `deliver_report(filepath, title=None, description=None)` (CLI: `python3 scripts/deliver_report.py <filepath> [title] [description]`) — a thin wrapper over the primitive `scripts/discord_file_delivery.py` → `post_file_sync(filepath, title=None, description=None, category=None)` that additionally scrubs any webhook URL from error strings. Both POST the file to #downloads then register it via `download_manager.save_download()` so it surfaces in the DOCS zone; both return `{ok, status_code, message_id, filename, manifest_entry, error}`. **Auth:** webhook read from `.env.local` as **`HUB_DOWNLOADS_WEBHOOK_URL`** (env-only, fail-loud, NO fallback) — NEVER print the value. The #downloads channel is **encoded inside the webhook URL**, so this WSL path needs no channel ID. **Don't cross the paths:** the VM has its own separate sender (`download_manager.py`, Discord **bot token** + channel ID `1492922559019225261`); both deliver to the same channel independently with no cross-fallback.
- **Never modify files under `/home/trevor/trevor/`** from a Hub prompt — only read its `trevor.db`.
- Bot-side engineering rules — file protection, deploy discipline, the honesty protocol — live in `/home/trevor/trevor/BEHAVIOR_RULES.md` + `/home/trevor/trevor/CLAUDE.md`. Cross-reference them; do not copy them here.
- Honesty protocol applies to Hub work too: never claim done / fixed / working without pasted command output.

## Parallel safety: file locks (use these — worktrees retired 2026-06-16)

- Before editing a file: `scripts/locks/with_file_lock.sh <path> -- <edit command>` — acquire → run → release (releases even on failure/signal).
- To commit: `scripts/locks/git_commit_serialized.sh -- "git add <specific-file> && git commit -m '...'"` — **pass the whole compound command as ONE quoted string** so the `add`+`commit` run together under the global git-index lock. The unquoted `-- git add x && git commit` form would split on `&&` at your own shell and run the commit OUTSIDE the lock. One committer at a time; bare `git add .` / `-A` / `--all` is refused (even behind `git -C <dir>`).
- Locks live in `.locks/` (gitignored, native ext4 only). Stale locks auto-reclaim only when age >15min AND the owner PID is dead (`kill -0`).
- Diagnostic: `scripts/locks/lock_status.sh`. Re-prove every guarantee anytime: `scripts/locks/selftest.sh` (8 live concurrency proofs in a scratch dir; never touches the live repo).
- **No worktrees / no branches** — all prompts run on `master`; the per-file lock + commit serialization prevent collisions. Worktrees retired 2026-06-16; this mirrors the VM LOCK-VM contract so both boxes behave identically.

## Hub Wave Changelog (additive — most recent at top)

### 2026-06-30 — B1 HEARTBEAT-FAILSAFE: make the open-set cache fail SAFE (to null), not STALE — staleness ceiling + log every failed fetch + replica closed-id eviction — BUILT (committed on master, restart pending)
- **Root cause of the −17% HYPE "ghost" (G1) fixed at the source.** A2 proved Hyperliquid was FLAT on HYPE the whole window — the −17% was the Hub's in-memory `heartbeat-open-set` SWR cache serving a CLOSED `auto_trades` row (101112) as still-open, with PnL recomputed client-side off its stale entry price. The cache's failed-refresh path kept serving the last-known set **silently, forever** (the Next process ran continuously, `NRestarts=0`, so the in-memory cache persisted across the incident), so a closed position haunted the ACTIVE card list for hours. This wave makes the cache fail **safe** (to "I don't know" → replica fallback), not **stale** (to "the last thing I saw"). WSL `TrevorHub` (`ghost@Ghost`), `master`, :3000. **Display/cache layer only — READ-ONLY over the replica, no trade-data/schema/`auto_config`/flag/money-path write, no bot/VM touch; the KPI-RECON live-overlay path (2026-06-29) is preserved.** [[hub-no-repo-local-db-stub]]
- **Causes found: 1 confirmed (M2) + 1 adjacent flagged.** M2 (the `heartbeat-open-set` SWR cache, consumed ONLY by `trades/route.ts:?type=open` — the exact surface that rendered the −17% card) is live-confirmed and gets all 3 fixes. M1 (VM Observatory cache) is upstream/untouchable but the Hub eviction kills a closed-id ghost regardless of what the heartbeat emits; M3 (replica fallback) queries `status='open'` so it can never serve a closed row as open; M4 (real reopen) is a genuine open — none are reachable Hub-side ghost emitters. **Flagged follow-up (Ghost-approved out of scope):** `state/route.ts` `equityCache` (key `real-hl-equity`) serves the header's `open_count` as a NUMBER under the SAME silent-keep-stale SWR flaw — but it's a count, not the −17% card, and is already age-flagged (`equity_stale` >60s). The eviction net cannot cover a count (no id-set to evict from); bounded separately, NOT touched this wave.
- **The last-successful-refresh stamp ALREADY EXISTED** — `single-flight.ts` writes the store ONLY on success, and `swr()` returns `SwrResult.ts` = epoch-ms the value was produced = last *successful* refresh. So Phase 2 reads `ts` straight off the `swr()` return; **no new field added.**
- **Fix 1 (Phase 2 — staleness ceiling, `heartbeat-open-set.ts`):** named `OPEN_SET_STALENESS_CEILING_MS = 6 × OPEN_SET_POLL_TTL_MS` (6 × the 10s cache TTL = **60s**). After `swr()`, if `Date.now() − ts > ceiling`, the getter returns **null** (logged) instead of the stale last-known set → caller falls through to the replica. A swallowed-background-refresh stale serve carries an OLD `ts`, so the ceiling catches exactly the silent-keep-stale path. The consumer already treats null = replica fallback (`trades/route.ts:100` → `{...replica, live:false}`, badges REPLICA; no crash) — confirmed, no consumer change needed.
- **Fix 2 (Phase 3 — log every failed fetch):** the incident left no Hub trace. Added `console.warn` at (a) the shared `single-flight.ts` background-refresh swallow (key + error + age of the kept stale value — this lights up failed refreshes for ALL SWR caches, intended), (b) both `heartbeat-open-set.ts` throw sites (HTTP non-200, `open_positions` absent), (c) the cold-miss/unreachable catch, and (d) the ceiling-expiry. Behavior unchanged (still keep-last-good / null); only the silence is gone.
- **Fix 3 (Phase 4 — replica cross-check eviction, `query_closed_ids.py` + `trades/route.ts`):** new `query_closed_ids.py` (repo root, `mode=ro`) takes the candidate ids and returns ONLY those the replica explicitly marks `status='closed'` via ONE batched `SELECT id FROM auto_trades WHERE id IN (?,…) AND status='closed'` (sanctioned dynamic-IN idiom, params bound — injection-safe). In the `?type=open` SWR compute, after building the merged set, `fetchClosedIds()` runs the helper and drops any closed id. **Fails OPEN** (any error → evict nothing; the Phase-2 ceiling is the complementary bound). Belt-and-suspenders with Fix 1: the ceiling kills the ghost when the heartbeat is DEAD >60s; the eviction kills it even while the heartbeat is ALIVE but (wrongly/stalely) reporting a closed id open.
- **Blast radius — does any fix mask a real fast-syncing open position?** No. **Eviction keys on `status='closed'` only — absence ≠ closed**, so the KPI-RECON case (FARTCOIN #101066: live on the heartbeat, not yet on the replica → thin "syncing" card) survives (it is NOT in `closed_ids`). Asserted in the exercise. The ceiling-null path falls through to the replica's `status='open'` query (itself closed-row-free), so even a >60s heartbeat outage shows no ghost; the only cost is a brief under-show of a brand-new not-yet-replicated live position during a sustained outage — the intended fail-safe direction (under-show > ghost).
- **Verification (honesty protocol):** (a) `query_closed_ids.py` — `py_compile` OK; controlled-DB test `closed_ids([open 101065, closed 101112, ABSENT 101066]) == [101112]` (open survives, absent FARTCOIN survives, empty/all-absent → `[]`); real-replica end-to-end via the CLI form `runPython` uses (real closed id 101135 → returned; absent id → `[]`; garbage/no-args → graceful `[]`). (b) Ceiling + log — transpiled the REAL `single-flight.ts` and drove it with an injectable clock: within-ceiling (age 20s<60s) → serves last-known set; the single-flight swallow now logs a warn (silent path KILLED); past-ceiling (age 70s>60s) → **null** + EXPIRING warn; recovery → fresh set served again. **7/7 assertions PASS.** Throwaway harnesses deleted. (c) `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0; `npm run build` exit 0 (✓ Compiled successfully; `/api/auto/trades` + `/autotrader` 12 kB compiled). Replica path `/home/trevor/trevor/trevor.db` → symlink `/home/ghost/trevor-replica/trevor.db` (sqlite 3.45.1, 1150 closed rows) confirmed.
- **Regression posture:** Hub display/cache + read-only-replica only — no bot restart → the bot-side recurring-bug canaries are untouched. No `trevor.db`/schema write (the new helper is `mode=ro` SELECT-only), no `auto_config`/flag write, no money path, no design tokens, no new npm deps, no sacred file (none on the Hub). **Do NOT restart `trevor-dashboard.service`** — the wave-close restart serves all Wave-B changes at once.
- **Lock-guard:** `src/lib/heartbeat-open-set.ts` · `src/lib/single-flight.ts` · `src/app/api/auto/trades/route.ts` · `query_closed_ids.py` · `CLAUDE.md` claimed via `scripts/locks/lock_acquire.sh` (owner `B1-HEARTBEAT-FAILSAFE`); committed via `scripts/locks/git_commit_serialized.sh` on `master` (selective `git add` of only these paths — `tsconfig.tsbuildinfo` NOT staged); locks released after.
- **Files (5):** `src/lib/heartbeat-open-set.ts` (ceiling + throw/cold-miss logs) · `src/lib/single-flight.ts` (swallow log) · `src/app/api/auto/trades/route.ts` (eviction in the open-set compute) · `query_closed_ids.py` (new, `mode=ro` closed-id cross-check) · `CLAUDE.md` (this entry).
- **Preference (2026-06-30):** the Hub `heartbeat-open-set` cache **fails SAFE, not stale** — past `OPEN_SET_STALENESS_CEILING_MS` (6× the 10s TTL = 60s) since the last *successful* refresh the getter returns null → replica fallback, NEVER the last-known set forever. Every failed heartbeat fetch + every swallowed SWR background refresh **logs a warn** (no silent path). The `?type=open` card list **evicts any heartbeat-"open" id the replica marks `status='closed'`** (replica closed-history is authoritative) — but NEVER an id merely absent from the replica (absence ≠ closed → a fast-syncing live position survives). The eviction net covers the ACTIVE card list; the header `open_count` (state/route.ts equityCache) is a separate count, bounded separately. Don't revert the cache to silent-keep-stale or evict on replica-absence.

The `profit-risk`/`leverage-regime` panels stay replica-badged (card detail, not the open count). ### 2026-06-28 — LOGIN-RESET: Hub login lockout fixed — stale stored password rotated (NO auth-read bug) — DEPLOYED
- **Ghost was locked out of the public Funnel Hub; both the intended new password and the old passphrase were rejected at the login form.** WSL `TrevorHub` (`ghost@Ghost`), `master`, :3000. Diagnose-first; **no `auth/route.ts` change, no lockdown touch** — the only edit was the gitignored `.env.local` credential. [[trevorhub-auth-salt]]
- **Root cause = stale stored value, NOT a code bug (Causes found: 1).** Phase-0 `cat -A` showed `DASHBOARD_PASS` was still the OLD passphrase (clean — no hidden char/space/quote/CRLF) and the intended new value was never written (its reset prompt never ran). `getCredentials()` (`auth/route.ts`) reads `.env.local` **per-request** (`force-dynamic` + `readFileSync`), regex `/^DASHBOARD_PASS=(.+)$/m` + `.trim()`, username compare **case-insensitive** / password **exact**, `process.env` only a fallback when the file line is absent (it isn't) — all correct. **Decisive proof:** server-side `curl` with the exact stored OLD value → **HTTP 200** (read/compare path flawless); the intended new value → 401 (never stored). So the lockout was a typed-credential mismatch against a stale stored passphrase — every other candidate (trailing/hidden char · regex/trim · empty `process.env` fallback winning · stale process · case-sensitive user) ruled OUT with evidence, not just the first.
- **Fix = rotate the credential ONLY.** Set `.env.local` `DASHBOARD_PASS` to the agreed new value (single line, no stray chars — `cat -A` verified `…$`), `DASHBOARD_USER=trevor` already clean, perms `600` preserved, **gitignored — never staged, value kept OUT of git** (reported to Ghost in-session, per the FUNNEL passphrase precedent). No `auth/route.ts` edit (no read bug to fix); no stale `process.env` to clear (the file value always wins because the line is present).
- **Verified (full gate, public Funnel URL).** Restarted `trevor-dashboard.service` for certainty (active, NRestarts 0, `[HUB] ready on :3000`, 0 boot errors — though no restart is strictly needed since the read is per-request). From `https://trevorhub-wsl.tail2bf7a3.ts.net/api/auth`: **`trevor`/new-pass → 200**, wrong → 401, empty → 401 (H2 fail-closed holds), `Trevor` (capital) → 200 (case-insensitive user), old passphrase → 401 (correctly retired).
- **Regression posture:** Hub creds-only — no code/route/flag/`auto_config`/DB/schema/money-path touch; the Read-Only Lockdown (B1–B5) + killswitch/auth survivors untouched. The only tracked file edited is this `CLAUDE.md` (lock-guarded); `.env.local` gitignored + unstaged.
- **Preference (2026-06-28):** Hub login rejecting a "correct" password is FIRST a stale/typo'd stored-value check (`grep … .env.local | cat -A` + a server-side `curl` of the exact stored value), NOT an auth-read-bug hunt — `getCredentials()` reads `.env.local` per-request and is correct; rotating `DASHBOARD_PASS` in the gitignored `.env.local` takes effect immediately (no rebuild, restart optional). Never write the password value into git.

### 2026-06-28 — FUNNEL: Hub made PUBLIC via Tailscale Funnel, locked down (login hardened + fail-closed auth) — DEPLOYED
- **The Hub is now reachable on the public internet at `https://trevorhub-wsl.tail2bf7a3.ts.net/`** so Ghost can demo the project — locked down so a stranger who finds the URL hits only the login wall. WSL `TrevorHub` (`ghost@Ghost`), SOLO on `master`, :3000. **Mechanism = Tailscale Funnel** (public HTTPS via Tailscale's edge): proxies ONLY `:3000`, opens **NO** inbound box firewall port, auto Let's Encrypt cert, no nginx/DNS/Let's Encrypt-of-our-own. **Rollback (instant): `sudo tailscale funnel --https=443 off`.** The Hub is on the WSL box, NOT the trading VM → blast radius is already limited.
- **Phase-0 audit verdict: GO after one MEDIUM fix.** Auth is enforced **server-side in Edge `middleware.ts` on every request** (matcher covers all but `_next` static/favicon) via HMAC-SHA256 signed `trevor_session` tokens (`session-token.ts`, 64-char `SESSION_SALT`, fail-closed). Unauth sweep: **all sensitive data GETs → 401, all pages → 307→/login, all action POSTs → 401, zero unauthenticated data/action**. Client bundle (`.next/static`) carries **no secrets** (0 hits for salt/pass/gateway-token/HL-key/wallet/webhooks/`NEXT_PUBLIC_*`). Dangerous writes flow Hub→gateway-client→`:3939`→VM gateway, **flag-gated default-OFF + VM-enforced (423)**; the `:3939` gateway is a separate 127.0.0.1 process Funnel does NOT expose. The one weakness was the login itself.
- **H1 (MEDIUM, fixed) — strong login.** Replaced the 7-char `DASHBOARD_PASS` with a cryptographically-random **4-word + 2-digit passphrase (~28 chars, ~37 bits)** in gitignored `.env.local` (perms `600` preserved, all other keys intact). The passphrase is the single public door — kept OUT of git (not in this file); reported to Ghost in-session. Ghost can swap it anytime (login re-reads `.env.local` per POST; no restart needed).
- **H2 (LOW footgun, fixed) — fail-closed auth.** `/api/auth` previously **auto-logged-in anyone if `DASHBOARD_PASS` was empty** (`if (!pass){issue token}`) — a real open-door risk on a public URL if the env key ever dropped. Now an empty/missing password **refuses ALL logins (401)** with a 500ms delay, never issues a session. Commit `d1c41b4`.
- **SKIPPED rate-limiting** (Ghost decision) — the ~37-bit random passphrase + the existing 500ms per-failure throttle make online brute-force infeasible; deferred defense-in-depth, not needed for safety.
- **Verification (honesty protocol):** `npx tsc --noEmit` 0 + `npm run build` 0; restart clean (NRestarts=0, `[HUB] ready :3000`). H1 live: new passphrase → 200+cookie, wrong/old → 401, authed `/api/status`+`/api/auto/state` → 200, forged cookie → 401, unauth sensitive → 401. **External (off-mesh via public Funnel ingress 209.177.145.97, `--resolve`): `/login` 200 + `ssl_verify=0`** (Let's Encrypt CN=trevorhub-wsl.tail2bf7a3.ts.net, valid→2026-09-14); `/`→307; `/api/health` 200 (liveness only); `/api/live|status|capital|killswitch|auto/state` → 401; `POST /api/killswitch` → 401. Funnel proxies only `/`→:3000; no `0.0.0.0` bind (`:3000`/`:3939` stay 127.0.0.1-only); no host firewall opened (`ufw` not installed; Funnel ingress rides the tailscaled tunnel).
- **Worst case if breached:** a logged-in viewer gets a **read-mostly** dashboard — cannot enable autotrader / change config / size / place / close (write flags OFF + VM-enforced → 423); the only trading-affecting action is the emergency killswitch (fail-safe HALT, never a trade). SSH/VM/gateway never reachable via Funnel.
- **Lock-guard:** `.env.local` (gitignored, NOT committed) + `src/app/api/auth/route.ts` + `CLAUDE.md` each claimed via `scripts/locks/lock_acquire.sh`, released after; route.ts + CLAUDE.md committed via serialized commit on `master` (NOT pushed — held for Ghost). Sacred manifest untouched (none on this box).
- **Files (2 tracked):** `src/app/api/auth/route.ts` (fail-closed `!pass`) · `CLAUDE.md` (this entry). Plus gitignored `.env.local` (new passphrase). Bot/VM untouched.
- **Preference (2026-06-28):** the Hub is **publicly exposed via Tailscale Funnel** (`https://trevorhub-wsl.tail2bf7a3.ts.net`, `:3000`-only, no inbound port). The **login is the single public door** — `DASHBOARD_PASS` must stay a strong (≥16-char) random passphrase; `/api/auth` fails CLOSED on an empty password (never auto-login). Rollback = `sudo tailscale funnel --https=443 off` (reverts to tailnet-only `serve`). Don't expose SSH / the `:3939` gateway / the VM through Funnel — only `:3000`.

### 2026-06-28 — B1: trailing-window cost forecast + run-rate-driven budget alert (kill the front-loaded-spike false alarm) — DEPLOYED
- **The naive linear forecast (`mtd ÷ days_elapsed × days_in_month`) over-projected a FRONT-LOADED spend pattern**, and the budget alert (`src/lib/cost-alert.ts` → `cost_alert_notify.py`) fired off that number. The one-time legacy GCS egress spike (Jun 10–22, ~$106) stopped dead at the Jun 23 cutover, but the linear forecast smeared it across the month → projected **$251** when go-forward is ~$1.5–2.5/day. WSL `TrevorHub` (`ghost@Ghost`), SOLO on `master`, port :3000. **Hub-side cost-display only — no money path, no `trevor.db`/`auto_config`/flag/schema write, no bot file.** [[gcs-cost-spike-egress-rootcause]]
- **⚠️ Ghost decision at the THOUGHTS gate (the forecast fix alone can't drop June <$80):** June's $226 MTD spike is **real spent money sunk in MTD**, not an extrapolation — no honest forecast lands June at ~$50–65 (that's a *clean-month* figure). The naive forecast's actual error for June is only ~$9–18 (it over-projects the last 3 days at the high month-average instead of the recent rate). So **Option 3** was chosen: keep the honest trailing month-end on the card (June ≈ $237, correctly >$80 — June genuinely overspent, do NOT mute it), and **drive the ALERT off the forward run-rate** so an ENDED spike stops re-firing while an ONGOING one still fires.
- **Forecast (`query_cost.py`, the one read both the card + alert share):** replaced the naive `projected = mtd/elapsed×dim` with a trailing-window model emitting three numbers:
  - `projected_month_end_usd` (CARD) = `mtd_actual + trailing_daily_mean × days_remaining` — known elapsed actuals + recent run rate for the days left (honest month-end; the sunk spike stays counted because it was real).
  - `alert_forecast_usd` (ALERT) = `trailing_daily_mean × days_in_month` — forward run-rate projection; an ended spike → run-rate normalizes → stops firing.
  - `forecast_naive_usd` (SHADOW) = the old naive value, emitted only for the side-by-side record.
  - `trailing_daily_mean` = mean per-day NET total over the last **N=7** (`COST_FORECAST_WINDOW_DAYS`, `.env.local`, default 7) **complete** days (`snapshot_date < today` → the partial current day excluded) that recorded spend (`HAVING day_total > 0` → a missing/zero refresh is excluded, not a free $0 day). Falls back to the naive projection if no complete trailing day exists yet.
- **Alert rewired (`cost-alert.ts`):** the decision now compares **`alert_forecast_usd`** (run-rate) to `COST_ALERT_THRESHOLD_USD` ($80), NOT `projected_month_end_usd`. The notifier (`cost_alert_notify.py`) embed reframed to "GCP spend on pace for $X/mo at the current run rate (trailing window)" + an honest "This month's projected total: $Y" context line. Anti-spam latch / once-per-day / fail-safe paths unchanged.
- **Shadow (Phase 1.5):** `cost-alert.ts` logs one `[cost-alert] shadow: naive=$.. trailing_month_end=$.. alert_run_rate=$.. threshold=$.. | old_alert(naive>thr) would_fire=.. new_alert(run_rate>thr) fires=..` line every refresh — naive↔trailing forecast AND old↔new alert logic side-by-side. The new logic drives; naive is logged only for the record.
- **Config:** `COST_FORECAST_WINDOW_DAYS=7` appended to gitignored `.env.local` (perms 600, append-only). `runPython` passes `{...process.env}` to the child, so `query_cost.py`'s `os.environ.get` reads it after a rebuild+restart; default 7 / garbage → 7.
- **Verification (honesty protocol):** `py_compile` OK (both helpers); `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0; `npm run build` exit 0 (`/health` 16.7 kB). **Live data (real cache, Jun 28):** naive **$242.2** (≈ the documented $251 false alarm) → trailing month-end **$236.58** (card) · run-rate alert **$157.9**; N override (window=3 → $64.23) + garbage→7 proven. **Synthetic tests via the ACTUAL `query_cost.py` code on a throwaway DB (zero live-cache pollution):** ENDED front-loaded spike → run-rate alert **SILENT ($60 < $80)** while naive **would've false-fired ($443)**; SUSTAINED recent spike → run-rate alert **FIRES ($450 > $80)** — honest both ways. Notify embed wording + end-to-end dry-run (no real post) confirmed. [Phase-2 live-route + alert-check verification recorded below at deploy.]
- **Regression posture:** Hub cost-display only — the 5 bot-side recurring-bug canaries are bot/Discord surfaces, untouched; no money path, no `trevor.db`/`auto_config`/flag/schema write, no new npm deps, no design tokens, no sacred file (none on the Hub). Only the forecast NUMBER + the alert's input metric changed; MTD total, per-service breakdown, history graph, daily refresh, anti-spam plumbing all byte-behavior-identical.
- **Lock-guard:** all 5 code files claimed via `scripts/locks/lock_acquire.sh` (owner `B1`), released after; committed via `scripts/locks/git_commit_serialized.sh` on `master`. `.env.local` gitignored (NOT staged); `tsconfig.tsbuildinfo` NOT staged.
- **Files (6):** `scripts/db/query_cost.py` · `scripts/db/cost_alert_notify.py` · `src/lib/cost-alert.ts` · `src/app/api/cost/route.ts` · `src/components/memory/cost-tracker-card.tsx` · `CLAUDE.md` (this entry). Commit `fix: trailing-window cost forecast, kill false front-loaded-spike alerts (B1)` on `master` — NOT pushed (held for Ghost).
- **Preference (2026-06-28):** the GCP cost forecast is **trailing-window-aware**, not naive linear. The **card** shows the honest month-end (`mtd_actual + trailing_7d_mean × days_remaining`) — a sunk spike already in MTD stays counted (June correctly reads ~$237 >$80; do NOT mute a real overspend). The **budget alert** triggers on the **forward run-rate** (`trailing_7d_mean × days_in_month > COST_ALERT_THRESHOLD_USD`), NOT the sunk month-end — so an ENDED spike stops re-firing once the run-rate normalizes, while a real ONGOING spike still fires. Trailing mean = last **N=7** (`COST_FORECAST_WINDOW_DAYS`) **complete** days with spend (current incomplete day + missing/zero days excluded). Don't revert to the naive `mtd/elapsed×dim` forecast or point the alert back at the month-end number.

### 2026-06-22 — B5: Discord budget alert — cost-forecast > ceiling → one #downloads alert/day — DEPLOYED
- **Early-warning Discord alert so a runaway GCP bill pings Ghost BEFORE it blows up, not after the charge.** Piggybacks on B4's daily cost refresh: after the cache write, it checks the **month-end FORECAST** (not just MTD) against a ceiling and posts a Discord alert if the projection crosses it. WSL `TrevorHub` (`ghost@Ghost`), SOLO on `master`, port :3000. Fix code G5. **Read-only over cost data — no money path, no `trevor.db`, no `auto_config`/flag write, no bot file; alert is strictly best-effort (a webhook failure can never break the refresh or the Hub).** [[gcs-cost-spike-egress-rootcause]]
- **Phase-0 audit findings (drove the design):** (1) B4's forecast lives in the READ path `scripts/db/query_cost.py:91` (`projected = mtd_net / days_elapsed * days_in_month`) + `breakdown` (sorted net DESC → `breakdown[0]` = top cost driver), NOT in `cost-refresh.ts` (the BigQuery writer). The alert **reuses** it by running `query_cost.py` and reading `projected_month_end_usd` + `breakdown[0]` — zero forecast divergence, no recompute. (2) `.env.local` carries exactly ONE webhook, **`HUB_DOWNLOADS_WEBHOOK_URL`** (no `HUB_MONITOR_WEBHOOK_URL`/alerts channel on this box; `DISCORD_BOT_TOKEN` is the documented empty placeholder) → reuse it; a dedicated alerts channel is correctly **deferred** (needs Ghost to mint a webhook + add the env). (3) `cost_snapshots` is empty today (GCP export still backfilling) → `query_cost.py` returns `no_data_yet` → no forecast → no alert (honest no-op until real data lands).
- **Threshold = one config line:** `COST_ALERT_THRESHOLD_USD=80` appended to `.env.local` (gitignored, append-only, perms 600 preserved) — well above the ~$50 target, well below the $200 disaster. Read server-side by `readCostAlertThreshold()` (default 80 on unset/invalid/≤0).
- **Anti-spam = one alert per UTC day.** New additive migration **`migrations/002_cost_alert_state.sql`** → single-row `cost_alert_state` table (`last_alert_date`/`last_forecast_usd`/`last_threshold_usd`/`updated_at`) in the Hub-owned `data/hub.db` (NOT the replica; NO change to `cost_snapshots`). Applied via the existing `apply_hub_migration.py` (tracked in `_migrations`; auto-backed-up `hub.db` first). The date is recorded **ONLY after a successful post** → a webhook failure leaves it untouched and the next refresh retries.
- **`scripts/db/cost_alert_notify.py` (new)** — the anti-spam gate + embed poster. Reads ONE stdin JSON `{projected, threshold, top_service, mtd_total_usd, month_label}`; gates in order: defensive `under_threshold` → `refused_replica_path`/`hub_db_missing` → `state_unreadable` (fail SAFE — if anti-spam can't be enforced, do NOT post) → `already_alerted_today` → post → record-on-success. Posts an embed-only message (gold `0xD4A64A` warning, NOT red) to #downloads **reusing `discord_file_delivery._read_webhook_url()`** (env-only, fail-loud, never printed, NO fallback) — `post_file_sync` itself can't be reused (it needs a FILE + would register a DOCS manifest entry; an alert is a message). **NEVER raises** — always one JSON object + exit 0, so the caller treats it as best-effort. `--dry-run` flag (gates + build embed, no post, no record) for tests.
- **`src/lib/cost-alert.ts` (new)** — `maybeSendCostAlert()`: runs `query_cost.py` (reuse forecast) → if `status!="ok"` no-op (`no_forecast:<status>`) → if `projected <= threshold` `under_threshold` → else hand `{projected, threshold, top_service:breakdown[0], mtd, month_label}` to `cost_alert_notify.py`. **Wrapped in try/catch → NEVER throws** (no-data, Python error, webhook down, bad JSON all resolve to a `CostAlertResult`).
- **Phase 1 verification (honesty protocol):** `py_compile` OK; `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0; migration applied (table present, 0 rows, `cost_snapshots` untouched). **6/6 unit tests PASS** against the runtime python (`venv/bin/python3` → `/usr/bin/python3`, `requests 2.34.2`), **zero real Discord posts** (dry-run + bogus-webhook paths only): T1 `under_threshold` (silent); T2 fire-decision dry-run (`would_post:true`, 0 rows recorded); T3 anti-spam — seeded `last_alert_date=today` → `already_alerted_today`, no post; T4 webhook failure (bogus URL) → `post_failed`, **date NOT recorded** (0 rows — proves retry-next-refresh); T5 bad stdin; T6 missing projected/threshold. Final state 0 rows, `cost_snapshots` 0 rows (untouched).
- **Phase 2 — wired + live-proven.** `maybeSendCostAlert()` is called in `POST /api/cost/refresh` **after** `result.status === "ok"` (the prompt's hook), inside its own try/catch; the response adds an optional `alert` field only on the `ok` path (byte-identical on `no_data_yet`/`error`). `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0; `npm run build` exit 0 (`/api/cost/refresh` recompiled); `sudo systemctl restart trevor-dashboard.service` → active, **NRestarts=0**, new PID 3206361, `[HUB] ready on :3000`, clean boot (only the benign `url.parse` DEP0169). **Live route doesn't break the refresh:** unauth → **401**; authed → **200 `no_data_yet`** (BigQuery empty → alert correctly NOT invoked, journal clean — the inert-until-backfill state, exactly like B4).
- **Phase 2 — REAL Discord post proven (honesty protocol).** Drove the exact chain the route invokes (`query_cost.py` forecast → `cost_alert_notify.py` → #downloads), using **synthetic cache rows over the REAL $80 threshold** (cleaner than a forced-$1 threshold — no `.env.local` mutation to restore, and it exercises the production threshold path): inserted Cloud Storage $50 + Compute Engine $15 (this month) → `query_cost.py` → `status:ok, projected $84.78 (65/23×30), top=Cloud Storage $50` → notify → **`{"alerted":true,"reason":"posted","message_id":"1518813436660813925"}`** (real embed in #downloads: "GCP spend projected at $84.78 by month-end · threshold $80.00 · Top service: Cloud Storage $50.00 MTD · /health?tab=cost"). `cost_alert_state` recorded `('2026-06-23',84.78,80.0)` (UTC date). **Anti-spam proven:** immediate re-run → **`already_alerted_today`, NO second post**. **Cleanup verified:** synthetic rows + state row DELETEd → `cost_snapshots` 0, `cost_alert_state` 0, `query_cost.py` back to `no_data_yet`. Threshold left at **80** (never changed). Exactly ONE test alert posted.
- **Regression posture (Phase 4):** Hub-side cost-display feature only — **no bot file touched** (the only file changed since the P1 commit is `src/app/api/cost/refresh/route.ts`; the 5 bot-side recurring-bug canaries — signal dedup, HOLD-card delete, orphaned reminders, reply handler, results auto-delete — are all bot-side, untouched); no money path, no `trevor.db`/`auto_config`/flag write, no schema change to existing tables (additive `cost_alert_state` only). Alert is strictly best-effort: a webhook failure leaves the anti-spam date unrecorded (retried next refresh) and can never change the refresh response or break the Hub (T4 unit test + route try/catch + `maybeSendCostAlert` never-throws + live `no_data_yet` 200).

### 2026-06-22 — B1: tailnet VM→WSL DB sync — retire the GCS restore loop (kill the ~$110/mo egress) — DEPLOYED
- **Replaces the GCS litestream-restore loop with a direct tailnet VM→WSL snapshot + rsync-delta sync.** WSL `TrevorHub` (`ghost@Ghost`), SOLO on `master`. The Hub's read-replica was refreshed by `litestream restore` from `gcs://trevor-prime-backups/litestream/trevor.db` ~65×/day (`trevor-restore.timer`) — **~75-80% of a $158/mo Cloud Storage bill (~0.9 TB/mo GCS egress)**. Both boxes are on Tailscale (**DIRECT WireGuard P2P** — `pong … via <pub-ip>:41641`, not DERP), so the Hub now pulls a consistent snapshot of `trevor.db` straight from the VM over the tailnet. Fix code G1. [[gcs-cost-spike-egress-rootcause]] [[trevorhub-read-path]]
- **⚠️ THE EGRESS WIN IS rsync DELTA-TRANSFER, NOT THE TAILNET.** The VM is GCP — bytes leaving it are Compute-Engine egress at ~the same per-GB rate as GCS egress, so a full pull every cycle would be NO cheaper (it would just move the bill GCS→GCE). The saving is real only because: (1) rsync's delta algorithm (a remote-shell transfer → delta is ON by default, `-z` compressed) sends only the CHANGED blocks (the DB churn, ~MB); (2) the staging file `/home/ghost/trevor-replica/.staging/trevor.db` is PERSISTENT — it is the delta BASIS and is NEVER wiped (wiping it, or using `scp`/whole-file, forfeits the saving); (3) the snapshot is largely page-stable run-to-run. **Measured live: cold pull 258 MB received; warm delta pull 7 MB received (1.37 GB matched, 193× speedup).** Per-sync GCP egress drops from ~0.46 GB (the old per-restore) to ~MB → the ~$110/mo egress line falls toward ~$2-15/mo.
- **⚠️ SNAPSHOT METHOD REVERSED MID-BUILD: `.backup` → `VACUUM INTO` (Ghost-approved at the gate, evidence-driven).** The Phase-0 plan (and Ghost's confirmation) was `sqlite3 .backup` (best page-stability for delta). But `.backup` **LIVELOCKS on this hot, continuously-written DB**: its page-by-page copy restarts every time the live bot writes an already-copied page → observed the VM `sqlite3` spinning at **67% CPU with the output frozen at 390 MB for 11+ min** (the first manual runs only succeeded by catching quiet write-windows). Switched to **`VACUUM INTO`**, which reads inside ONE transaction → a consistent snapshot IMMUNE to concurrent writes (35s, reliable) and — because this DB is append-mostly (trade/shadow logs) — still delta-friendly (7 MB/sync, 188× speedup). Reliability > delta-perfection. The script header records the livelock evidence + the pivot.
- **The mechanism (`deploy/scripts/trevor-tailsync.sh`, new):** `ssh vm` (MagicDNS alias → `ghost@trevor-prime`, NOT the prompt's stale hardcoded IPs `100.93.113.117`/`100.125.247.52` — post-migration the VM is `100.96.113.73`) → `sudo -n -u trevor sqlite3 "VACUUM INTO /tmp/…"` (run as the DB owner, WAL-safe alongside the bot; `sudo -u trevor` for the snapshot AND its `rm`, since /tmp is sticky and the snap is trevor-owned) → `rsync -z --partial` delta-pull onto the persistent staging basis → `cp` to a throwaway publish copy → python3 `wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)`+`journal_mode=DELETE` (WSL has **NO sqlite3 CLI** — every WSL-side DB op uses python3's stdlib sqlite3; DELETE-mode publish matches the proven `mode=ro` read contract, done on the throwaway copy so the basis stays page-stable) → `PRAGMA integrity_check` + monotonic `auto_trades MAX(id)` regression gate → atomic `mv` over `/home/ghost/trevor-replica/trevor.db` → cleanup VM temp. Any failure leaves the existing replica untouched + exits non-zero. flock single-flight. Consumers read `/home/trevor/trevor/trevor.db` (symlink → the replica) or `$TREVOR_DB_PATH`, all `mode=ro` — publishing to the same path means zero consumer change.
- **systemd units (`deploy/systemd/wsl/trevor-tailsync.{service,timer}`, new; installed byte-identical to `/etc/systemd/system/`):** system scope, `User=ghost`, `HOME=/home/ghost` (so ssh finds the `vm` alias + passphraseless key), `After=tailscaled.service`, `TimeoutStartSec=15min`, `Nice=10`/`IOSchedulingClass=idle`. Timer `OnUnitInactiveSec=20min` (≈ the old loop's freshness, near-$0 egress).
- **Phase 1 verification (honesty protocol):** manual run (cold) + manual run (warm delta) + **`systemctl start` run under the systemd/ghost context all succeeded** (the systemd run proves the exact ExecStart the timer fires). Published replica: DELETE-mode, `integrity_check = ok`, `auto_trades` **972 / 100957 = EXACT parity with VM live**. VM temp cleaned, STAGE basis persisted, DST `0444`. (An early systemd `start` once failed with an rsync "protocol incompatibility" — a transient from a concurrent `enable --now` timer-fire racing the explicit start around a `.backup`-era staging state; did NOT recur once tested cleanly. Production has no concurrency — only the timer fires it; flock + systemd serialize.)
- **Phase 2 — GCS restore loop retired:** `trevor-restore.timer` + `trevor-restore.service` `systemctl disable --now` (both `disabled`/`inactive`). `litestream.service` stays **masked** (the dangerous WSL push unit — untouched). **GCS read paths found + silenced: 1** (exhaustively confirmed — no other timer/cron/`/etc/cron.*`/unit-file reads GCS). The VM-side litestream PUSH (B2 trims its retention) and `query_system_health.py`'s VM litestream probe are **untouched** (that probe reads the *VM's* litestream.service over ssh, not GCS). `trevor-restore.sh` kept on disk with a rollback header (re-enable = `systemctl enable --now trevor-restore.timer`).
- **Phase 3 — egress gone, verified:** Hub `active`, `/api/health` HTTP 200 (still serving the replica on **:3000**, not the doc's stale :3333); no litestream/restore process; the last GCS restore ended **18:51:56, before the cutover** — none since; replica fresh from tailsync; **the tailsync timer fired AUTOMATICALLY at 19:13:19 EDT (Result=success, published 19:14:46, `integrity_check = ok`, 0 ABORT) and self-scheduled the next fire 19:34:48 (+20 min)**. Billing console will confirm the egress drop over the next 1-2 days (billing lags); watch it in the upcoming Hub cost tracker (B4).
- **Regression posture:** WSL-Hub-only refresh-mechanism swap, read-only over the VM (VACUUM-INTO snapshot + ssh), no money path, no `trevor.db`/`auto_config`/flag/schema write, no bot file touched (zero `/home/trevor/trevor/` write), no new npm deps, no sacred file (none on this box). The replica is read-only from WSL except the atomic swap of a locally-verified file.
- **Lock-guard:** claimed `deploy/scripts/trevor-tailsync.sh` · `deploy/systemd/wsl/trevor-tailsync.service` · `deploy/systemd/wsl/trevor-tailsync.timer` · `deploy/scripts/trevor-restore.sh` · `CLAUDE.md` via `scripts/locks/lock_acquire.sh` (owner `B1`); committed via `scripts/locks/git_commit_serialized.sh`; released after. `tsconfig.tsbuildinfo` NOT staged.
- **Files (5):** `deploy/scripts/trevor-tailsync.sh` (new) · `deploy/systemd/wsl/trevor-tailsync.service` (new) · `deploy/systemd/wsl/trevor-tailsync.timer` (new) · `deploy/scripts/trevor-restore.sh` (rollback header) · `CLAUDE.md` (this entry). Commit `feat: tailnet VM→WSL db sync, retire GCS restore loop (B1)` on `master` — NOT pushed (held for Ghost).

Snapshot = **`VACUUM INTO`** run as `sudo -u trevor` on the VM (NOT `.backup` — it livelocks on the hot DB); pull = **`rsync -z` delta** against the **persistent** `.staging/trevor.db` basis (the delta IS the egress saving — never wipe the basis, never use `scp`/whole-file); publish = DELETE-mode copy, integrity + monotonic-gated, atomic `mv` to `/home/ghost/trevor-replica/trevor.db`. ssh via the `vm` MagicDNS alias (stale hardcoded tailnet IPs are wrong). The VM-side litestream GCS push stays (the cheap backup write side; B2 trims retention). Supersedes the W-H-P2-HUB "continuous GCS restore" read-path preference.
