### 2026-06-21 — C1: DAILY EDGE "Download recon prompt" button + evidence-render fix + window-badge fix — DEPLOYED
- **Adds a dynamic Markdown meta-prompt download to the Intel DAILY EDGE tab (B2) + fixes two silent render bugs on the same tab.** WSL `TrevorHub` (`ghost@Ghost`), SOLO on `master`, port :3000. **Read-and-display ONLY** — the button READS the JSON the tab already fetched (`/api/daily-edge`) and TEMPLATES a client-side Blob download; it never writes a shadow, never flips a rule, never edits the VM bot, never calls the engine. Consistent with the B2 "DAILY EDGE is read-and-display only" preference. One component file touched. [[daily-edge-source-census-a2]]
- **⚠️ Prompt-vs-reality correction #1 (the evidence bug):** the prompt's premise — `evidence = shadows + trade_ids + counterfactual` — is **stale**. The engine writes `evidence` as a **per-dimension object** whose only common key is **`source`** (the shadow/table + dedup key); the dimension cells vary (`worst_cell`/`all_cells`, `by_level`/`live_level_pct`, leverage figures, `total_fees`) and are summarised by the engine inside a candidate-level **`tweak`** sentence (already carries n/WR/net). `evidenceLine()` read `counterfactual`/`shadows`/`trade_ids` — none of which exist — so EVERY candidate hit the blanket `"no evidence summary"` fallback. The component also never read `tweak`/`caveat`/`id`.
- **Phase 1 — evidence render fix (`daily-edge-section.tsx`).** Rewrote `evidenceLine(c)` to lead with the engine's `tweak` sentence (→ legacy string → legacy `counterfactual` → honest `"—"`); added `evidenceSource(c)` (reads `evidence.source`, null when absent). Hero now renders the tweak line + a muted `src:` provenance line; runner-up expanded `<dl>` adds `source` + `caveat` (gold) rows. Extended `Evidence` (+`source`) + `Candidate` (+`id`/`tweak`/`caveat`/`directional`) interfaces; legacy string/counterfactual/shadows/trade_ids branches kept as harmless back-compat. **Proven against the live JSON:** the $6.33 `direction_into_regime` candidate (+ all 4) now render their real tweak + source (not "no evidence summary"); genuinely-empty → `"—"`.
- **Phase 1 (folded in) — window-badge fix.** Same root cause: `WindowBounds` read `data.window.start`/`.end` but the engine writes `start_utc`/`end_utc`/`start_et`/`end_et` → the freshness badge's `window <date>–<date>` segment silently never rendered. Mapped to `start_utc`/`end_utc` (legacy `start`/`end` fallback) via derived `windowStart`/`windowEnd` consts. Now resolves (`Jun 20–Jun 21`).
- **Phase 2 — "Download recon prompt" button + meta-prompt generator (`daily-edge-section.tsx`).** New module-level `buildMetaPrompt(data)` templates the already-fetched JSON into a SHORT Markdown meta-prompt (~1.8 KB / ~34 lines for today's 4 candidates) FOR a Claude.ai chat to write the next Daily Recon CC prompt — 1 instruction paragraph + verdict/window/generated + ranked `candidates[]` (dimension · edge · n · safety · reversibility · forward-unpriced/low-n flags · evidence line · source · caveat) + 6 bullet rules (read-only, dedup-per-trade, edge-first, per-tweak-n, recommendation-only, flag low-n/forward_unpriced, A1 depth). `metaPromptFilename(generated_at)` → `daily_recon_metaprompt_<UTC-date>.md` (date from `generated_at`, NOT the browser clock; null → `_latest`). Button mounted in the freshness-badge row (right-aligned `ml-auto`, lucide `Download` icon, labeled "Download recon prompt", `disabled` when no data) — **distinct, no collision** (the tab had NO existing file icon). Download via the proven `download-format-sheet.tsx` Blob/Object-URL idiom (`new Blob` → `createObjectURL` → `link.download` → click → `revokeObjectURL`). The `useCallback` handler is declared **before** the early-return Skeleton so hook order never changes (HUB-C3 lesson).
- **Honest states:** `not_generated_yet`/`no_tweak_today`/`error`/empty-candidates → the `.md` says so + "write a STANDARD recon prompt", **never a fabricated candidate**; button stays enabled. Filename falls back to `_latest` when `generated_at` is null. Proven for both states.
- **Gates:** `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0 (both phases). Logic replicas over the real `data/daily_edge_candidates.json` proved evidence/source/window/filename/meta-prompt for `tweak_recommended` + both empty states.
- **Phase 3 — build + deploy + live-verify (DONE).** `npm run build` exit 0 (`/intel` **17.1 → 18.7 kB** for the button + meta-prompt generator). `sudo systemctl restart trevor-dashboard.service` → active/running, NRestarts 0, PID 1887432, `[HUB] ready on :3000`, clean boot (only the documented-benign `url.parse` DEP0169). Verified directly (curl `--retry`, NOT `sleep N`): `/api/health` **200** in 0.06s. **Live (authed):** `/api/daily-edge` → `tweak_recommended`, 4 candidates, `window.start_utc` present, top candidate carries `tweak` + `evidence.source` (the exact fields the fix renders); `/intel?tab=daily-edge` → **200**. **Fresh build proven (not stale `.next`):** served client chunk carries `Download recon prompt` / `daily_recon_metaprompt_` / `TREVOR Daily Recon`, and the bug-causing blanket `"no evidence summary"` string is **GONE (0 chunks)**. **DOM-verify ceiling (honest):** the section is a client component (renders post-hydration after the `fetch`) and no headless browser is on this box — so the post-hydration evidence paint + button click are verified by inference (new component-in-served-bundle + live API carrying tweak/source/window + tsc + logic-replica render/meta-prompt proofs + route 200), NOT a live headless paint. trevor-prime.com proxies to THIS WSL dashboard, so the local render is the served artifact. ### 2026-06-21 — E1: $-Rank Hub Intel View — every shadow ranked by distinct-trade $ impact + new "Impact" sub-tab — DEPLOYED
- **The payoff of the shadow overhaul: a ranked "what to flip next" page.** WSL `TrevorHub` (`ghost@Ghost`), SOLO on `master`, port :3000. A new Intel **"Impact"** sub-tab lists every shadow sorted by its **dollar impact** so the most-profitable rule to flip live is obvious at a glance. **Read-and-display only over the litestream replica (`mode=ro`)** — never writes a shadow, never flips a live rule, never touches the VM bot. No `trevor.db`/`auto_config`/flag/schema write, no money path. [[trevorhub-shadow-aggregate-only]] [[auto-trades-pnl-semantics]]
- **⚠️ THE METRIC HONESTY CALL (Ghost-approved at the gate) — distinct-trade Σ, NOT raw per-row Σ.** The prompt's literal `outcome_sum_pnl = Σ(realized_pnl_usd)` over a shadow's footprint reproduces the exact "+$743 counting artifact" the SH-HUB wave killed: most shadows log one row **per monitor cycle per trade**, each stamping that trade's realized P&L, so a raw `SUM` multiplies by rows-per-trade. Proven live: `trail_be_stale_altparam_shadow` raw Σ **−$57,220** vs honest distinct-trade Σ **−$48.55** (~1001× overcount); `regime_exit` raw −$4,442 vs −$76; `partial_trigger` raw +$2,598 vs +$4.14. Only `perfect_setup_trade_summary_shadow` is natively 1-row-per-trade (−$80 = −$80) — which is why A4 calls it the flagship. **E1 ranks on `outcome_sum_pnl` = Σ realized $ over DISTINCT trades** (dedup by the first available trade key `auto_trade_id`→`trade_id`→`signal_id`, `MAX(realized) per trade-group` then SUM) — a per-cycle sum is NEVER presented as realizable money.
- **⚠️ REGISTRY-MECHANISM CORRECTION (the prompt/A4 premise was stale for the E1 target).** A4's "hardcoded `TABLE_DEFS`" finding describes **`query_shadow_status.py`** (`/api/intel/shadow`, the Intel deep-dive grid) — a DIFFERENT surface. The E1 target **`query_shadow_registry.py`** (`/api/shadow/registry`, what the rank view reads) is **DYNAMIC** (`_enumerate()` reads `sqlite_master WHERE name LIKE '%shadow%'`), so the 5 live Wave-D tables **already auto-surfaced** with zero registration. Phase 2's real work was curated `OVERRIDE` rows (display + function group + `expected_active`), NOT "making them appear." `query_shadow_status.py` was **NOT touched** (one surface at a time, Ghost decision).
- **⚠️ D2's 5 tables NEVER LANDED — E1 ranks the real 5.** Of the 10 Wave-D tables, only **5 exist** in the live DB: `correlation_cluster_shadow` (5 rows), `aggressive_mode_entry_shadow` (4), `perfect_setup_trade_summary_shadow` (919 — flagship), `winner_cell_upweight_shadow` (0), `sizing_v2_multi_strategy_shadow` (0). **D2's 5** (`layer6_disabled_shadow`, `layer6_per_ticker_threshold_shadow`, `momentum_fee_gate_shadow`, `external_close_outcome_shadow`, `entry_stop_distance_shadow`) are absent from the VM **and** replica — no name drift, genuinely not there: **committed on disk, awaiting the deferred wave-end `trevor.service` restart.** They auto-fold into the rank once the bot restarts (dynamic registry). **Flagged as a separate "D2 didn't land" item — not an E1 problem.**
- **Phase 1+2 — backend `query_shadow_registry.py` (additive, lock-guarded).** Each shadow now emits `outcome_sum_pnl` (distinct-trade Σ realized $), `footprint_n` (distinct-trade count), `confidence_tag` (HIGH n≥50 / MED n≥15 / LOW n≥5 / WEAK), and `metric_type` (`flip_delta` for the 2 tables logging a true would-vs-actual counterfactual — `winner_cell_upweight_shadow` + `sizing_v2_multi_strategy_shadow`; `footprint` for the rest; null when no realized-outcome column). **`metric_type` only CLASSIFIES the table — `outcome_sum_pnl` is ALWAYS the distinct-trade footprint Σ; the field never relabels a footprint as a flip-delta.** Curated `OVERRIDE` rows for the 5 live D tables: correlation→**Risk**, aggressive→**Entry**, winner_cell→**Scoring** (expected_active=False), sizing_v2→**Risk** (expected_active=False), perfect_setup→**Scoring**. The 2 zero-row tables are `expected_active=False` so they read **DORMANT (pending)**, NOT a false **BROKEN** alarm. `route.ts` `ShadowTable` interface +4 fields. `replica_age_seconds` was **already** emitted (no new work).
- **Phase 3 — frontend `src/components/intel/shadow-rank-section.tsx` (new, lock-guarded).** Fetches the extended `/api/shadow/registry` (60s poll), sorts by `outcome_sum_pnl` **descending** (no-footprint shadows sorted LAST under a "No footprint yet (N)" header — never a fabricated number), and renders each via the **reused `CompactShadowCard`** (not a forked card) under a prominent $-impact rank line (rank · signed $ mono · `n trades · CONF` · metric badge). `flip_delta` gets a mint `△ flip-delta` `Pill` (stronger-evidence class); `footprint` gets a neutral `footprint` pill + the honest framing line "*footprint is the realized $ the rule's trades made/lost — NOT proven '$ if flipped'*". **REPLICA badge** (`<Pill intent="warn">REPLICA ~Nm</Pill>`) sourced from `replica_age_seconds`. Loading/empty/error states handled. Slotted as the 5th Intel sub-tab "Impact" (`navigation.ts` + `intel-zone-view.tsx` `case "impact"`). **No new endpoint, no new Python helper, no new npm deps, no new design tokens.**
- **Gates:** `py_compile` OK; `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0 (both commits); `npm run build` exit 0 (`/intel` **13.2 → 15.1 kB** for the new section). **Honest distinct-trade ranking proven:** the −$57k `trail_be_stale` artifact collapses to −$48.55; `metric_type` correct; the 2 empties read DORMANT + "no footprint yet".
- **Phase 4 — deploy + live-verify.** `sudo systemctl restart trevor-dashboard.service` → **active**, PID 1410299, `[HUB] ready on :3000`, clean boot (only the benign `url.parse` DEP0169). Verified via a backgrounded health-poll → authed checks (NOT `sleep N && check`): `/api/health` 200 in 1s; **`/api/shadow/registry`** total 58, `replica_age_seconds` present, all 4 new fields on `perfect_setup` (sum −$80, n=919, HIGH, footprint); **live rank top-5** live_partial +$13.55 · partial_trigger +$4.14 · trail_v3 −$1.00 · aggressive_mode_entry −$2.03 · correlation_cluster −$2.03; **bottom-5** session_exit −$76 · perfect_setup −$80 · shadow_scorer_v2 −$85 · momentum_exit −$92 · risk_budget_account_snapshot −$5,452. **`/intel?tab=impact`** authed → **200**, SSR carries `Impact` / `REPLICA` / `distinct trades` / `flip-delta` / `footprint`. Monitors stopped.
- **Flagged (NOT in E1 scope, honest note):** `risk_budget_account_snapshot_shadow` ranks −$5,452 over n=2658 — it's an account-snapshot table (its `realized_pnl_usd` is account-level, not a clean per-trade delta), so its footprint Σ is large but semantically not a per-trade rule edge. Honestly labeled `footprint` ("not proven $-if-flipped"); per-table column-semantics curation is a separate follow-up, not a surgical E1 edit.

No `auto_config`/flag/DB-schema write, no new npm deps, no design tokens, no money path. **Sacred 13/13 OK** (VM — E1 touched zero `/home/trevor/trevor/` files). `query_shadow_status.py` left byte-untouched.

### 2026-06-21 — B6 P4: Shadow Lab card + `registered_shadows[]` registry fold (B6 unified registry, Hub read-side) — DEPLOYED
- **Hub read-side of the B6 unified shadow registry.** WSL `TrevorHub` (`ghost@Ghost`), SOLO on `master`, port :3000. B6 introduces a UNIFIED model — one row per shadow in **`shadow_registry`** + every observation in the single **`shadow_observations`** table (keyed by `shadow_key`) — superseding the legacy per-table inventory for newly-registered shadows. P4 builds the Hub surfaces: extend the registry query additively + a read-only `<ShadowLabCard>` on the Health home. **Read-only over the litestream replica (`mode=ro`), pure SELECT; no bot/VM file, no `trevor.db`/`auto_config`/flag/schema write, no money path.** Both B6 tables exist but are **EMPTY** today → the card renders its empty-state until **B7 registers ssl.py** (the first shadow). ### 2026-06-21 — NUM-B1/B2/B3: realized-P&L NUMERATOR now sums `pnl_usd + partial_pnl_realized` (fixes the ~12% undercount on the Auto Capital card) — DEPLOYED
- **Completes the same fraction the BASE wave fixed the bottom of.** WSL `TrevorHub` (`ghost@Ghost`), SOLO on `master`, port :3000. `query_auto_state.py:compute_windows`/`compute_custom` summed only `pnl_usd` (the final-close leg) and ignored `partial_pnl_realized` (separately-banked scale-out profits), so every realized window read MORE negative than the truth. TREVOR's exit engine scales out routinely → 48 closed-live rows carry banked partials totalling **+$10.2506**. Now each row contributes `pnl_usd + COALESCE(partial_pnl_realized,0)`. **Display-only numerator; the denominator ($122.18 lifetime HL net-deposits base, BASE-B1/B2/B3) is byte-untouched; no bot/VM file, no `trevor.db`/`auto_config`/flag/schema write, no money-path order logic.** [[auto-trades-pnl-semantics]]
- **World A re-proven (NO double-count):** the final close sizes its fill to the REMAINING position and EXCLUDES already-banked partials, so `pnl_usd` (final leg) and `partial_pnl_realized` (scaled-out legs) cover non-overlapping fractions. Summing both is the true realized total — verified three ways (bot code, `trail_partials.py`, row arithmetic) AND by the per-window-delta proof below (each window rose by EXACTLY its rows' partial sum, not a cent more).
- **NUM-B1** — `compute_windows`: widened the SELECT to read `partial_pnl_realized` + per-row sum now `pnl + COALESCE(partial,0)`. **NUM-B2** — `compute_custom`: identical change so custom ranges match the presets. **NUM-B3** — robustness: the old `if pnl is None: unknown += 1; continue` dropped the whole row; now a row is `unknown` (excluded) ONLY when it booked no number at all (`pnl_usd` NULL *and* partial null/zero) — a banked partial on a not-yet-final-closed trade still contributes its partial. 6 surgical sites, ONE file: SELECT (`:381`) + both generators (`:430`/`:439`) + both per-row loops + 3 docstrings.
- **Live undercount magnitude (ALL, fetch-time): −$83.7373 → −$73.4867** (`SUM(pnl) −83.7373 + SUM(partial) +10.2506`); +$10.2506 less negative (~12.2%). NB the Phase-0 snapshot read 866 rows / −$72.8456; the bot closed a fresh −$0.6411 trade (`2026-06-21 04:54:16`) mid-task → 867 rows / −$73.4867. The fix tracks the live DB to the cent (the +$10.25 partial recovery is the constant; the base moves with live trading).
- **`pnl_today_usd` legacy field inherits the fix for free** (`:512` reads `realized["today"]`; no separate re-sum).
- **Phase-1 verification (honesty protocol, frozen 867-row VM snapshot vs the EDITED functions):** `py_compile` OK. Harness OVERALL **PASS** — (1) no-double-count: each window rose by EXACTLY its partial sum [today +0.0634 / yest +0.5183 / week +1.8937 / month +10.2506 / all +10.2506]; (2) ALL = −73.4867 = independently re-queried live DB corrected total; (3) `compute_custom` full-span byte-equal to ALL (−73.4867); (4) NUM-B3 injection: NULL-pnl+$0.50-partial row counted (+0.50 ALL & today, +1 count) while a both-null row → +1 unknown; (5) `realized_unknown_count` unchanged (=1, the lone genuinely-unbooked `external_close` row).
- **Causes found: 1** (one numerator path + its custom mirror, one missing column, one mechanism). No sacred file (none on this box), no recurring-bug adjacency (all 5 canaries bot-side; Hub read-only-DB single-Python-helper change). ### 2026-06-21 — BASE-B1/B2/B3: Auto Capital realized-% base → ONE fixed lifetime HL net-deposits figure (new public-HL ledger reader) — DEPLOYED
- **The realized-P&L % denominator on the Auto Capital card is repointed.** WSL `TrevorHub` (`ghost@Ghost`), SOLO on `master`, port :3000. Each time-window used to divide its realized P&L by its OWN start-of-window equity (WA-P1, `equity_snapshots`) — so the % shifted window-to-window and went past **−100%** (impossible vs real capital: ALL read **−117%**, MONTH **−100%**). Ghost's decision: divide EVERY window by ONE fixed base = **total lifetime HL net deposits** (all capital in − all capital out, ever). Display-only denominator change; the `realized` numerator is **untouched**. No bot/VM file, no `trevor.db`/`auto_config`/flag/schema write, no money-path order logic.
- **⚠️ Phase-0 correction — the Hub CANNOT make a Python `import hyperliquid` call (this is WHY the reader is TS-side).** This WSL box has **no `hyperliquid` module** (not even in the local venv), **no HL creds, no `.env`, no wallet address in env** → `query_auto_state.py:_fetch_hl()` always fails here (live equity is heartbeat-sourced, EQT-A3/W-H-P4). BUT `userNonFundingLedgerUpdates` is a **public** HL info endpoint needing only the **public** wallet ADDRESS, and this box reaches `api.hyperliquid.xyz` directly (proven HTTP 200) — the SAME pattern as `prices/route.ts`. So the reader is a **direct TS `fetch`**, not a Python SDK call.
- **BASE-B1 — new `src/lib/hl-deposits.ts` (TS reader).** `fetch('https://api.hyperliquid.xyz/info', {POST type:"userNonFundingLedgerUpdates", user:addr})`, sums **`lifetime_net_deposits = Σ(deposit.usdc) − Σ(withdraw.usdc)`**. Internal moves EXCLUDED: `accountClassTransfer` (own perp↔spot) + `spotTransfer` (token move to another addr) — confirmed against the live ledger 2026-06-21 (10 events: **5 deposits $153.36 − 1 withdraw $31.18 = $122.18**; 2 accountClassTransfer $64.36 + 2 spotTransfer excluded). Address read from **`HL_WALLET_ADDRESS`** (env, public, fail-open if absent).
- **BASE-B3 — cache + fail-open (wired into the reader).** Module-level `createSwrCache` (the proven single-flight primitive), **1h TTL** (deposits change ~monthly), `staleWhileRevalidate` so a failed refresh serves the last-good value. `fetchLifetimeNetDeposits()` **THROWS on any failure** (no/bad address, network/HTTP/timeout, non-array body, no deposit events, net ≤ $1 floor) so the cache never stores a bad/zero base; the exported `getLifetimeNetDeposits()` catches → returns **`null`**. `null` = base unavailable → the % renders **"—"**, **NEVER** a wrong number off a stored proxy ($50 seed / $70.91 snapshot). A wrong base is worse than no base on a money-path display.
- **BASE-B2 — `route.ts` override (NOT query_auto_state.py — Ghost-approved deviation).** The prompt's wording was "edit `realized_base` in query_auto_state.py", but Python spawns are **ephemeral** → no cross-request cache; a Python reader would re-hit HL every 10s spawn. So the reader + 1h cache live in the long-running Node process, and `/api/auto/state` overrides the base TS-side: `getLifetimeNetDeposits()` joins the existing `Promise.all`; when the base is usable, every window's `realized_base = deposits` and `realized_pct[k] = round(realized[k]/deposits*100, 4)` (4-dp, matching the old Python rounding); when null, every window's pct/base stays null → "—". The legacy `pnl_today_pct` tracks the same rebased today value. **`query_auto_state.py` is byte-untouched** — `get_equity_at` + the `realized` numerator + `equity_snapshots` reads all intact (the prompt's "don't delete get_equity_at" is trivially satisfied). `capital-hero.tsx` got comment/tooltip honesty updates only (the renderer ALREADY maps null→"—" at `:258`, so no render-logic change).
- **Live before→after (authed `/api/auto/state`):** `realized_base` now a **single value $122.18 across all 5 windows** (was per-window $47.74 / $21.30 / $37.52 / $70.91 / $70.91). `realized_pct` ALL **−68.03%** (was **−117.34%** ❌), MONTH **−58.19%** (was **−100.39%** ❌), YESTERDAY **−2.97%**, TODAY **0%** (new ET day, 0 closed). **No window past −100%.** Equal dollar P&L now reads as equal % (the window-to-window inconsistency is gone). `equity_usd`/`open_count`/`deployed`/`unrealized` UNCHANGED (heartbeat-sourced).
- **Verification (honesty protocol):** `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0 (both phases). Phase-1 harness over the REAL `createSwrCache` + verbatim reader, run from the Hub box: figure **$122.1798** (matches the VM sample), **cache-hit** (2nd call within TTL → fetch count stays **1**), **sentinel** (no-addr / network-throw / bad-body all → `null`, no crash, failures NOT cached → retried). `npm run build` exit 0 (`/autotrader` 21.9 kB). `sudo systemctl restart trevor-dashboard.service` → active, PID 1305251, NRestarts 0, `[HUB] ready on :3000`, clean boot. **Live sentinel proof:** blanked `HL_WALLET_ADDRESS` + restart (fresh process, cold cache) → all 5 windows `realized_pct`/`realized_base` **null** (render "—"), `/api/auto/state` **HTTP 200** (card does NOT crash), `equity_usd` still correct → restored address + restart → base back to $122.18.
- **Numerator caveat (flagged, NOT touched — Ghost-confirmed out of scope):** the `realized` numerator (`compute_windows`) sums only `pnl_usd`, undercounting by `partial_pnl_realized` ([[auto-trades-pnl-semantics]]). Pre-existing; this wave is **denominator-only**. Left as-is.
- **Regression posture:** Hub display denominator only, no bot restart → the 5 bot-side recurring-bug canaries (signal dedup/cooldown, HOLD-card delete, orphaned reminders, reply handler, results auto-delete) trivially untouched. No `auto_config`/flag/DB-schema write, no new npm deps, no design tokens, no sacred file (none on this box).
- **Files (3 + docs):** `src/lib/hl-deposits.ts` (new reader) · `src/app/api/auto/state/route.ts` (base override) · `src/components/autotrader-v2/capital-hero.tsx` (comment/tooltip) · `CLAUDE.md` (this entry). `.env.local` gained `HL_WALLET_ADDRESS` (public address, **gitignored — NOT committed**); `tsconfig.tsbuildinfo` + untracked recon docs NOT staged. Lock-guarded every file (`scripts/locks/`, owners `BASE-B1/B2/B3`); committed via `scripts/locks/git_commit_serialized.sh` — `30d3631` [BASE-B1 reader] · `35ce84c` [BASE-B2 override] · this docs commit — on `master`, **NOT pushed** (held for Ghost). Locks released after.
- **⚠️ Preference change (2026-06-21) — INTENTIONAL OVERRIDE of WA-P1.** The Auto Capital realized-% base changes from **per-window start-of-window equity** (WA-P1, 2026-06-12, `equity_snapshots`) → **ONE fixed lifetime HL net-deposits figure** (all capital in − out, ever; `deposit − withdraw` from `userNonFundingLedgerUpdates`, internal transfers excluded). The figure is fetched TS-side (`src/lib/hl-deposits.ts`, direct public-HL `fetch`, 1h `createSwrCache`, fail-open `null`→"—"), NOT computed in `query_auto_state.py` (whose ephemeral spawns can't hold the cross-request cache). The base is the SAME for every window, so equal dollar P&L reads as equal % and the % can't exceed −100% of real capital. **Gotcha:** the public wallet address lives in `.env.local` as `HL_WALLET_ADDRESS` (gitignored, NOT in git) — if an env reset / fresh box / CC-env-sync drops it, the realized-% silently fails open to "—" (not a crash); re-add `HL_WALLET_ADDRESS=0x…` to restore. Supersedes WA-P1's start-of-window-equity base and the 2026-05-29 RM-PNL "base = current equity" note for the realized-% denominator.

### 2026-06-20 — B1+B2: deregister 6 dead shadows + stale-when-expected monitoring alarm + register signal_ab_results + would_fire_v1 de-rank — DEPLOYED
- **Two sibling shadow-overhaul waves committed together** — they touched the same two Hub registries (`query_shadow_registry.py` + `query_shadow_status.py`), so splitting the intermingled hunks was fragile; Ghost-approved a single combined `[B1+B2]` commit. WSL `TrevorHub` (`ghost@Ghost`), SOLO on `master`. Read-only over the litestream replica (`mode=ro`, zero write SQL); no bot/VM file, no `auto_config`/flag/DB-schema write, no money path. Sacred **13/13 OK** (Hub never touches `/home/trevor/trevor/`).
- **B1 — deregister 6 confirmed-dead shadows** ([[a2-dead-shadow-cutlist]]). `query_shadow_registry.py` gains a `DEREGISTERED` frozenset (filtered in `_enumerate`); `query_shadow_status.py` moves them `TABLE_DEFS`→`RETIRED_TABLE_DEFS`: **`regime_gate_shadow` (v1), `whale_source_shadow_log`, `group_weight_shadow`** (B1 2026-06-20) + `leverage_v2_shadow`, `exit_engine_shadow`, `orderflow_entry_shadow` (P06/prior context). Tables + historical rows RETAINED (additive-DB; reverse by removing a name). **The LIVE v2 siblings are NOT cut** — `regime_gate_v2_shadow` stays registered (verified ACTIVE); `regime_gate_shadow` (v1) + `group_weight_shadow` share flags with the live v2 scorers (`REGIME_GATE_V2_SHADOW` / `GROUP_WEIGHTS_V2`) → **deregister-ONLY, NO flag flip** (those flag names appear only in explanatory comments — byte-identical, untouched).
- **B2 Fix 1 — the monitoring blind spot (highest-leverage).** `_classify()` in BOTH registries only alarmed on `rows==0`, so a registered `expected_active=True` shadow that goes silent with `rows>0` was silently demoted to DORMANT — indistinguishable from by-design dormant. `regime_gate_shadow` read DORMANT for ~20 days unnoticed. **Fix:** a new **STALE** status — `expected_active && rows>0 && silence > threshold`, where `threshold = max(12×median_gap, 2×longest-ever-gap, 6h floor)`, capped at 21d, derived per-table from its OWN write cadence (generic, never hardcoded). The `2×max-gap` term is load-bearing: it stops activity-driven/bursty exit shadows (e.g. `trail_v3`, historically quiet ~9 days between trail bursts → 18d threshold) from false-firing during a normal lull. Gated `STALE_ALARM_ENABLED` (default-OFF→**ON** after the gate proved before/after). Surfaced as a prominent red **"⚠ N STALE — registered active but silent beyond cadence"** section at the TOP of the Shadow page (never the hidden toggle), a red `STALE` count in the freshness line, a red `STALE` badge + per-card silence-vs-cadence telemetry (`compact-shadow-card.tsx`). **Proof (frozen real-replica snapshot):** flag OFF → `regime_gate_shadow` DORMANT (silent); flag ON → **STALE** (silence 20.3d > 12.5d threshold). `trail_v3`/`alo`/`partial_trigger` stay ACTIVE (zero false positives). **Live deployed:** registry `by_status STALE:0` (guard armed-but-dormant — every registered expected_active table is writing; the one real stale table, `regime_gate` v1, is B1-deregistered).
- **B2 Fix 5 — register `signal_ab_results`** (2k+ rows, writing live every signal, but invisible — no "shadow" in the name, not enumerated). Added to `EXTRA_TABLES` + an OVERRIDE (Scoring, expected_active). **Live: ACTIVE, 2020 rows.**
- **B2 Fix 2 (Hub-side de-rank) — `would_fire_v1` dropped from `DIV_PRIORITY`.** `live_partial_shadow.would_fire_v1` is a hardcoded-`True` stub (all 1775 rows =1), so it falsely read 100% would-fire → promotion-ready. De-ranked so a stub column never feeds promotion (**live: `live_partial` promotion=na**). The writer-side NULL fix is VM-side (handoff below).
- **VM-tab split (Fixes #2 writer / #3 / #4)** — bot writers live under `/home/trevor/trevor/` (forbidden from a Hub prompt). Handoff spec delivered to #downloads: **#2** set `live_executor.py:3205-3206` `would_fire_v1/v2=None`; **#3** add a separate ~100bps audit-only `alerted` threshold (the live 5%/500bps gate never trips on the real 122.7bps peaks → `alerted=1` count is 0; keep Discord alerts at 5%); **#4** log the FAIL/BLOCK branch on 6 PASS-only gates (`trend_floor`, `exhaustion_entry`, `orderflow_entry`, `cvd_oi_exit`, `reversal_exit`, `session_exit`).
- **Gates:** `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0; `npm run build` exit 0 (`/intel` 14 kB); both Python `py_compile` OK; read-only confirmed (`mode=ro`, no write SQL). `sudo systemctl restart trevor-dashboard.service` → active, PID 922989, NRestarts 0, `[HUB] TREVOR Hub ready on http://127.0.0.1:3000` (only the benign `url.parse` DEP0169). Live-verified authed `/api/shadow/registry`: STALE field + `stale_count` present, `signal_ab_results` ACTIVE/2020, `live_partial` promotion=na, `regime_gate_shadow` absent.
- **Regression posture:** Hub read-only registries + display, no bot restart → the 5 bot-side recurring-bug canaries (signal dedup/cooldown, HOLD-card delete, orphaned reminders, reply handler, results auto-delete) trivially untouched. No `auto_config`/flag/DB-schema write, no new npm deps, no sacred file (none on this box).

Lock-guarded (owner `B2`); committed via `scripts/locks/git_commit_serialized.sh` on `master`.

The `2×max-gap` term prevents false positives on bursty/activity-driven exit shadows. ### 2026-06-19 — B4: Health home — Data-Freshness/Drift + Reconcile cards, /api/hub/drift-state endpoint, Health nav promotion, panel badges (Phase 1 Hub) — DEPLOYED
- **The Hub is now the home for data + health.** FIX/EXTEND of the existing `/memory?tab=health` view (`<HealthSection>`), not a build. WSL `TrevorHub` box (`ghost@Ghost`), SOLO on `master`, port :3000. Read-only over the heartbeat + the litestream replica; no bot/VM file, no `auto_config`/flag/DB-schema write, no money path. **Phase 2 (the VM `reconcile` heartbeat category) is HANDED OFF to the VM tab** — see the handoff block Ghost runs there.
- **New surfaces: 2 cards, 1 endpoint, 1 nav, 2 badges. Reused: everything else** (`ui/` primitives + `@theme` tokens only; mirrors `profit-risk-panel.tsx` structure — no new design language).
- **`GET /api/hub/drift-state` (new, read-only)** — computes the live-vs-replica open-count delta + replica age from ingredients that already reach the Hub (no new VM probe for the freshness half): LIVE = Observatory heartbeat `categories.autotrader.open_count` (server-side `fetch` of the `:8443` Serve, mirrors `heartbeat/route.ts`); REPLICA = `COUNT(*) FROM active_trades WHERE status='open'` over `file:{db}?mode=ro` (the `nav-badges` pattern); replica age = `os.stat(os.path.realpath(db)).st_mtime` (the `query_shadow_registry._replica_age` idiom — `trevor.db` symlink → the continuously-restored replica). **Both reads independent + error-handled (`Promise.all`, each half try/caught → null) — structurally cannot 500.** Payload: `{live_count, replica_count, drift_delta, drift, replica_age_seconds, replica_mtime, live_positions[], replica_positions[], live_source, errors{}}`. Live-proven: `live_count:0` (`live_source:"heartbeat"`), `replica_count:0`, `drift_delta:0` → in-sync, `replica_age_seconds:470`; bad-db-path read → graceful `replica_error` JSON (no crash).
- **`<DataFreshnessCard>` (new, `memory/data-freshness-card.tsx`)** — reads drift-state (30s poll), `border-l-4` severity: `delta===0` → mint "IN SYNC"; `delta≠0` & replica age ≤ ~30m restore window → gold "LAGGING (replica ~Nm — expected, not an error)"; age > ~30m → red "STALE beyond window"; heartbeat unreachable → neutral "LIVE UNAVAILABLE" (replica-only). This is the home for the deferred "2 open vs 3" confusion — labels the gap as *lagging/expected*, NOT *wrong* (implements the W-H-P4-HUB doctrine: live/open ← heartbeat, closed ← replica). Reuses `Card`/`MetricTile`/`Pill`/`Skeleton` + `@theme` tokens.
- **`<ReconcileHealthCard>` (new, `memory/reconcile-health-card.tsx`)** — reads the heartbeat `reconcile` category through the existing `/api/heartbeat` proxy (30s poll): last reconcile time/age, status, HL/DB/gaps tiles, per-kind open-mismatch pills, queue pending/escalated + stuck flag. **GRACEFUL ABSENCE: renders a clean `EmptyState` ("Reconcile telemetry pending — Phase 2/VM") when the category is absent or the heartbeat is unreachable — NEVER errors** (proven: the live heartbeat has 18 categories, no `reconcile` yet → EmptyState renders). Phase 2 lights it up with no further Hub change.
- **Both cards mounted in `<HealthSection>`** after `<KillswitchControlCard>`, before `<HeartbeatView>` (the existing Killswitch + Heartbeat + Sentinels are untouched).
- **Health nav promotion (`navigation.ts`)** — additive top-level **HEALTH** zone (`/health`, `Activity` icon, reuses the `green`/mint accent so `accentTextClass`/`accentGlowClass` need no new case; single-view, no subTabs). New `src/app/health/page.tsx` renders the same `<HealthSection>` inside MemoryZoneView's `mx-auto max-w-screen-2xl` centering wrap. **The old `/memory?tab=health` deep link is PRESERVED** (the memory zone keeps its `health` sub-tab — live-proven HTTP 200 with both new cards). Bottom-nav/sidebar reflow automatically (4→5 zones via `ZONES.map` + `justify-around`, no hardcoded grid). `/health` + `/api/hub/drift-state` are auth-gated by `middleware.ts` (neither matches the `/api/health` liveness allowlist).

Makes those replica-sourced open counts read as *lagging-and-explained*. Live-proven rendering `REPLICA ~11M` ×2.

### 2026-06-18 — FLAG-ON: flip `NEXT_PUBLIC_LIVE_TERMINAL` ON (=1) — RM-LIVE live-terminal overlay live for all sessions — DEPLOYED
- **Display-only flag flip, no math / no data source / no bot files.** WSL `TrevorHub` box (`ghost@Ghost`), SOLO on `master`, HEAD `287f512` (the B4/B8 wave tip — all RM-LIVE consumers B2–B8 committed+pushed). Turns the RM-LIVE live-terminal overlay ON for every session by setting the build-time env flag, rebuilding, and restarting. The feature shipped dark behind `NEXT_PUBLIC_LIVE_TERMINAL` (default OFF, B1–B8); this flips the base ON. No `.env.local` other keys touched, no DB/`auto_config`/gateway/VM, no money path.
- **The flip (append-only):** appended `NEXT_PUBLIC_LIVE_TERMINAL=1` to `/home/ghost/projects/trevor-dashboard/.env.local` (newline-safe `>>`, never overwrite). All 17 prior keys intact (esp. `PORT=3000`); the file went 17→18 keys, `chmod 600` preserved. `.env.local` is gitignored → NOT committed; the flag lives in this box's local env + build. Lock-guarded the edit via `scripts/locks/lock_acquire.sh .env.local FLAG-ON`.
- **Why a rebuild is required:** `NEXT_PUBLIC_*` is **inlined at build time**. `src/lib/live-terminal.ts:47` reads `process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_LIVE_TERMINAL === "1"` as the base of `isLiveTerminalEnabled()` (with a `localStorage "trevor-live-terminal"` "1"/"0" client override on top). So flipping = set env → `npm run build` → restart.
- **Flag-baked evidence (HARD gate — proven on the freshly-built bundle BEFORE restart):** `grep -rl "trevor-live-terminal" .next/static/chunks` → present in chunk `2335-b0623829c9f02dd7.js` (live layer in the bundle, not tree-shaken — vs B1's `tree-shaken / unimported`). The minified flag module reads `...if("1"===e)return!0;if("0"===e)return!1}catch(e){}return!0` — the trailing **`return!0`** is the env base `process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_LIVE_TERMINAL === "1"` constant-folded by terser to **`!0` (true)** → the flag resolved to `"1"` at build (OFF would fold to `return!1`); the localStorage `"0"` override still forces OFF, preserving the rollback contract.
- **Build + deploy:** `npm run build` exit 0 (✓ Compiled successfully in 5.6s; `/autotrader` **21.7 kB**, up from 21.1 kB flag-OFF — the live layer is now included). `sudo systemctl restart trevor-dashboard.service` → **active**, fresh PID 3630815 (was 3604101), NRestarts 0, `[HUB] TREVOR Hub ready on http://127.0.0.1:3000` (PORT=3000 preserved). Verification gate (monitor-based, no sleep-poll): `/api/health` → **200**, `/autotrader` unauth → **307** (auth gate intact); boot log clean since the restart timestamp (only the pre-existing benign `url.parse` DEP0169 deprecation line). The visual tick+flash on a live position is Ghost-verified in-browser; CC confirms the deploy + flag-baked, not the pixel-level flash.
- **Rollback (verbatim):** to flip OFF — set `NEXT_PUBLIC_LIVE_TERMINAL=0` in `/home/ghost/projects/trevor-dashboard/.env.local` (or remove the line), `npm run build`, `sudo systemctl restart trevor-dashboard.service` (~2–3 min). The OFF path is byte-identical to today's prior production. A no-rebuild per-browser OFF is also available: `localStorage.setItem("trevor-live-terminal","0")`.
- **Regression posture:** Hub env + rebuild only — the 5 bot-side recurring-bug canaries (signal dedup/cooldown, HOLD-card delete, orphaned reminders, reply handler, results auto-delete) are all bot-side → trivially untouched. No bot file, no `trevor.db`/`auto_config`/flag-row/schema write, no gateway/VM, no design tokens, no new npm deps, no sacred file (none on this box).
- **Files (2 committed):** `CLAUDE.md` (this entry + corrected the B1 "consumers arrive in Round 2" line to record B2–B8 landed at HEAD `287f512`) · `BEHAVIOR_RULES.md` (SECTION 2 live-terminal rule: `default OFF` → `ON as of 2026-06-18` + rollback). `.env.local` is gitignored (the flag flip itself) — NOT committed; `tsconfig.tsbuildinfo` + untracked recon docs NOT staged. Lock-guarded both docs via `scripts/locks/lock_acquire.sh` (owner `FLAG-ON`); committed via `scripts/locks/git_commit_serialized.sh`; locks released after. Commit `docs(hub): live-terminal flag ON [FLAG-ON]` on `master`, then `git push origin master`.
- **Preference (2026-06-18):** Live-terminal flag `NEXT_PUBLIC_LIVE_TERMINAL` set to **ON (=1)** on 2026-06-18 — RM-LIVE overlay live for all sessions. Rollback: set `=0` (or remove the line) + `npm run build` + restart. ### 2026-06-18 — RM-LIVE Wave B / B1: dormant HL `allMids` WS price store (live-terminal foundation, Round 1) — COMMITTED, NOT pushed
- **Foundation for the browser-side live-price terminal — DORMANT, additive, inert at rest.** WSL `TrevorHub` box (`ghost@Ghost`), on `master`, lock-guarded (owner `B1-live-ws`). Path A (additive, no VM touch). B1 is the **CLAUDE.md + BEHAVIOR_RULES doc owner for Round 1**; the sibling Round-1 prompts B2 (`<LiveValue>` primitive) and B3 (`NEXT_PUBLIC_LIVE_TERMINAL` flag, default OFF) do NOT edit these docs. **⚠️ Correction (2026-06-18, FLAG-ON):** B1's original line read *"Consumers arrive in Round 2 — nothing imports the store yet"* — that was forward-looking (B1 ran first and documented the plan before the consumers landed). **Consumers B2–B8 have since landed and are all committed/pushed at HEAD `287f512`** — the `<LiveValue>` primitive, the position-card / watchlist / pricestrip / capital-hero overlays, the stat-panel flashes, and the WS reconnect + status pill. So the store is no longer dormant; flipping `NEXT_PUBLIC_LIVE_TERMINAL=1` (done 2026-06-18 — see the FLAG-ON entry at the top of this changelog) surfaces the overlay live for all sessions.
- **ONE new file: `src/lib/hl-ws-store.ts`** (~210 lines, `"use client"`) — a lazy, subscriber-counted singleton WS client for Hyperliquid's public `allMids` feed (`wss://api.hyperliquid.xyz/ws`, subscribe frame `{"method":"subscribe","subscription":{"type":"allMids"}}`), exposing a `useLiveMark(ticker, enabled=false)` hook (returns `{price, ts, connected}`). Module-level singleton: `Map<TICKER,{price,ts}>` of marks + `Set` of listeners + ref-count + one `WebSocket | null`. `subscribe()` opens the socket on the FIRST subscriber (or re-opens if a prior socket was cleared by a drop), `unsubscribe` closes it when the LAST subscriber leaves. The hook subscribes ONLY when `enabled===true` — so Round-2 consumers call it unconditionally (React hook rules) wired to the live-terminal flag (`useLiveMark(t, liveTerminalFlag)`); flag-OFF subscribes to nothing and opens no socket → **byte-identical to the Hub today**. Implemented via `useSyncExternalStore` (SSR-safe inert server snapshot, cached per-ticker snapshot, no tearing).
- **Ticker keying MIRRORS `/api/prices`** (`src/app/api/prices/route.ts`): marks keyed by `normalizeCoin(coin) = coin.toUpperCase()` (the route's `hlByUpper` ingest) and looked up by `ticker.toUpperCase()` (the route's `t.toUpperCase()` match) — **uppercase is the MATCH key only, never a stored/rendered casing**, so `kPEPE` (HL's 1000x PEPE perp) resolves with the exact `kPEPE` TICKER key a consumer would pass to `/api/prices`. No `.toUpperCase()` of a coin as a displayed value.
- **Inert at rest (DO-NO-HARM contract):** `new WebSocket(...)` lives inside `openSocket()` (called only from `subscribe()`), NEVER at module eval — so importing the module opens nothing, and with zero importers/subscribers (today's state) ZERO sockets are opened. The browser talks to HL directly: no new call hits the WSL server, the VM gateway, or HL REST from the server — **no VM load**. Resilience is minimal-only (on close/error: `connected=false` + clear the socket so a later subscribe re-opens); **full reconnect/backoff is B7, deliberately NOT built here**.
- **Verification gate (all PASS):** `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0 (zero errors project-wide; my file clean). `npm run build` exit 0 — the store is **tree-shaken** (unimported → in no route bundle; route sizes unchanged, `/autotrader` 21.1 kB). `grep -rn "hl-ws-store" src` (excluding the file) → **empty** (nothing imports it). Inertness proof: the sole `new WebSocket` is line 130 inside `openSocket()`, never module-level. Sacred manifest 13/13 OK before + after (VM, read-only — Hub work never touches it). No `npm run build` restart of `trevor-dashboard.service` (the round's single push+restart happens after all 3 are reviewed).
- **Regression posture:** dormant additive Hub file, no consumers, no service restart → the 5 bot-side recurring-bug canaries (signal dedup/cooldown, HOLD-card delete, orphaned reminders, reply handler, results auto-delete) trivially untouched (all bot-side). `/api/prices` + every existing component byte-identical (not edited). No `auto_config`/flag/DB write, no design tokens, no new npm deps, no money path, no sacred file (none on this box).
- **Files (3):** `src/lib/hl-ws-store.ts` (new) · `CLAUDE.md` (this entry) · `BEHAVIOR_RULES.md` (SECTION 2 flag rule registering `NEXT_PUBLIC_LIVE_TERMINAL`). Lock-guarded all three via `scripts/locks/lock_acquire.sh` (owner `B1-live-ws`); committed via `scripts/locks/git_commit_serialized.sh` (`git add` of only these 3; `tsconfig.tsbuildinfo` + untracked recon docs NOT staged); locks released after. Commit `feat(hub): add dormant HL allMids WS price store + live-terminal docs [B1]` on `master` — **NOT pushed** (Ghost pushes once the whole round is reviewed). Note: registered the flag in BEHAVIOR_RULES **SECTION 2 (Dashboard Coding Standards)** — the Hub mirror has no separate "feature registry" section (Section 4 stubs the VM's registry as VM-only; the Hub's feature history is this Wave Changelog), and SECTION 2 is where dashboard flag conventions (`LIVE_EDIT_ENABLED`, `HUB_*_TOGGLE_ENABLED`) already live.

### 2026-06-18 — B1: portfolio_positions soft-delete leak — every Hub read filtered `status IS NOT 'deleted'` + hard-DELETE → soft-delete UPDATE — DEPLOYED
- **Closes the Hub-side soft-delete leak DBA-01 C-P3 flagged.** The bot converted `portfolio_positions` hard-deletes → soft-deletes (`status='deleted'`) and filtered every bot reader, but the Hub's standalone `manage_portfolio.py` CLI still had a bare `SELECT * FROM portfolio_positions ORDER BY opened_at DESC` (no status filter → would surface a soft-deleted row) plus its own hard `DELETE`. **0 live rows today (nil impact now)** — a real leak once positions repopulate. **WSL Hub only (`/home/ghost/projects/trevor-dashboard`), SOLO on `master`; read-only over the 0444 replica, no bot/VM file touched.**
- **Sentinel confirmed = `status='deleted'`** (read-only `ssh vm`): bot `portfolio_manager.py:172` `UPDATE … SET status='deleted'`; `tests/test_soft_delete_a2.py` ("remove_position → status='deleted'"); the 06-12 snapshot still hard-DELETEd while 06-16/17/18 soft-delete → pinpoints the C-P3 conversion. **⚠️ Prompt-vs-reality correction (Ghost-confirmed at the gate):** the prompt's example `WHERE status != 'closed'` was WRONG — `open` AND `closed` are legitimate, intentionally-shown statuses; only `'deleted'` is excluded. Live schema has a `status` TEXT col, NO `deleted_at`/`is_deleted` col, 0 rows.
- **Read-side fix — NULL-safe `status IS NOT 'deleted'` on EVERY read** (so a NULL-status row still shows, only `'deleted'` is hidden): `cmd_list` (the display leak) · `cmd_update`/`cmd_remove` existence checks · `cmd_update`/`cmd_close` re-reads · analytics GROUP-BY aggregate + totals. Left the leverage query (`WHERE status='open' AND leverage>1`, already excludes deleted). Defense-in-depth: a deleted row is invisible to every read, so no mutation path can re-close/re-remove it.
- **Write-side — hard DELETE → soft-delete UPDATE (`cmd_remove`):** `DELETE FROM portfolio_positions WHERE id=?` → `UPDATE … SET status='deleted' WHERE id=?` for bot-convention parity + the additive-only law (recoverable, never hard-delete). **MOOT on the live 0444 ro replica today** — the write fails harmlessly with SQLITE_READONLY (no route wires to this CLI; dangerous Hub writes ride the gateway, never the replica) — but correct-by-construction if the CLI is ever pointed at a writable DB. Documented inline.
- **DB path:** `manage_portfolio.py` opens `sqlite3.connect(TREVOR_DB_PATH)` (plain read-write, no `mode=ro`) → `.env.local:15` `TREVOR_DB_PATH=/home/ghost/trevor-replica/trevor.db`, which is `-r--r--r--` (0444). Reads work; any write fails at the OS level. No write attempted against the replica this wave (only `list`/`analytics` read paths run). `trade_reflections`: ZERO Hub readers (grep) — C-P3 was right.
- **Verified:** `py_compile` clean; grep proves every SELECT carries the filter (the only `WHERE id=?` matches lacking it are the 3 UPDATE writes, each guarded by a now-filtered existence check) + no hard DELETE remains. In-memory proof (real schema, open/closed/deleted/NULL rows): cmd_list returns open+closed+NULL but HIDES deleted; existence(deleted)=None / existence(open)=row / existence(NULL)=row (NULL-safe); analytics total 3-not-4; control (no filter) confirms the deleted row WOULD leak without the fix. Live read-path against the real replica: `list`→`{"positions":[]}`, `analytics`→clean zeros (0 rows); replica still 0444, no write attempted. `npm run build` clean; `trevor-dashboard.service` restarted → `[HUB] ready on :3000`, NRestarts 0, `/api/health` 200. (Build+restart = formal deploy; the Python helper is hot-spawned + unwired, so the fix was live on the next spawn regardless.)
- **Regression posture:** Hub-only Python helper, read-only over the replica → the 5 bot-side recurring-bug canaries trivially untouched. No `.ts`/`.tsx` changed, no DB schema/write, no `auto_config`/flag, no new npm deps, no design tokens, no bot/VM file touched, no sacred file (none on this box).
- **Files (2):** `manage_portfolio.py` (6 SELECT/aggregate filters + hard-DELETE→soft-delete UPDATE + 2 docstrings) · `CLAUDE.md` (this entry). Lock-guarded both via `scripts/locks/lock_acquire.sh`; committed via `scripts/locks/git_commit_serialized.sh`; released after. `tsconfig.tsbuildinfo` NOT staged. Commit on `master` — **manual `git push origin master`** (WSL has no auto-push).
- **Preference (2026-06-18):** the Hub `portfolio_positions` soft-delete sentinel is **`status='deleted'`** (matches bot DBA-01 C-P3); `open` and `closed` are legitimate displayed statuses. Every Hub read of the table filters NULL-safe **`status IS NOT 'deleted'`** (a NULL-status row still shows). `manage_portfolio.py` soft-deletes (never hard-deletes) — additive-only/recoverable. Do NOT use `status != 'closed'` (it hides legitimate closed positions).

- **The gap was the docs, not the code.** WSL `TrevorHub` box (`ghost@Ghost`), SOLO on `master`. The working sender already exists + was proven this session-era ([[downloads-delivery-sudo-trevor]], W-J-P1 `…975692`, W-H-P2-DOWNLOADS `…660487`): `scripts/deliver_report.py` posts to #downloads via the Hub webhook AND auto-registers in the manifest. This wave writes the same non-negotiable contract the VM side carries (DELIV-01) into the docs CC reads in Phase 0. **Doc-only — the sender is UNTOUCHED (byte-identical); no Hub app/route/gateway/manifest/DB change.**

- **No competing delivery path.** `grep -rl deliver scripts/ | grep -i report` → only `scripts/deliver_report.py`. The 3 webhook-touching files are ONE layered chain, not competitors: `deliver_report.py` (canonical wrapper + scrub) → `discord_file_delivery.py:post_file_sync` (sender) → `download_manager.py:save_download` (manifest backend, defined `download_manager.py:207`, called at `discord_file_delivery.py:163`). ### 2026-06-16 — LOCK-WSL: per-file lock + commit-serialization guard (replaces retired worktrees) — BUILT + PROVEN, not pushed
- **New parallel-safety infrastructure on the WSL Hub** so multiple CC sessions (tmux, auto-mode) can edit + commit on `master` at once without colliding on files or the git index. Mirrors the VM **LOCK-VM** contract so both boxes behave identically; worktrees retired 2026-06-16. **Additive, self-contained — `scripts/locks/*.sh` + `.gitignore` + this entry + the new `## Parallel safety: file locks` section only; no dashboard app/service/DB touch.**
- **Phase 0 — native ext4 confirmed** (`stat -f` → ext2/ext3 family, `/dev/sde`, NOT drvfs/v9fs) → atomic `mkdir` is the safe lock primitive. No `.sacred_manifest.sha256` on this box (sacred files are VM-only — nothing to verify). `flock` is present but deliberately NOT depended on (VM parity).
- **Design = lock is a directory + a `meta` file (`owner epoch pid`).** 5 scripts + 1 sourced helper + a self-test under `scripts/locks/` (POSIX `sh`, `set -eu`): **`lock_acquire.sh <relpath> [owner] [stale]`** (atomic `mkdir` claim; reclaim ONLY when age>900s AND owner PID dead via `kill -0`; reclaim is an atomic `mv`-aside so two reclaimers can't both win; meta records the long-lived HOLDER pid via `LOCK_HOLDER_PID`/`$PPID`, NOT the transient acquire pid; block up to 600s then fail loud) · **`lock_release.sh`** (release only your own lock, idempotent) · **`with_file_lock.sh <path> -- <cmd>`** (acquire → run → release via trap, even on failure/signal) · **`git_commit_serialized.sh -- "<cmd>"`** (global `.locks/__git_index__.lock`, **quoted-string form** so `git add X && git commit` runs as ONE unit under the lock — not split on `&&`; **refuses bare `git add .` / `-A` / `--all`**, even behind `git -C <dir>`) · **`lock_status.sh`** (read-only diagnostic) · **`selftest.sh`** (the concurrency proof harness) · **`_common.sh`** (sourced: `encode_path` `/`→`__`, `resolve_lock_dir`). `.locks/` gitignored.
- **Phase 2 — every guarantee proven live (`selftest.sh`, 8/8 PASS, in a `mktemp` scratch + throwaway git repo; live repo history untouched):** (1) same-file mutual exclusion — 10 concurrent on one flag → observed max concurrency **1**, all 10 writes present, **0** overlaps; (2) different-file parallelism — 6 distinct locks ran in **615ms** (serial would be ~3600ms); (3) stale reclaim BOTH ways — old+dead reclaimed; fresh+live NOT stolen (acquire times out, meta intact); (4) commit serialization — 5 concurrent committers → 6 commits, linear, `git fsck` clean, no `index.lock` residue; (5) `git add .` refused (exit 3, zero mutation); (5b) every bare-add form refused incl. `git -C /tmp add .`, specific-file add allowed; (6) slash-path `app/page.tsx` → `app__page.tsx.lock`, locked + released. One harness-tuning fix during Phase 2 (retry granularity 1s→0.2s + overlap line-count) — lock scripts unchanged.
- **Known accepted edge:** the `/`→`__` encoding means a literal path containing `__` collides with a `/`-encoded path (matches the VM contract by design).
- **Regression posture:** new infra only — no dashboard app code, no service restart, no `trevor.db`/`auto_config`/flag/schema touch, no sacred manifest on this box. The bot-side recurring-bug canaries are untouched (Hub-side tooling only).
- **Files:** `scripts/locks/{_common,lock_acquire,lock_release,with_file_lock,git_commit_serialized,lock_status,selftest}.sh` (new) · `.gitignore` (+`.locks/`) · `CLAUDE.md` (this entry + the new `## Parallel safety: file locks` section). Committed via `git_commit_serialized.sh` itself (dogfood). `tsconfig.tsbuildinfo` NOT staged; the pre-existing **RM-PIPE-PUBLIC** CLAUDE.md change left UNSTAGED in the working tree for its own commit (selective-staged out). Commit `feat: add per-file lock + commit-serialization guard (replaces worktrees)` on `master` — **NOT pushed** (held for Ghost).

### 2026-06-15 — RM-PIPE-W2: 4 live-data routes off the dead FQDN/hosts-pin → new tailnet suffix (MagicDNS, pin-independent) — DEPLOYED (live-200/cert-SAN DEFERRED on the keystone VM :8443 Serve)
- **Read-path follow-up to RM-PIPE-W1's tailnet IP migration.** SOLO on `master`, WSL `TrevorHub` box (`ghost@Ghost`). 4 Hub routes fetched the VM Observatory at the **dead OLD MagicDNS FQDN** `https://trevor-prime.tail068f72.ts.net:8443/api/heartbeat` and only resolved because `/etc/hosts` pins that OLD FQDN → the new IP. That pin is a W3-scheduled landmine; the instant it's removed all 4 routes die on `gaierror`. This wave repoints them onto the **new-suffix FQDN** `trevor-prime.tail2bf7a3.ts.net` which **MagicDNS resolves natively** (pin-independent) and which matches the cert SAN — so they no longer DEPEND on the pin. New VM IP `100.96.113.73`, new suffix `tail2bf7a3.ts.net` ([[trevor-tailnet-migration-ipost09122003]]).
- **The swap (surgical, 5 string literals across 4 files):** `tail068f72` → `tail2bf7a3` only — host suffix, nothing else. `heartbeat/route.ts:7,8` (TWO: base + `/refresh`) · `status/route.ts:43` · `auto/state/route.ts:143` · `auto/leverage-regime/route.ts:160`. URL duplicated as literals, NOT a shared constant → 4 file edits (introducing a shared const would be a refactor, out of scope). Port (`:8443`) + path (`/api/heartbeat`) unchanged — confirmed no port/path drift.
- **TLS-correct target.** All 4 call the HTTPS:8443 endpoint via bare `fetch(URL, {cache:"no-store", signal:AbortSignal.timeout(...)})` — **no `rejectUnauthorized:false`, no custom agent, no `NODE_TLS_*`** → standard Node TLS hostname verification applies, so the cert SAN must match the URL host. The new-suffix FQDN is the migration-proof target (MagicDNS-resolvable + matches a Tailscale-issued SAN, which tracks the live MagicDNS name).
- **⚠️ live-200 + cert-SAN proof DEFERRED — the VM Observatory `:8443` Serve is DOWN.** Phase 0 probe: VM reachable on the tailnet (`ping 100.96.113.73` 0% loss) but `:8443` is **`Connection refused`** on BOTH old and new FQDN (same IP) + raw TCP + `curl` (`http_code=000`). - **Phase 5 proof (cross-box, end-to-end):** (a) WSL→VM `curl http://100.96.113.73:3940/healthz` → **HTTP 401** (auth-gated alive, connect 55ms) — VM gateway reachable on the new IP. (b) **End-to-end no-op write** `POST /gateway/write {op:"watchlist.remove", ticker:"ZZZTEST"}` → **HTTP 423** `{ok:false, gate:"HUB_LIST_WRITE_ENABLED"}` in 0.23s — the `gate` verdict is the VM gateway's authoritative `auto_config` flag check, so the write traversed Hub→Tailscale→**live VM** and got a real response (NOT 502/504/000 — the exact broken mode). Flag OFF → **correctly locked, zero mutation** (a real mutation would need flipping `HUB_LIST_WRITE_ENABLED`, Ghost-controlled — not done). (c) VM monitor `write_path: OK — HTTP 401` for 2 consecutive 5-min cycles; state file `.hub_monitor_state.json` → `{"write_path":"ok"}`.

### 2026-06-15 — RM-CLOSE-E1: re-point /api/memory/daily to VM `brain/memory` over `ssh vm` + restore the stale ssh-vm target post-tailnet-migration — DEPLOYED
- **Fixes the last deferred RM-CLOSE item: `/api/memory/daily/[date]` was a DEAD pre-migration local-FS read.** SOLO on `master`, WSL `TrevorHub` box (`ghost@Ghost`). So the prompt's "re-point to the replica mode=ro" was **impossible** (nothing in the DB to read). The correct source matches the RM-MEM-A3 sibling memory routes: read `brain/memory/<date>.md` over the **read-only `ssh vm`** pipe.
- **🚨 ROOT BLOCKER found + fixed — the ENTIRE Memory zone was silently broken by the tailnet migration.** `ssh vm` was timing out: the `vm` host targeted `trevor-prime.tail068f72.ts.net`, pinned in `/etc/hosts` to the OLD IP `100.93.113.117`, but `trevor-prime` migrated to `100.96.113.73` (new tailnet — `tailscale ping` pong confirmed; see [[trevor-tailnet-migration-ipost09122003]]). So EVERY RM-MEM-A3 route (ChromaDB / Memory / Brain tabs + Health tiles) was "VM unreachable", not just daily. **Fix (WSL system files, NOT in the repo):** `/etc/hosts` IP `100.93.113.117 → 100.96.113.73`; `~/.ssh/config` `HostName trevor-prime.tail068f72.ts.net → trevor-prime` (clean short name, resolves via /etc/hosts); `~/.ssh/known_hosts` added `trevor-prime` as an alias on the SAME already-trusted host-key line (no TOFU — same VM, unchanged host key). Backups in `/tmp/*.rmclose-e1.bak`. **Post-fix sanity: `ssh vm` reaches `trevor-prime` (91 daily files present); MEMORY 91 entries / BRAIN 8 files / CHROMADB 21 collections all reachable again** — the dead-IP regression is closed for the whole zone.
- **New helper `query_memory_daily.py`** (repo root) — modeled on `query_brain_read.py`: validate `YYYY-MM-DD` locally, base64-embed the date (never touches the remote shell), fetch `brain/memory/<date>.md` over `ssh vm` (stdlib-only remote read, HOME reset to `/home/ghost`), emit one JSON object + exit 0. Statuses `ok` / `not_found` / `error` (VM-unreachable / too-large / read-error). Read-only — no VM write, no schema.
- **Route rewrite** `src/app/api/memory/daily/[date]/route.ts` — `fs.readFileSync` → `runPython("query_memory_daily.py",[date])` + `safeJsonParse`. Mapping: **400** bad date (guard kept) · **200 `{date,content}`** on ok · **404 `{error}`** on genuinely-missing (graceful) · **200 `{error}`** on VM-unreachable/read-error (surfaces the real cause honestly — `safeFetch` drops the body on non-2xx, so a 404 there would MASK an outage as "no data") · **never 500**. Preserves the `{content?,error?}` consumer contract (`brain-editor.tsx:106` `loadDailyMemory`).
- **Build-not-just-restart honored:** `npm run build` (clean, `/memory` 15.4 kB) → `sudo systemctl restart trevor-dashboard.service` → ready on :3000 (PID 1780196, clean boot). Live-verified authed on :3000: unauth → **401** (gate intact); known-good `2026-06-15` → **200** real markdown (`# TREVOR Daily Memory — 2026-06-15`, 406 chars); no-data `2020-01-01` → **404** graceful `{error:"no memory file for 2020-01-01"}`; bad date → **400**. **Honesty proof: live-route content md5 == VM-source (`ssh vm`) content md5 for `2026-06-15` AND `2026-06-13` — byte-identical** → real data, not an empty shell.
- **Regression posture:** Hub display/read-path only, read-only over `ssh vm` + the date-format guard — no bot/VM file written, no `trevor.db`/`auto_config`/flag/schema/`hyperliquid` touch, no new npm deps, no design tokens. The 5 bot-side recurring-bug canaries trivially untouched (Hub read-only).

Plus the WSL system-file fixes (`/etc/hosts`, `~/.ssh/config`, `~/.ssh/known_hosts` — system files, not in the repo, per the W-J-P1 precedent). `tsconfig.tsbuildinfo` + untracked recon docs NOT staged. Commit on `master`.

- **Re-source path = read-only `ssh vm`** (the established WSL pipe; option (a) from the audit). Why it works from the Next.js runtime: `runPython` forces `HOME=/home/trevor`, so each helper's ssh subprocess **explicitly resets `HOME=/home/ghost`** to resolve `~/.ssh/config` + the GCE key. Verified `ssh vm` succeeds under a clean agentless `env -i HOME=/home/trevor` (the exact helper-child context) — server accepts the passphraseless key, so it works under the agentless systemd service too. **Read-only throughout — no VM file written; the only VM commands are `ls`/`cat`/`du`/`systemctl is-active`/`sqlite3 mode=ro`/stdlib `open()`.**
- **CHROMADB (`query_chroma_browse.py`, full rewrite) — pure read-only sqlite, NO PersistentClient (Ghost ruling).** All three modes run a STDLIB-ONLY remote python over `ssh vm python3 -` against `chroma.sqlite3` opened `mode=ro` (never a write-mode client — zero contention with the bot's live client on the 2-vCPU VM). **list** = the `collections⋈segments(METADATA)⋈embeddings` GROUP-BY (per-collection sum == 300,528 ✓). **peek** = sample docs from `embeddings`+`embedding_metadata`. **search** = **KEYWORD** match via the FTS5 trigram `embedding_fulltext_search` (LIKE-substring fallback for <3-char queries) — honestly labeled "keyword … not semantic" in the UI; semantic was declined (would need the write-mode client). User inputs (collection/q) are base64-embedded into the remote program (never touch the remote shell) + bound as SQL params → injection-safe.
- **MEMORY (`query_memory_entries.py`)** — remote stdlib python lists+reads `brain/memory/*.md` (content/size/mtime); the search `q` is filtered **locally on WSL** so it never crosses the pipe. **BRAIN (`query_brain_files.py` + `query_brain_read.py`)** — brain `*.md` listing + single-file content over ssh; the `HUB_BRAIN_EDIT_ENABLED` DB read stays local (already worked via the replica symlink). Subtle **day divider** (Today / Yesterday / "Jun 9") between date groups, so rows show just time-of-day, not a repeated date — *reduces* bulk.

Bad input → null → "—" (no crash). Ghost-approved.

### 2026-06-12 — WA-P2: Custom calendar date-range picker on the Auto Capital card — DEPLOYED
- **Feature.** Added a 6th "Custom" segment to the Auto Capital window control (`capital-hero.tsx`, AUTO → Dashboard). Tapping it opens a phone-friendly date-range picker; the card then shows realized P&L ($) + the correct % for that exact span, reusing the EXISTING query path and **A-P1's `get_equity_at(start_ts)` denominator unchanged**. Custom only DEFINES a (start_ts, end_ts) — **zero new P&L or denominator math.** Display-only; no bot logic, no breaker, no DB writes, no schema change. Depends on WA-P1 (`443b3e0`, present on `master`).
- **⚠️ Phase-0 honesty correction — there was no "window channel" to ride.** The presets are NOT passed to the API: `GET /api/auto/state` is called with no params, `query_auto_state.py` precomputes ALL 5 windows, and the client picks one purely client-side (`LABEL_TO_KEY`). So Custom needed a NEW channel. Chosen (faithful to "same route/helper"): an OPTIONAL `?start=YYYY-MM-DD&end=YYYY-MM-DD` on the SAME route → passed as argv to the SAME helper → adds a `custom` key to `realized`/`realized_pct`/`realized_base`/`realized_count` via the SAME `compute_windows`-style numerator + `get_equity_at` denominator. No args → no `custom` key (preset response byte-identical to before).
- **Picker UI (Ghost-approved Option 1).** Two native `<input type="date">` (start + end) inside the existing `BottomSheet` primitive — **zero new dependency**, OS-native date wheel on a phone, max=ET-today on both (no future), min=start on end (end ≥ start). Validation: Apply disabled until `draftValid` (both set, end ≥ start, neither future); single-day (start == end) allowed → that one ET day. No custom calendar grid built.
- **ET mapping (matches presets exactly).** `_parse_custom_args`: start = ET-midnight(start day); end = ET-midnight(end day **+1**), EXCLUSIVE — same boundary convention as `_et_window_starts`. `compute_custom` sums closed-LIVE `pnl_usd` over `[start_ts, end_ts)` (NULL-pnl excluded/counted), same numerator rule as `compute_windows`. Server-side guards: malformed dates / end<start / **future dates** → no `custom` key (defense-in-depth over the UI's `max=today`).
- **Cache-key discipline (the important one).** The route's `createSwrCache` is keyed `auto-state:<start>:<end>` for custom requests so a custom payload NEVER pollutes/serves the constant `"auto-state"` preset entry, and two distinct ranges don't collide. **Proven live:** after a custom hit the plain preset response has NO `custom` key; Jun1–5 (−3.5908) ≠ Jun6–12 (−28.9193) — distinct.
- **Client fetch.** `capital-hero.tsx` derives the fetch URL from the applied range (`stateUrl`, the effect's sole dep): preset↔preset switches reuse the last payload (no refetch — all presets already present); switching to Custom / changing the range triggers exactly one refetch. 15s poll preserved. Guards: `realized[win] ?? 0` (custom absent on a preset payload / in-flight); a missing custom % falls through to the existing "—" render.
- **Live proof (running service, authed, port 3000).** PID 532270→570456, NRestarts 0, `[HUB] ready`, journal clean (only the pre-existing `url.parse` DEP0169). **Custom "last 7 days" (2026-06-06..2026-06-12) == 1W preset BYTE-IDENTICAL:** realized −28.9193 / pct −43.6481% / base 66.255574 / count 158. **Custom single-day (today) == TODAY:** −2.2629 / −5.3171% / base 42.55925 / count 9. % uses A-P1's start-of-window base (no fixed-base regression, no −1XX%). **Presets byte-identical to pre-change baseline** (today −5.3171 / yest −16.777 / 1W −43.6481 / 1M −57.6692 / ALL −75.7885). New UI strings ("Custom range", validation copy) confirmed in the fresh served `.next/static` bundle.
- **Gates.** `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0; `npm run build` exit 0 (`/autotrader` 20.4→21.1 kB, +0.7 kB for the picker). `py_compile` clean. `mode=ro` replica (no writes, no `wal_checkpoint`). Sacred manifest 13 OK + the lone documented pre-existing FAIL on the BOT `/home/trevor/trevor/CLAUDE.md` (this Hub wave touched zero bot files — not attributable). 5 bot-side recurring-bug canaries (signal dedup/cooldown, HOLD card delete, orphaned reminders, reply handler, results auto-delete) trivially untouched (Hub display-only, no bot restart).
- **Files (4):** `query_auto_state.py` (+`_parse_custom_args` + `compute_custom` + main() wiring) · `src/app/api/auto/state/route.ts` (optional `?start&end`, varied cache key, +`custom?` on the 3 window interfaces) · `src/components/autotrader-v2/capital-hero.tsx` ("Custom" segment + BottomSheet picker + custom read) · `CLAUDE.md` (this entry). `tsconfig.tsbuildinfo` NOT staged. ### 2026-06-12 — WA-P1: Auto Capital realized-% uses START-OF-WINDOW equity (kills the −133% ALL%) — DEPLOYED
- **Bug (proven live).** The Auto Capital card's per-window realized **%** divided EVERY window by the *current* live account value, not the equity at that window's start — so ALL read ≈ −134% (impossible vs risked capital). The wrong denominator is in **`src/app/api/auto/state/route.ts:256-269`** (`realized[w] / realHlEquity`, `realHlEquity` = Observatory heartbeat `account_value_usd`), NOT the Python — `query_auto_state.py`'s own `realized_pct` was computed then DISCARDED by the route (EQT-A3, Hub box has no HL creds). Numerator (`compute_windows` over closed live `auto_trades`) is correct, untouched.
- **Source of truth.** `equity_snapshots.realized_equity` (de-floated realized basis, EQT-W1) in the LOCAL litestream replica `/home/ghost/trevor-replica/trevor.db` (read via the `/home/trevor/trevor/trevor.db` symlink, same file the numerator reads — no `ssh vm` at runtime). `realized_equity` is NULL for the 122 pre-EQT-W1 rows (≤2026-06-04 18:55) → COALESCE to legacy `equity`.
- **Phase 1 (DONE) — `query_auto_state.py` (additive).** Added `get_equity_at(conn, ts)` → most recent `equity_snapshots` realized-basis (`COALESCE(realized_equity, equity)`) with `ts ≤ window_start`; nearest-AFTER fallback when the window opens before the series; None when empty/None. Floor `EQUITY_BASE_FLOOR=1.0`. `main()` now emits `realized_base` per window (ALL = earliest snapshot ts) and recomputes `realized_pct = realized[w]/realized_base[w]` — **HL-independent, CLI-testable**. `mode=ro` (write blocked, proven). Worked figures (live): bases today $42.56 / 7d $66.26 / earliest $70.91; corrected % today −5.32 / yest −16.78 / 1W −43.65 / 1M −57.67 / **ALL −75.79** (was −134). Edge tests pass (before-series→fallback 70.91, after→40.08, None→None, no crash).
- **Phase 2 (DONE) — wire-up.** `src/app/api/auto/state/route.ts`: deleted the `realHlEquity`-rebase override (the bug); `realized_pct` + new `realized_base` now pass through from the Python (`...value`) unchanged; added `NullableWindows` type (pct/base are `number|null`); `equity_usd`/`equity`/`unrealized`/open numbers still mirror real HL AS-IS (EQT-A3 untouched — the live account-value line is correct). `src/components/autotrader-v2/capital-hero.tsx`: `realized_pct` widened to nullable; a null window-% renders "—" (title-tooltip), and the % now shows even when live equity is momentarily unreachable (snapshot-based, strictly better).

### 2026-06-11 — HUB-C3: Shadow tab render crash fixed — Rules-of-Hooks violation (useMemo after early return) from HUB-C2 — DEPLOYED
> Build code HUB-C3. WSL `TrevorHub` box (`ghost@Ghost`) at `/home/ghost/projects/trevor-dashboard`, SOLO on `master`, port **3000**. After HUB-C2 (`6f9df1a`) the Intel→Shadow tab threw the `src/app/error.tsx` boundary ("SYSTEM ERROR — A component failed to render"). **Pure client-side hook-ordering fix — display-only, READ-ONLY over the replica, no bot/live-DB/`auto_config`/flag/schema/`hyperliquid` touch, HUB-C2 layout NOT reverted.**
> - **ROOT CAUSE (real throw, not a guess):** `partial-shadow-card.tsx` — HUB-C2 added `const bands = React.useMemo(…)` **after** the pre-existing early return `if (loading && !data) return <Skeleton/>`. So on mount (`loading=true, data=null`) the component returned early with **4 hooks** (`useState×3`+`useEffect`); once the client `fetch` resolved (`data` set) it fell through to the `useMemo` → **5 hooks** → React 19 **#310 "Rendered more hooks than during the previous render"**, caught by the error boundary. Client-only crash (renders skeleton, then dies when data arrives) — which is why HUB-C2's status-code check (401/307) missed it and `npm run build` passed (runtime rule, not a type error). Evidence: exact file:line (early return :98, hook :108), `git diff 630e4ab 6f9df1a` showing the `useMemo` as a `+` addition, server log clean (no SSR error → client crash), payload shape verified correct (all 6 `outcome_*` keys + `by_level`/`by_ticker` present → no shape mismatch). Structural scan confirmed this was the SOLE hook-after-return across the 4 HUB-C2 components.
> - **THE FIX (1-line relocation, `partial-shadow-card.tsx`):** moved the `useMemo` **above** the early return so it runs unconditionally every render, deriving the array inside null-safe: `const bands = React.useMemo(() => bandRTriggers(data?.by_level ?? []), [data?.by_level]);`. `bands` is computed identically — only its position changed to obey the Rules of Hooks. **No layout change, HUB-C2 aggregate collapse + registry aggregation untouched.**
> - **Gates:** `npx tsc --noEmit` exit **0**; `npm run build` **"✓ Compiled successfully in 4.0s"** (`/intel` 13.4 kB / `/autotrader` 20.4 kB — unchanged, relocation only). `npm run build` THEN `sudo systemctl restart trevor-dashboard.service` → `active`, **PID 314365**, clean boot `[HUB] TREVOR Hub ready on http://127.0.0.1:3000`.
> - **DOM-VERIFIED (the check HUB-C2 missed — real browser, not a status code):** headless Chromium (Playwright, installed for verification only — not a repo dep) logged in via `/api/auth` (real creds from `.env.local`, never printed) and rendered `/intel?tab=shadow` with the client JS executing. Result: **hook-crash (#310) errors = 0**, **SYSTEM ERROR boundary NOT present**, **6/6 shadow markers rendered** (`gate-ready · promotion leaderboard · would-fire trades · active signal · no per-trade outcome · refreshes ~15 min`) — leaderboard (Live Partials READY n=1727 · 100%, Regime-Aware Exits 94.1%, …) + aggregate card lines all present. **Page renders.**
> - **Flagged (pre-existing, NOT this fix, out of scope):** one React **#418 hydration mismatch** warning fires on the shadow tab — but it **also fires on the control `/autotrader` page**, so it's the global live header clock (`16:52:58` / "REPLICA 18M AGO"), not HUB-C2/C3. It does not trigger the error boundary; the page renders fine. A separate header-hydration pass could address it.
> - **Files (2):** `src/components/autotrader-v2/partial-shadow-card.tsx` (useMemo moved above early return) · `CLAUDE.md` (this entry). `tsconfig.tsbuildinfo` + the `/tmp` verification script NOT staged. No `.py`/route/schema/flag change; bot/VM untouched; HUB-C2 intact.

### 2026-06-11 — HUB-C2: Shadow page → aggregate-only summary lines, per-entry row dumps killed (incl. the 1,881-row hero list) — DEPLOYED
> Build code HUB-C2. WSL `TrevorHub` box (`ghost@Ghost`) at `/home/ghost/projects/trevor-dashboard`, SOLO on `master`, port **3000**. Collapses EVERY shadow card to a one-line aggregate summary and removes per-entry row dumps entirely; leaderboard (HUB-C1) left exactly as-is. **READ-ONLY over the replica (`mode=ro`) — no bot/live-DB write, no `auto_config`/flag/threshold/schema change, no `hyperliquid` import, no new persisted field. The one backend touch is a pure read-only SELECT aggregation of EXISTING columns.**
> - **🛑 THE INFINITE SCROLL (root cause).** `partial-shadow-card.tsx` rendered `data.by_level` with **no slice** — and `by_level`'s bucket key is `partial_level_r`, a **near-unique float per trade** (`0.0338…`/`0.0405…`/`0.0443…` → 0.75). Live replica: **1,881 by_level rows** + a **10-row "Recent (last 10)"** true per-trade list. THAT was the endless "0.03R/0.04R" list. Both dumps are now **gone**. The 1,881 levels fold into **4 coarse R-bands** (0.00–0.25R holds 1,864 levels) via a pure client-side `bandRTriggers(0.25)` — aggregate sub-rows shown only on expand, never raw levels.
> - **PHASE-0 PREMISE CORRECTION (honesty gate — like HUB-C1/SH-HUB before it).** The prompt assumed cards carry a realized outcome to compute `mean·range·WR`. **Evidence (replica schema + fill rates):** the registry payload carried **zero** outcome fields (`outcome-like keys: NONE` — only counts + a *would-fire/divergence rate*). The realized columns (`realized_pnl_usd`/`actual_pnl_usd`) **do exist in the DB** on ~16 tables and are populated (50–88% linked), but were **not surfaced**. And the **Partial Exit hero (`partial_trigger_shadow`) has NO realized outcome column at all** (Group C). So `mean·range·WR` was un-computable from the payload. Ghost ruled **Option B at the gate: surface the existing columns read-only** (not fabricate, not stay blank).
> - **READ-ONLY REGISTRY AGGREGATION (`query_shadow_registry.py`).** `_inspect()` now also runs, for any table with a realized P&L column, a **pure SELECT**: `COUNT(col) linked, SUM(col>0) wins, AVG(col) mean, MIN/MAX(col)` over `WHERE col IS NOT NULL`. New payload fields: `outcome_col · outcome_linked_n · outcome_mean_pnl · outcome_min_pnl · outcome_max_pnl · outcome_win_rate` (all `null` for Group C). **No write keyword anywhere; no schema/persisted field added.** WR is computed over **LINKED rows only**, with linked-n surfaced, so a 50-60%-linked table reports an honest sample (e.g. Regime-Aware Exits `WR 28.3% · n=9,649 linked`), never a silent full-count WR.
> - **The honesty split (realized WR computed vs n/a):** **23 tables** got real aggregates — strongest: regime_exit/session_exit (WR 28.3%, n≈9.6k), trail_v3 (42.3%), cvd_oi/reversal_exit (40.4%), momentum_exit (11.1%), hard_stop_alt (7.4%), trail_be_stale (6.6%). **The Partial Exit hero + ~23 other ACTIVE/dormant tables = Group C → `count + "n/a — no per-trade outcome"`** (no fabricated WR/mean). `divergence_pct` is rendered as a **would-fire rate, explicitly NOT a win-rate** — the two are never conflated.
> - **Card collapse (all four surfaces):** (1) **`PartialShadowCard`** (hero) → one line `N would-fire trades · M near · K evals · would-fire Z% · n/a — no per-trade outcome`; tap → tiles + modes + **R-bands** + by-ticker (10) aggregate; **no recent-10, no raw levels**. (2) **`ShadowScoringHero`** → one line `method · rows/req · ready · last score`; tap → the 4 tiles + progress bar (pipeline meta-card → no WR, honest). (3) **`CompactShadowCard`** (dense rows) → name+badge + a wrapping `WR Y% · μ $X · $A–$B · n=N linked` line (outcome) or `n=N · would-fire Z% · no per-trade outcome` (divergence-only) or `n=N rows · n/a` (neither); expand `<dl>` gains the outcome stats, still aggregate-only. (4) **Leaderboard — untouched.**
> - **New helper `src/lib/shadow-aggregate.ts`:** pure, no-storage `aggregateShadow(entries,getPnl)→{mean,min,max,count,winRate|null}` (winRate `null` when no entry has an outcome — never fabricated), `bandRTriggers(rows,0.25)` (the 1,881→4 fold), `fmtUsd/fmtPct/fmtCount`. React `useState` for every expand — **no `localStorage`/`sessionStorage`**.
> - **Units verified (honesty):** `realized_pnl_usd` is genuinely small-dollar (−$1.57 ↔ −18.69% paired), so the `$` label is correct, not an R-multiple mislabel.
> - **Gates:** `npx tsc --noEmit` exit **0**; `npm run build` **"✓ Compiled successfully in 3.4s"** (`/intel` 13.2→13.4 kB, `/autotrader` 20.4 kB). `npm run build` THEN `sudo systemctl restart trevor-dashboard.service` → `active`, **PID 282640**, clean boot `[HUB] TREVOR Hub ready on http://127.0.0.1:3000`. **Live proof (fresh bundle, not stale `.next`):** served `.next/static` has **`Recent (last 10)` + `By R-trigger (7d)` GONE**, new `By R-trigger band` / `no per-trade outcome` / `would-fire trades` / `levels folded` **present**; `/api/shadow/registry` → **401** unauth (gate intact), `/intel?tab=shadow` → **307**→login; registry Python emits the 6 outcome fields; live fold **1,881 levels → 4 bands**. SELECT-only confirmed (no INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE/etc. in `query_shadow_registry.py`); leaderboard `promotion-leaderboard.tsx` **0-diff**.
> - **Files (6 + this entry):** `query_shadow_registry.py` (read-only outcome aggregation) · `src/app/api/shadow/registry/route.ts` (interface +6 fields) · `src/lib/shadow-aggregate.ts` (new helper) · `src/components/ui/compact-shadow-card.tsx` (summary line + outcome expand) · `src/components/autotrader-v2/partial-shadow-card.tsx` (collapse, kill 1,881-row + recent-10) · `src/components/intel/shadow-scoring-hero.tsx` (collapse) · `src/components/intel/shadow-overview.tsx` (interface + adaptCard pass-through). `tsconfig.tsbuildinfo` + untracked recon docs NOT staged.
