### 2026-06-11 — SH-HUB: Shadow page — kill the misleading +$743 would-have-PnL artifact + dynamic enumeration + divergent-n/promotion/freshness — DEPLOYED
> Build code SH-HUB. Runs on the **WSL `TrevorHub` box** (`ghost@Ghost`) at `/home/ghost/projects/trevor-dashboard`, SOLO on `master`, port **3000**. Reshapes the Intel→Shadow page (`<ShadowOverview>`). **Display-only, READ-ONLY over the WSL litestream replica (`mode=ro`); no bot/live-DB/`auto_config` write, no `hyperliquid` import, no trading-behavior change.** Data path: `query_*.py` hardcode `/home/trevor/trevor/trevor.db`, which on this box is a **symlink → `/home/ghost/trevor-replica/trevor.db`** (the replica) — so reads hit the replica with no env override needed.
> - **🛑 THE $743 FIX (the whole reason for the prompt).** `partial-shadow-card.tsx` rendered a green `MetricTile "Would-have P&L 7d"` = `would_have_profit_usd_7d`, computed in `query_partial_shadow.py` as the **SUM of `notes_json.profit_usd` across EVERY per-cycle `would_fire=1` row** in `partial_trigger_shadow`. The bot writes one row **per monitor cycle per trade**, so each trade's slice was re-counted ~40–70× → a hugely inflated "+$X profit" that was **not realizable money**. **Measured on the live replica (7d):** raw SUM ≈ $575.88 today (Ghost saw $743.48 at recon — it floats with the window/data) across **1,534 fired-rows / 37 distinct trades**; honest de-duped realizable (one partial / distinct trade) ≈ **$8.95**. **Ghost ruling at the THOUGHTS gate: REMOVE the dollar entirely, show honest COUNTS** — Option 2 (banked from `live_partial_shadow`) was proven non-viable (its `executed=1` rows carry no `profit_usd`), and even the de-duped ~$8.95 is a fragile per-cycle counterfactual on a live feature. The green tile is **gone**; replaced by **distinct-TRADE counts** (`would_fire_trades_7d/24h` via `COUNT(DISTINCT auto_trade_id)`) + near-miss + total-evals tiles, with an explicit caveat line ("Counts are distinct trades… eval-rows run ~40–70× higher… the per-cycle profit sum was a counting artifact, not realizable money"). `by_ticker` likewise drops the dollar → shows distinct would-fire trades + eval-rows. The per-row `profit_usd` in the Recent list and the now-dead `fmtMoney` helper were also removed (no green money anywhere). `would_have_profit_usd_7d` is deleted from the Python payload, the TS interface, and the route fallback.
> - **DYNAMIC ENUMERATION (`query_shadow_registry.py` rewrite — the biggest gap).** The registry was a **hardcoded 31-entry `TABLE_DEFS`**; the replica actually has **49 shadow-named tables (+3 audit adjuncts = 52)**, so **~18 SHADOW-RM roadmap tables were 100% invisible** (direction_gate, vol_adaptive_leverage, risk_budget_account_snapshot / risk_budget_alt, exit_attribution, slippage_model_vs_realized, hard_stop_level_alt, per_ticker_leverage_cap, the *_altparam sweeps, entry_threshold_regime, major/weak_ticker_threshold, scan_cadence/execution_timing, cooldown_alt_window, counter_trend_penalty). Now **enumerated from `sqlite_master`** (`name LIKE '%shadow%'` ∪ curated adjuncts), with the old `TABLE_DEFS` converted to an `OVERRIDE` **metadata map** (curated display / function / ts_col / ts_kind / expected_active / **retired**). Unknown tables auto-derive: prettified display, ordered **name-keyword function heuristic** (→ "Other" fallback), best-available **ts-col priority pick** (`created_at`>`ts`>`timestamp`>`cycle_timestamp`) with unix-vs-iso detection by column type. New 0-row tables default `expected_active=False` → classify **DORMANT, not a false BROKEN**. **New roadmap tables now appear automatically with ZERO code change here.** The 2 P06-retired tables (`leverage_v2_shadow`, `exit_engine_shadow`) re-appear marked **retired** (override `retired=True`, expected_active=False) — visible-but-honest in the Dormant tab, not active/stale. **Live: 52 tables, 45 ACTIVE / 7 DORMANT / 0 BROKEN.**
> - **Per-shadow glance fields: divergent-n · %-divergence · promotion-readiness.** Registry detects a boolean divergence/would-fire column per table (`divergent` > `would_block` > `would_fire` > `would_fire_v1`); when present, computes `divergent_n` + `divergence_pct`; else honest `null`/"n/a" (no fabricated universal signal). **Promotion-readiness** = `accruing N/30` until **n≥30 divergent samples**, then **`ready`** iff the **Wilson 95% lower bound of the divergence rate excludes the no-divergence null** (closed-form, no scipy). `<CompactShadowCard>` (the only consumer) gained optional props rendering divergent-n + %div inline + a promotion `<Pill>` (READY mint / N/30 gold) in the collapsed row and a divergence/promotion block when expanded. **Live: 15 promotion-ready.**
> - **Promotion-ready surfaced at top + freshness badge.** `<ShadowOverview>` Active tab now leads with a highlighted **🎯 Promotion-Ready** `CollapsibleSection` (ready shadows pulled out of their function group, most-divergent first; the rest stay function-grouped below), then the function blocks (now incl. an **"Other"** group for unmapped tables). A top-of-page **freshness badge** reads `os.stat(realpath(DB)).st_mtime` → "replica Xm ago · refreshes ~15 min" (atomic-mv restore stamp; live ≈19 min) + "{N} shadows · {M} promotion-ready". The replica `trevor-restore.timer` cadence (~15 min, effective ~20–30 min) means the counts can lag — the badge makes that legible at a glance.

### 2026-06-08 — W-F-P3: Hub `trevor.running` derived from the Observatory heartbeat (OFFLINE-banner bug fixed) — DEPLOYED
> Build code W-F-P3. Runs on the **WSL `TrevorHub` box** (`ghost@Ghost`) at `/home/ghost/projects/trevor-dashboard`, SOLO on `master`. Fixes the permanent OFFLINE banner on trevor-prime.com. **WSL/Hub-only — the VM bot + `trevor.service` were NOT touched; deploy restarted `trevor-dashboard.service` only.**
> - **Root cause (W-F-P2 recon, re-verified live):** `src/app/api/status/route.ts` `computeStatus()` computed `trevor.running` by running `systemctl show trevor --property=MainPID --value` **locally on the WSL box** — but the bot unit (`trevor.service`) only exists on the VM. Live proof: `systemctl status trevor.service` → *"Unit trevor.service could not be found"*; the `show` query returns MainPID `0` → `parseInt→0` → `running:false`, **always**, since the W-D migration made WSL the Hub primary. The 60s SWR cache made the false value sticky. The DB half (`signals.total` from the local replica via the Python bridge) already worked and was left alone.
> - **The fix (one file, `route.ts`):** replaced the local-systemctl block with `resolveBotRunning(serviceName)` — a `fetch` of the Observatory heartbeat the Hub **already proxies** for `account_value_usd` (`https://trevor-prime.tail068f72.ts.net:8443/api/heartbeat`, same VM endpoint + same `fetch`/`AbortSignal.timeout(5000)` idiom as `src/app/api/auto/state/route.ts` — **no new VM dependency, no new fetch mechanism**). Dropped the now-unused `runCommand` import. Response shape byte-identical (`trevor: { running, pid }`), so the 3 banner consumers (`header.tsx:84`, `status-bar.tsx:30`, `header-legacy.tsx:70/80/82/86`) needed **no edits**.
> - **Derivation rule:** `running = heartbeat HTTP 200 AND timestamp parseable AND (now − timestamp) < 3h AND the services.items entry named "trevor"(.service) has active===true`. `pid = parseInt(entry.pid) || 0` (improves the legacy PID display). The service-name match strips a trailing `.service` so env `TREVOR_SERVICE_NAME=trevor` matches the heartbeat's `"trevor.service"`. **Gated on the entry's `active` flag (mirrors the VM `systemctl is-active`), NEVER on `overall_status`** — that's a separate, independently-flapping health axis (recon saw `critical`; live now `ok`).
> - **Freshness threshold N = 3h (1.5× the 2h heartbeat cadence).** `heartbeat-view.tsx:208` `HEARTBEAT_CADENCE_SECONDS=7200` → the snapshot republishes every 2h, so its age ranges 0–7200s in normal operation. A threshold tighter than 7200s would false-OFFLINE during the back half of every window (the exact bug being killed). 3h = a full window + 1h grace before declaring the Observatory stale; bot death is still caught fast by the next publish capturing `active:false` + the VM watchdog.
> - **Fail-safe:** heartbeat unreachable / non-200 / fetch timeout / **stale (>3h)** / missing-or-inactive trevor entry → `{running:false, pid:0}` (honest OFFLINE — the Hub genuinely can't confirm the bot). The helper catches internally → never throws → can't 500 the route; the GET catch stays as belt-and-suspenders.
> - **Verification (honesty protocol):** `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0; `npm run build` exit 0 (`/api/status` 328 B). Throwaway fail-safe harness (verbatim helper logic) **7/7 PASS** — fresh+active→`{true,1717651}`, fresh+inactive→false, stale-4h+active→false, missing-entry→false, no-timestamp→false, http-500→false, **genuinely-unreachable URL→false (no crash)**; deleted after. Live-heartbeat proof (real bot up): `resolveBotRunning('trevor')` → `{running:true, pid:1717651}` — the field flips **false→true**. Served `/api/status` (authed) on local `:3000` AND public `trevor-prime.com` → `trevor.running:true` (see deploy confirmation). DB half unchanged (`signals.total` intact). Env untouched (only `TREVOR_SERVICE_NAME`+`TREVOR_DB_PATH` consumed; heartbeat URL hardcoded to match `auto/state`). 60s SWR cache kept (now caches a CORRECT value; a transient blip self-heals within ≤60–90s, takes no action — not a harmful sticky-false risk at the 2h heartbeat cadence).
> - **5-bug canary (all bot-side, none reachable by a read-only Hub status route):** (1) signal dedup/cooldown — untouched; (2) HOLD deleting trade cards — untouched; (3) orphaned reminders — untouched; (4) reply handler post-deploy — untouched; (5) results auto-delete — untouched.
> - **SEPARATE open item (NOT fixed here):** the heartbeat's `overall_status` health axis (recon saw `critical`, currently `ok`) is its own investigation — deliberately NOT wired into `running`.
> - **Files (2):** `src/app/api/status/route.ts` (−`runCommand` import, −13-line systecmtl block, +`resolveBotRunning` helper) · `CLAUDE.md` (this entry). `.env*`/`tsconfig.tsbuildinfo` NOT staged. Commit `fix: derive Hub trevor.running from Observatory heartbeat instead of impossible local systemctl check (W-F-P3)` on `master`.

### 2026-06-07 — RM-WSL W-D-P1: edge auth hardening — HMAC-signed expiring tokens + salt regen + Tailscale Serve (TrevorHub WSL box) — COMPLETE
> Build code D-P1. Runs on the **WSL `TrevorHub` box** (`ghost@Ghost`, tailnet `100.125.247.52`) at `/home/ghost/projects/trevor-dashboard`, SOLO on `master`, no worktree. Makes the WSL dashboard safely reachable as the **primary** ahead of the W-D-P2 cutover. **Hybrid auth** kept: tailnet membership (Serve) + the app login (cookie). VM untouched; VM dashboard NOT stopped (that's W-D-P2).
>
> **Token model hardened.** Replaced the legacy **reversible, unsigned** `base64url(user:pass:salt)` token — which the middleware "validated" by only checking `parts[2] === salt`, so anyone with the salt could forge a session — with an **HMAC-SHA256-signed, expiring** token: `base64url(JSON{u,iat,exp}) . base64url(HMAC_SHA256(key=SESSION_SALT, msg=payload))`, 7-day `exp`, verified server-side (recompute HMAC → constant-time compare → reject on bad sig OR past `exp`). Password is **no longer embedded** (the signature proves issuance; password is checked at login before signing).
> - **New shared module `src/lib/session-token.ts`** — the SOLE encode/decode authority (`signToken`/`verifyToken`), imported by both `middleware.ts` (Edge runtime) and `/api/auth/route.ts` (Node) so the two sites can't drift. Edge-safe: Web Crypto (`crypto.subtle`) + `Buffer` base64url only. All 6 call sites updated (3 encode in `/api/auth`: login, no-pass login, change-password; 2 decode: middleware + the GET `?token=` verifier; +1 salt read) — no old-format path remains (grep-clean).
> - **`SESSION_SALT` regenerated** off the dead 14-char default `trevor-mc-2025` → `openssl rand -hex 32` (64-char), in gitignored `.env.local` (never printed/committed). **Hardcoded fallback killed** at both `middleware.ts:4` and `route.ts:30`; `getSalt()` now **fails closed** (throws → verify rejects) if `SESSION_SALT` is unset or `<16` chars — no default, ever.
> - **One-time re-login (expected):** the salt + format change invalidated all existing 7-day cookies. Verified the fresh login round-trip issues a new signed cookie end-to-end.
> - **Exposure = Tailscale Serve** (kept the safer localhost bind; no rebind). `sudo tailscale serve --bg --https=443 http://127.0.0.1:3000` → **`https://trevorhub-wsl.tail068f72.ts.net/`** with a real Let's Encrypt cert (`issuer Let's Encrypt CN=YE2`, TLS verify ok), **tailnet-only** (NOT Funnel). Required enabling **HTTPS Certificates** + **Serve** in the tailnet admin console first (owner-gated; CertDomains was empty until then). Config persisted in tailscaled ipn state (`_serve/…`, `--bg`) + `tailscaled.service` enabled → survives reboot/distro-restart.
> - **Verification (all PASS, evidence-backed):** route verifier `GET /api/auth?token=` → valid `true`, tampered/expired/legacy-format all `false`; middleware gate on `/api/status` → tampered/expired/legacy/no-cookie all **401**, good signed cookie **200**; bad-cred login **401**; **write-gateway still gated** — forged/absent session → **401 before** the gateway (tested `/api/trades/close|flip|edit-entry`); `/api/health` exempt **200**, gateway `/healthz` **200**. Full hybrid round-trip over the TLS Serve URL: unauth `/` → `/login` (200), authed `/` → `/autotrader` (200). Dashboard bind unchanged `127.0.0.1:3000`; service rebuilt (`next build`, prod mode `next({dev:false})`) + restarted, active.
> - **Flagged follow-up (NOT fixed — pre-existing + out of scope):** `change-password` still doesn't invalidate previously-issued tokens. The Edge-runtime middleware is **credential-blind by design** (verifies only signature+expiry; can't read `.env.local`, and `process.env` is frozen until restart), so enforcing token invalidation on password change needs either a service-restart-on-change or a request-time shared store the middleware can read — an architecture change beyond this surgical auth rework. New change-password still issues a fresh valid signed token.
> - **W-D-P2 (next, separate prompt after VM-side recon):** cut the primary over to the WSL dashboard and retire the VM dashboard. Do NOT do it here.

### 2026-06-06 — RM-WSL W-C-P2a: write-gateway LIVE for the dangerous routes (Hub half) + brain sacred-guard fix
> Build code C-P2a. Runs on the **WSL `TrevorHub` box** at `/home/ghost/projects/trevor-dashboard`, SOLO on `master`, no worktree. Makes the two-hop write path real for the 18 dangerous `trevor.db` writers, makes audit mandatory (VM-side), and fixes the `/api/brain` sacred-guard `.md` bug. **The VM-side gateway (Phase 1) is NOT built here — no VM shell this session; it is deferred to a separate VM-SSH'd paste. Until it lands, every gated write fails CLOSED with 502/504 (the correct safe state — no write is possible).**
>
> **Architecture (two-hop, op-allowlist).** Hub route → local gateway (`127.0.0.1:3939`, bearer `GATEWAY_TOKEN`) → Tailscale → VM gateway (to be built) → bot helper → live `trevor.db`. The client never sends a helper path or SQL — only an **op name** from a fixed allowlist + input-validated args. Reads are untouched (still hit the local `0444` replica directly).
> - **`gateway/ops.js` (new):** zero-dep op-allowlist registry (19 ops) — `op → {flag, validate(args)}`. Hub-side validation mirrors each route's existing checks; rejects unknown ops (400) + bad args (400) before forwarding.
> - **`gateway/server.js` (was the skeleton):** added `POST /gateway/write` at the old placeholder. Bearer-auth (reused), parse + validate the op envelope `{op,args,actor,source_type,reason,idempotency_key}`, then forward over Tailscale to the VM (`VM_GATEWAY_IP:VM_GATEWAY_PORT`, bearer `VM_GATEWAY_TOKEN`). Timeout→504, conn-error/VM-5xx→502, else pass through 200/423/400/401. **Holds NO DB connection — still zero Hub-side writes.** `GET /gateway/health` now reports `gateway:"live", writes_enabled:<vm-configured>`.
> - **`src/lib/gateway-client.ts` (new):** `callGateway(op,args,opts)` / `gatewayWrite(...)` — the route side. POSTs to the local gateway with a fresh `idempotency_key`, normalizes fail modes (423 locked / 502 unreachable / 504 timeout / 400 validation), unwraps the helper `result` on 200 so client contracts stay byte-compatible.
> - **18 routes rewired** to call the gateway instead of writing the read-only replica: `capital` · `admin/reset-capital` · `admin/reset-pnl-stats` · `trades` PATCH (the inline `UPDATE active_trades SET margin_usd` SQL is **removed** — no raw SQL leaves the Hub) · `trades` DELETE (single + bulk, one op + mode arg) · `trades/edit-entry` (Discord card edit preserved off the helper result) · `trades/flip` · `trades/close` · `dca` · `auto/config-full/[key]` · `auto/control-full/[key]` · `auto/exit-controls` · `auto/partials-toggle` · `killswitch` · `memory/autotrader-toggle` · `memory/aggressive` · `quality/[id]` · `watchlist` (POST+DELETE). Each keeps its existing route-level input validation; reads/GETs untouched.
> - **3 new write-enable flags = `auto_config` DB rows** (Ghost ruling 2026-06-06 — overrides the prompt's "VM env" wording to match the established flag convention): **`HUB_CAPITAL_WRITE_ENABLED`** (capital.set/reset, pnl_stats.reset) · **`HUB_TRADE_EDIT_ENABLED`** (trades.patch_margin/edit_entry/flip/close/delete, dca.set) · **`HUB_LIST_WRITE_ENABLED`** (quality.set, watchlist.add/remove). **Default OFF = row absent or != 'true'.** Read live by the VM gateway via `SELECT value FROM auto_config WHERE key=?` (hot-flip, no restart, control-panel-visible) — exactly like `LIVE_EDIT_ENABLED`, the toggle flags, and `HUB_BRAIN_EDIT_ENABLED`. The already-gated routes KEEP their existing flags (the VM re-checks via the helper's exit-3). **`killswitch` is intentionally ungated** (emergency stop — always actionable).
> - **Audit + idempotency (VM-side, deferred):** the VM gateway will write a mandatory `change_log` row via the existing `audit_logger` (fail-CLOSED for 🔴 ops) before dispatch, and record `idempotency_key`s in a new `gateway_idempotency` table (`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS` — the ONLY permitted schema add, additive). Not created this session (no VM shell).
> - **Brain sacred-guard FIX (`/api/brain`):** the old guard compared `name.toUpperCase()` against suffix-less entries, so the real filename `IDENTITY.md` → `IDENTITY.MD` **missed** the guard and could overwrite a sacred file. Now normalized (strip `.md`, any case) → hard **423**, and the `confirm_sacred` override is **removed entirely** (sacred files inviolable, matching the robust `/api/memory/brain/[name]`). `manage_brain.py` `write_file` hardened with the same sacred check + a `HUB_BRAIN_EDIT_ENABLED` auto_config gate (exit 3 → 423), defense in depth. **No `brain/*.md` file touched** (none exist on the Hub anyway); the robust sibling + `write_brain_file.py` untouched.
> - **Env (gitignored `.env.local`, append-only, values never printed):** added `VM_GATEWAY_TOKEN` (`openssl rand -hex 32` — **the VM gateway MUST use the identical value**), `VM_GATEWAY_PORT=3940`, `VM_GATEWAY_IP=100.93.113.117` (VM **tailnet** IP — deliberately separate from `HUB_VM_IP`, which `middleware.ts` uses for the VM's *public* host).
> - **Verification (Hub half, all PASS — honesty protocol):** gateway `node --check` clean; `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0; `npm run build` exit 0. Gateway: no-token→401, unknown-op→400, bad-args→400 (validation), valid-op (VM down)→**502 `vm_gateway_unreachable` in 0.27s** (Tailscale forward genuinely fired + got refused — no hang, no non-tailnet fallback). Authenticated routes after restart: `capital`/`watchlist`/`trades`DELETE/`killswitch`/`aggressive`/`control-full` all → **502 fail-closed (NOT a 500 / readonly-DB error)**; route input-validation still 400 (bad ticker). Brain: `IDENTITY.md`→423, `identity`→423, `SOUL`→423, non-sacred+flag-off→423 (helper exit 3); helper guard fires BEFORE any write (exit 3 proven standalone). `/api/health` **200** throughout; `trevor.service` (bot, on the VM) **never touched**; `trevor-gateway` + `trevor-dashboard` restarted clean (gate-approved).
> - **DEFERRED to the VM-SSH'd paste (Phase 1 + VM half of Phase 5):** build `gateway/vm_gateway.js` (Node, tailnet-only, bearer) + the op→helper map + server-side flag check (auto_config) + mandatory `change_log` audit + `gateway_idempotency` table + `trevor-vm-gateway.service`; create the 3 new `auto_config` flag rows (default OFF); then the **423-from-VM** proof + one benign **ON** round-trip (watchlist add/remove → 200 + `change_log` + idempotency rows). Also: the Tailscale ACL lock (if not yet authored) and the 4 benign C-P2b writers + 2 GET-path budget writers.
> - **Files:** `gateway/ops.js` (new) · `gateway/server.js` · `src/lib/gateway-client.ts` (new) · 17 route files (18 ops) · `src/app/api/brain/route.ts` · `manage_brain.py` · `CLAUDE.md` (this entry). `.env.local` modified (append-only) but **NOT staged**.

### 2026-06-06 — W-C-P2-DELIVERY: report-delivery pipeline ported to the WSL hub (#downloads via env webhook + DOCS surfacing)
> Build code C-P2-DELIVERY. Runs on the **WSL `TrevorHub` box** at `/home/ghost/projects/trevor-dashboard`, SOLO on `master`, no worktree. Mirrors the VM-side delivery pipeline so recon/audit reports generated on WSL post to Discord **#downloads** AND surface in the Hub DOCS zone. **WSL-local only — no VM touch, no bot touch, no trevor.db write.**
>
> - **Webhook is env-only (hard separation).** The hub uses its OWN webhook read from `.env.local` as `HUB_DOWNLOADS_WEBHOOK_URL` — never hardcoded, never printed, no fallback to the bot webhook/`DISCORD_BOT_TOKEN`. Missing var → `post_file_sync()` raises loudly (verified). `#downloads` channel id `1492922559019225261`.
> - **Delivery script (new):** `scripts/discord_file_delivery.py` — `post_file_sync(filepath, title=None, description=None, category=None)`. Reads the webhook from `.env.local` (manual parse, fail-loud), POSTs the file multipart with `?wait=true` to capture the Discord message id, then registers it via the **real `download_manager.save_download()`** (exact schema, additive). Handles file-not-found, webhook 4xx/5xx (surfaces status, no crash), and timeout (30s). No new deps — `requests` already present; `loguru`/`tz_utils` absent on WSL (stubbed/fallback, see below).
> - **`download_manager.py` ported from the VM into the repo root.** Two surgical, schema-preserving changes only: (1) `loguru` import wrapped in a try/except stdlib-logging fallback (loguru not installed on WSL; mirrors the existing `tz_utils` fallback); (2) the VM-hardcoded `DOWNLOADS_DIR` (`/home/trevor/trevor/downloads`) + delete-log path repointed to **repo-relative, env-overridable** (`HUB_DOWNLOADS_DIR` / `HUB_DOWNLOADS_LOG`, default `<repo>/downloads` and `<repo>/logs/...`). The manifest/categories schema + all read-modify-write logic are **byte-identical** to the VM. ⚠️ The bot-delete path (`_delete_discord_message`) still loads `.env` (absent on WSL) → its Discord cleanup fails-open; not in scope (delivery doesn't use it; DOCS delete relies on a `DISCORD_BOT_TOKEN` that is a placeholder here).
> - **DOCS read path repointed.** `query_downloads.py` + `query_docs_categories.py` previously hardcoded `sys.path.insert(0, "/home/trevor/trevor")` (where only the 0444 replica lives) → the DOCS zone listed nothing on WSL (ImportError → REL-04 fail-safe). Both now insert the script's own dir (the repo root, where `download_manager.py` was ported) + add `import os`. The DOCS downloads listing works on WSL again.
> - **Downloads tree:** created `downloads/{active,archive}/` at the dashboard-read path with seeded empty `manifest.json`/`categories.json` (via the module's own functions, so schema matches). `.gitignore` extended: commit the `.gitkeep` scaffolding, **ignore runtime data** (delivered files + the mutated `manifest.json`/`categories.json` — `download_manager` recreates empty ones on demand; avoids git churn + keeps delivered data/secrets out of git).
> - **End-to-end verified (honesty protocol).** Test post of the real recon report `/home/ghost/W-C-P2-recon.md` → **webhook HTTP 200** (message id `1512951982543999038`); manifest went `[]` → 1 entry (additive); authed `GET /api/intel/downloads` lists `2026-06-06_w-c-p2-recon.md` (`active_count:1`); read path `/api/health` stayed **200** throughout. Ghost visually confirms the file in #downloads. Phase-1 gates: import clean under system python (loguru stub exercised), missing-webhook path raises a clear `RuntimeError`, no `/home/trevor/trevor` functional path remains. (Known pre-existing quirk, not introduced: the TS route returns `discord_msg_id` as a JS number → precision loss past 2^53; `manifest.json` holds the correct int.)
> - **Files:** `scripts/discord_file_delivery.py` (new) · `download_manager.py` (ported, +loguru fallback + path repoint) · `query_downloads.py` + `query_docs_categories.py` (sys.path repoint + `import os`) · `.gitignore` (downloads runtime rules) · `downloads/active/.gitkeep` + `downloads/archive/.gitkeep` (scaffolding) · `CLAUDE.md` (this entry). `.env.local` NOT committed; no webhook URL in the diff.
> - **Preference (2026-06-06):** WSL hub report delivery = `scripts/discord_file_delivery.py` `post_file_sync()` → #downloads via **`HUB_DOWNLOADS_WEBHOOK_URL` (env-only, fail-loud, no fallback)** + `download_manager.save_download()` for the manifest. `download_manager.py` now lives in the **dashboard repo root** (repo-relative `downloads/` tree, env-overridable via `HUB_DOWNLOADS_DIR`); `query_downloads.py`/`query_docs_categories.py` import it from the repo, NOT `/home/trevor/trevor`. Downloads runtime data (delivered files + manifest/categories) is gitignored; only the `.gitkeep` scaffolding is committed.

### 2026-06-06 — RM-WSL W-C-P1: Tailscale mesh client + write-gateway skeleton (TrevorHub WSL box)
> Build codes C-P1-MESH, C-P1-GW-SKEL. Runs on the **WSL `TrevorHub` box** at `/home/ghost/projects/trevor-dashboard` (NOT the VM). Builds the TrevorHub-side mesh client + a local gateway auth skeleton so W-C-P2 can route writes to the live `trevor.db` on the VM. **This prompt wires NO write routes, makes NO DB connection, and never touches the VM.** trevor-prime (the VM) is already a tailnet peer at `100.93.113.117` (installed manually by Ghost — untouched here).
>
> **WSL environment (Phase 0 audit, 2026-06-06):** WSL2 Ubuntu 24.04, distro `TrevorHub`, user `ghost`, **systemd ON**. NAT mode (`eth0 172.27.110.9/20`, gw `172.27.96.1`); Windows host owns its own separate Tailscale. **This distro runs its OWN tailscaled.** `/dev/net/tun` present → **kernel networking mode** (no userspace-networking needed). Dashboard here runs on **:3000** (`.env.local` `PORT=3000` overrides server.js' 3333 default), bound `127.0.0.1`, started detached via `node server.js` (no systemd unit on this box yet — it dies on session exit; restarted this session). Replica `/home/ghost/trevor-replica/trevor.db` is `0444` read-only. **Public health endpoint is `/api/health` (200, unauth);** `/api/status` is auth-gated (401 without `trevor_session` cookie) — use `/api/health` for read-path health checks, not `/api/status`.
>
> - **Phase 1 (DONE):** Tailscale **1.98.4** installed via official script; `tailscaled` runs under **systemd** (`systemctl enable --now tailscaled`, active, pid-managed, survives reboot) in **kernel mode** — `tailscale0` TUN interface created (NOT `--tun=userspace-networking`). Userspace-networking remains the documented fallback if the mesh ever fails. No `tailscale up` in Phase 1.
> - **Phase 2 (DONE):** `tailscale up --hostname=trevorhub-wsl`, auth URL approved by Ghost in browser. This box is now a tailnet peer — **TrevorHub tailnet IP = `100.125.247.52`** (`trevorhub-wsl`). `tailscale status` lists both `trevorhub-wsl` and `trevor-prime` (`100.93.113.117`). **Mesh reachability CONFIRMED:** `tailscale ping 100.93.113.117` → pong (≈67ms **via DERP relay**; "direct connection not established" — NAT/WSL routes over the DERP relay, functional, slightly higher latency than a direct WireGuard path; direct may negotiate later). `curl :80` on the VM → 000 (closed, **expected** — the VM gateway is C-P2's job; this only proves L3 routing). **No DB connection, no write, no auth attempted against the VM.**
> - **Phase 3 (DONE):** Write-gateway **skeleton** at **`gateway/server.js`** — a **standalone Node `http` process** (zero deps), bound `127.0.0.1:`**`3939`** (`GATEWAY_PORT`-configurable), deliberately separate from the dashboard process so it can't disturb :3000. **Machine-to-machine bearer auth** (`GATEWAY_TOKEN`, fresh `openssl rand -hex 32`, in gitignored `.env.local`, never printed/committed; constant-time compare) — SEPARATE from the dashboard's `trevor_session` cookie auth. Routes: `GET /gateway/health` → `{"status":"ok","gateway":"skeleton","writes_enabled":false}` (200 w/ valid bearer), 401 without/with bad token, 404 else; every path wrapped so a handler can't crash the process. **ZERO DB connection / ZERO write logic** — only a marked `// W-C-P2 WILL ADD WRITE ROUTES HERE` block. Started detached (`node gateway/server.js`); not yet a systemd unit (the production gateway runs ON the VM in C-P2). **Verification gate (all PASS):** no-token→401, bad-token→401, valid-token→200+`writes_enabled:false`, unknown-path→404, **dashboard `/api/health` still 200** (skeleton didn't disrupt reads). `.env.local` confirmed gitignored (`.gitignore:10`).
> - **Phase 5 / durability (DONE, 2026-06-06):** closed the gap that left the read path down at Phase 0 (services were session-children that died on session exit). Two **system** systemd units now own both processes on this WSL box (`User=ghost`, `Restart=on-failure`, `EnvironmentFile=/home/ghost/projects/trevor-dashboard/.env.local`, `WantedBy=multi-user.target` → **survive WSL reboot**): **`trevor-dashboard.service`** (`/usr/bin/node server.js`; `PORT=3000` supplied by the EnvironmentFile) and **`trevor-gateway.service`** (`/usr/bin/node gateway/server.js`). Both `enabled` + `active`; post-`daemon-reload` health re-verified: dashboard `/api/health` → 200, gateway `/gateway/health` → 200 (auth) / 401 (no auth). Unit files are version-controlled at **`deploy/systemd/wsl/`** (kept separate from the VM's `User=trevor` units in `deploy/systemd/`; the installed `/etc/systemd/system/` copies are byte-identical). **NOTE for future sessions:** the dashboard + gateway are now systemd-managed — `sudo systemctl {status,restart} trevor-dashboard trevor-gateway`, NOT detached `node` — don't hand-start them.
> - **Status / what's NOT done (left for C-P2):** NO write routes wired, NO `trevor.db` connection from the gateway, NO VM-side gateway, NO ACL lock yet. Transport is **Tailscale-only, ACL-locked (LOCKED by Ghost) — NO nginx + token fallback.** Read path stays on the local `0444` replica; writes still fail-safe until C-P2.

### 2026-06-05 — PKF-01: peak-% display guard — peak can never render below live% (ACTIVE position card)
- **Display-only guard, no money path, no data logic, no bot touch.** Single file: `src/components/autotrader-v2/active-position-card.tsx`. The "peak" chip on each open-position row read the stored `peak_pnl_pct` raw, which can show BELOW the live unrealized % — impossible for a high-water mark. **Live-proven bug (PKF-02, deferred):** the bot's `peak_pnl_pct` ratchet stalls because a live trade stuck in the `PARTIAL_EXIT` branch never reaches the HOLD persist path (`monitor.py:3419-3429`) — confirmed on open trade #100335 HYPE SHORT: stored `peak_pnl_pct=1.0763` / `trough_price=66.935` frozen while the price ran to 65.503 (real pnl ≈ +7.47%), with a `[PARTIAL-LIVE-NOOP] outcome=error` storm (40×/20min) keeping it in the partial branch every cycle. **Root cause is the DFF-W1-sibling partial-storm exit bug; PKF-02 (bot-side ratchet fix) is DEFERRED to that prompt** — not patched inside the exit engine tonight.
- **The change (1 memo + 1 render line):** the `enriched` `useMemo` now derives `peak_display = storedPeak === null ? null : (typeof pnl_pct === "number" ? Math.max(storedPeak, pnl_pct) : storedPeak)` (live% is the existing `pnl_pct = price-move% × leverage`, already computed there), and the peak chip renders `p.peak_display` instead of `p.peak_pnl_pct`. So the chip is `max(stored peak, live%)` and can NEVER render below live; when live price is unavailable (`pnl_pct` null) it falls back to the stored peak (byte-identical to before). The show/hide condition is unchanged in shape (`typeof === "number" && !== 0`), just keyed off `peak_display`.
- **No fetch/calc/API/DB change** — same `/api/auto/trades?type=open` (`positions[]`) + `/api/prices` responses; the helper `query_auto_trades.py` already SELECTs `peak_pnl_pct`/`leverage`/`entry_price`/`direction`. `peak_price`/`trough_price` are NOT exposed to the Hub and are NOT needed — the live% recompute + stored peak suffice.
- **Verified:** `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0; `npm run build` exit 0 (`/autotrader` 20.5 kB, no drift). Authed live API confirmed open #100335 returns `peak_pnl_pct=1.0763`+`leverage=3`+`entry_price=67.176`. Guard demonstrated against live values — price 65.503 → live +7.47% → `peak_display=max(1.08, 7.47)=+7.47%` (≥ live ✓, was rendering the impossible +1.08%); price 66.935 → +1.08% ✓; underwater 67.50 → live −1.45% → peak_display +1.08% (≥ live ✓). `trevor-dashboard.service` restarted clean (`[HUB] ready`, 0 boot errors); **`trevor.service` NOT touched** (same PID 2573006 throughout).
- **Regression posture:** Hub display-only pixels — the 5 bot-side recurring-bug canaries (signal dedup/cooldown, HOLD card delete, orphaned reminders, reply handler, results auto-delete) trivially untouched. No DB/schema/`auto_config`/flag, no design tokens, no new deps, no Python, sacred files untouched. Solo on `master`, no worktree.
- **Files (2):** `src/components/autotrader-v2/active-position-card.tsx` · `CLAUDE.md` (this entry). `tsconfig.tsbuildinfo` NOT staged.
- **Preference (2026-06-05):** the ACTIVE position card's **peak% is a display-guarded high-water mark — `max(stored peak_pnl_pct, live unrealized %)`** — it can never render below the live %. This is belt-and-suspenders over the bot-side `peak_pnl_pct` ratchet, which can stall while a live trade is stuck in the `PARTIAL_EXIT` branch (the bot-side fix, PKF-02, is deferred to the partial-storm/DFF-W1-sibling prompt). The **monotonic locked-profit/exit ratchet floor (`ratchet_locked_r`/`ratchet_stop`, `monitor.py:3518-3535`) is SEPARATE and was NOT touched** — this is the display/tracking peak only.

### 2026-06-04 — EQF-W1: de-double-count the equity headline source (Hub half)
- **Hub half of the cross-repo EQF-W1 money-path fix** (bot `main`: `live_executor.py:~2721` `account_value = perps_value + spot_usdc` → `spot_usdc + Σ unrealized`). The equity formula DOUBLE-COUNTED the held isolated margin: live raw HL proved perps `marginSummary.accountValue` == spot USDC **`hold`** == `totalMarginUsed` == Σ isolated `marginUsed` (isolated margin is HELD INSIDE spot USDC `total`, not separate collateral). `perps_AV + spot_total` counted it twice (~$106–116 inflated vs true honest cash ~$69). **Read-only over HL, no money path on the Hub side, no `.tsx` touched.**
- **The change (1 Python helper):** `query_auto_state.py:122` `_fetch_hl()` `equity = perps_value + spot_usdc` → **`equity = spot_usdc + unrealized`** (true MTM equity; `unrealized` was already computed there). Dropped the now-dead `perps_value`/`margin` reads. Docstrings (module + `_fetch_hl`) updated to the EQF-01 semantics.
- **`capital-hero.tsx` NOT touched** — its EQT-D1 de-float `realizedEquity = equity − unrealized` already recovers honest cash once `equity` is true MTM. Verified: `equity_usd` $72.95 (MTM, was the double-counted ~$106) → de-float headline **$68.68** honest cash. So the "$82 = account value" inflation is fixed at the source the headline reads.
- **`query_leverage_regime.py:310` (margin-util denominator, BRK-R3) NOT touched** — it carries the same `perps+spot` double-count in `util = totalMarginUsed / account_value` (understates util%, ~35% shown vs ~50% real) but is **display-only** and does NOT feed the breaker or sizing → flagged as a clean follow-up, out of EQF-W1 scope.
- **`query_auto_state.py` is a hot helper** (spawned fresh per `/api/auto/state` request via `runPython`) — the corrected equity is **live on the next request; NO `npm run build`, NO dashboard restart needed** (no `.ts`/`.tsx` changed). `npx tsc --noEmit` / `npm run build` therefore unaffected. Verified the corrected JSON via a fresh helper spawn (`equity_usd` 72.95, `unrealized_usd` 4.28, de-float 68.68).
- **Regression posture:** Hub read-only Python-helper change, no bot restart from the Hub side → the 5 bot-side recurring-bug canaries trivially untouched. No `auto_config`/flag flips, no DB writes, no design tokens, no new deps, sacred files untouched. Solo on `master`, no worktree. **Paired bot wave on `main` ships the canonical source + the snapshot-writer inheritance.**
- **Files (2):** `query_auto_state.py` · `CLAUDE.md` (this entry). `tsconfig.tsbuildinfo` NOT staged.
- **Preference (2026-06-04):** the Hub `equity_usd` is **true MTM equity = spot USDC `total` + Σ unrealized PnL**, NOT `perps accountValue + spot USDC` (which double-counted the spot-USDC-held isolated margin). The capital headline de-floats it (− unrealized) to honest cash = spot total. Never reintroduce `perps_value + spot_usdc` as the equity.

### 2026-06-04 — W-A-P2: Kill EXIT HINT raw-JSON blob → parsed glance line (ACTIVE position card)
- **Display-only parse, no money path, no data logic.** Single file: `src/components/autotrader-v2/active-position-card.tsx`. The ACTIVE position card was dumping `p.exit_signals_log.slice(0, 80)` — a truncated raw JSON string (`[{"ts":"…","event":"TRADE_OPENED","entry":67.18,"stop":71.62,"regime":"RANGING"},…]`) that bled off the right edge. Replaced with a clean two-line glance. **No fetch/calc/API/DB change** — same `/api/auto/trades?type=open` response, parsed in-component for display.
- **Real data shape (confirmed live, not assumed):** `exit_signals_log` is a raw JSON **string** column on `auto_trades` (via `query_auto_trades.py`), an **array of event objects** with **lowercase** keys `ts` · `event` · `entry` · `stop` · `regime` — **no `target` key in the log.** Entry/stop/target levels are sourced instead from the **top-level parsed fields** `p.entry_price` / `p.stop_price` / `p.target_price` (separate `SELECT` columns, always present, real non-zero values incl. target). The log is parsed ONLY for the event + time.
- **The change (2 render lines + 2 helpers):** removed the `exit hint:` + `.slice(0, 80)` blob (old lines 243–250). Added (after `fmtPrice`): `latestExitEvent(raw)` — safe `JSON.parse` in try/catch, returns the **last (most-recent) event** object, or `null` on any failure (null/empty-string/malformed/empty-array/non-array/non-object); `fmtEventTime(ts)` — short ET time-of-day (`"4:59 PM ET"`) via `toLocaleTimeString` + `timeZone: "America/New_York"` (matches `dca-section.tsx`'s `ET_TZ` convention), reusing the card's existing `parseUTC`. Glance render (two tight lines at `text-caption text-fg-muted`, matching the sibling meta row — off `fg-faint`): line 1 `entry $X · stop $Y · tgt $Z` (stop/tgt truthy-guarded so a missing/0 level is omitted, never `$NaN`); line 2 `{event} · {ET time}` (omitted entirely when the log is null/empty/malformed — levels line still renders).
- **Verified (honesty protocol):** `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0; `npm run build` exit 0 (`✓ Compiled successfully`, `/autotrader` 20.5 kB). `grep "EXIT HINT|exit hint|slice(0, 80)"` → **no matches** (raw blob gone); `exit_signals_log` now only reaches `latestExitEvent(p.exit_signals_log)` — no raw render. Parse + degradation traced against the **two live positions** (HYPE populated → `TRADE_RESUMED · 4:59 PM ET`; NEAR `exit_signals_log: null` → event line omitted, levels line shows) plus malformed/empty-array/non-array/empty-string — all degrade to "event line omitted, no raw string / `—` / `undefined` / `$NaN`".
- **G8 (no other raw-JSON on Dashboard tab):** scanned all 6 Dashboard-tab components (scalper-header, capital-hero, active-position-card, profit-risk-panel, leverage-regime-panel, watchlist-grid) for `.slice(0,` / `stringify` / `_log` / `_json` / `JSON.parse` rendering to screen. **EXIT HINT was the ONLY raw-JSON-to-screen offender.** The `leverage-regime-panel:30` hit is a code comment (`hmm_inference_log` table name); the 7 `JSON.stringify` hits across the zone are all `body: JSON.stringify(...)` **fetch-request bodies** in Config/Control-tab components (not Dashboard tab, not renders). No deferred follow-ups.
- **Regression posture:** Hub display-only, frontend pixels — the 5 bot-side recurring-bug canaries (signal dedup/cooldown, HOLD card delete, orphaned reminders, reply handler, results auto-delete) trivially untouched. No DB/schema/`auto_config`/flag, no design-token change, no color-meaning change, no new deps, no Python, sacred files untouched. Solo on `master`, no worktree.
- **Files (2):** `src/components/autotrader-v2/active-position-card.tsx` · `CLAUDE.md` (this entry). `tsconfig.tsbuildinfo` NOT staged. Commit `fix: parse EXIT HINT json to glance line, kill raw blob [W-A-P2]`.
- **Preference (2026-06-04):** **no raw JSON / code blobs ever render to screen on the Hub dashboard** — parse to a clean glance line. The ACTIVE position card's `exit_signals_log` renders as two tight `text-caption text-fg-muted` lines: **trade levels** `entry $X · stop $Y · tgt $Z` (levels from the top-level `entry_price`/`stop_price`/`target_price` fields, NOT the log — the log has no `target`) + **most-recent event · ET time** (`TRADE_RESUMED · 4:59 PM ET`). Parse via `latestExitEvent` (safe, null on any failure); empty/malformed → omit the event line, never show the raw string. Don't reintroduce `.slice()` of a JSON string as a render.

### 2026-06-03 — RHR-04: short-TTL in-process flag cache — drops the `query_feature_flags.py` spawn off first byte (RM-HUB-R Wave C — SOLO on master)
- **The TTFB lever PERF-07 deferred, now built.** PERF-07 closed no-code but surfaced the real first-byte lever: the sole SSR first-byte blocker is the root-layout `AppShell` `await getAllFlagsCached()` → `query_feature_flags.py` spawn, and React `cache()` only dedupes WITHIN a render pass — so EVERY SSR request re-spawned Python. This wave adds the **cross-request** cache PERF-07 named (a PERF-03-style in-process cache, NOT Suspense). No money path; Hub-only; read-only on flags.
- **The change (1 file, ~30 lines net):** `src/lib/feature-flags-server.ts` — wrapped the flag read in a module-level **`createSwrCache`** (the established RM-DASH single-flight helper, reused not reinvented) at **45s TTL, `staleWhileRevalidate:false`, concurrency 1, single key `"flags"`**. Split the read into `readAllFlags()` (THROWS on failure — never caches a failure) + the `getAllFlagsCached` outer wrapper that keeps the fail-safe `{}` (cold error → `{}`, byte-identical to before; warm error → serve last-good stale). React `cache()` (intra-render dedup, PERF-03) is retained UNDER the new module cache (cross-request dedup). Single-flight collapses a burst of concurrent cold/expired renders to ONE spawn. **No flag exists/default/query/downstream changed — the read only is cached.**
- **Measured baseline (authed live `time_starttransfer`, minted local-salt session cookie to reach the real render):** `query_feature_flags.py` CLI spawn 0.06–0.08s; per-request SSR TTFB **~0.11–0.15s typical (0.09–0.31s)**, the spawn firing on every request.
- **TTFB win proven on the real path:** cold first hit `/autotrader` **0.31s** (spawn) → warm hits **0.04–0.08s** (render floor, spawn off first byte). The cache is **shared across all routes** (one `"flags"` key) — the first render warms it for every page (`/stocks`/`/intel` were already warm at 0.04–0.13s). Net: ~1 spawn per 45s window, all other requests instant.
- **Flag-values-unchanged proof:** cached `/api/feature-flags` output `diff`'d **byte-identical** to the raw `query_feature_flags.py` spawn (10 flags, sorted/normalized). **TTL-refresh proof:** warm 0.07s → idle 47s (>45s TTL) → first post-expiry hit **0.19s** (re-spawn — entry expired, re-read live source, not stale-forever) → re-warmed 0.06s; values still identical to live DB after refresh. **Read-only confirmed:** `query_feature_flags.py:22` opens `mode=ro`, `SELECT` only — no INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE/`wal_checkpoint`; the cache path never writes `auto_config`/`trevor.db`.
- **Build/restart/monitor:** `npm run build` exit 0; `sudo systemctl restart trevor-dashboard.service` → active, `[HUB] TREVOR Hub ready`. Bounded event-driven journal monitor armed across the test window → **zero** flag-read/stale/render errors; stopped before completion.
- **Regression posture:** Hub-only flag-read cache → all 5 bot-side recurring-bug canaries (signal dedup/cooldown, HOLD card delete, orphaned reminders, reply handler, results auto-delete) trivially untouched. No DB schema/TLS/bot-repo touch; `trevor.service` untouched; no new npm deps; no sacred-file touches.
- **Files (2):** `src/lib/feature-flags-server.ts` (the cache) · `CLAUDE.md` (this entry). `tsconfig.tsbuildinfo` NOT staged. Commit `perf: short-TTL in-process flag cache to drop spawn off first byte (RHR-04)` on `master`.
- **Preference (2026-06-03):** `getAllFlagsCached` (`feature-flags-server.ts`) now has a **module-level `createSwrCache` 45s cross-request layer** (TTL `45_000`, `staleWhileRevalidate:false` await-on-expiry, conc 1, single key) ON TOP OF PERF-03's React `cache()` intra-render dedup — the `query_feature_flags.py` spawn is off first byte on cache hits (TTFB ~0.13s → ~0.05s warm). The fail-safe `{}` lives in the OUTER wrapper so a transient spawn failure is never cached as all-flags-off. **45s** is short enough that a CC-toggled flag (flags change only via prompts editing `auto_config`, never live UI) is picked up within 45s — a stale-forever flag cache is a bug, not a feature. The first request after expiry re-spawns and immediately reflects current values (no N+1 lag; mirrors `/api/heartbeat` swr:false).

### 2026-06-03 — REL-12: watchdog detection tighten `CHECK_INTERVAL` 30→15 — PSI-GATED, GATE PASSED, APPLIED (RHR-01, RM-HUB-R Wave B — SOLO on master)
- **PSI-gated tighten; gate PASSED on a genuinely healthy box (not manufactured).** REL-12 was deferred at original execution because tightening detection into an unhealthy baseline causes false restarts. Stricter 8GB/2vCPU bar applied; 5.57 cleared it comfortably. Holistic health all green: event-loop **p99 11.53ms / max_ms 142.87** (no latched wedge), **no OOM** (`journalctl -k` clean), CPU **steal ≈0**, swap statically full but **not thrashing** (`vmstat so≈0`).
- **The single approved change — `dashboard_health_watchdog.sh:62` `CHECK_INTERVAL` `30`→`15`.** Wedge-detection reaction drops **~75s → ~45s** (`FAIL_THRESHOLD × CHECK_INTERVAL + MAX_TIME` = 2×30+15 → 2×15+15). Nothing else in the watchdog touched.
- **Loop protection + layering intact (verified in the diff):** `FAIL_THRESHOLD=2` kept (transient-blip filter — a single starved poll is still ignored; only 2 *consecutive* misses fire), `MAX_RESTARTS_IN_WINDOW=3 / RESTART_WINDOW_SEC=1800` windowed give-up untouched, `MAX_TIME=15` untouched. **Layering preserved/strengthened:** ~45s REL-06-primary reaction sits cleanly below the 60s INFRA-02 systemd `WatchdogSec` backstop → primary still fires first (subordinate ordering held). **CPUWeight (REL-01) NOT touched.**
- **Deployed + verified live:** edited live `/home/trevor/scripts/dashboard_health_watchdog.sh` + synced byte-identical repo copy `deploy/scripts/...`; `bash -n` clean; `sudo systemctl restart trevor-dashboard-watchdog.service` → active, new boot line `watchdog start — … interval=15s max_time=15s fail_threshold=2 … window=1800s max_in_window=3`. Bounded event-driven monitor over the new-cadence baseline → **zero false restarts** (Hub `NRestarts` stayed 0, no `RESTARTING`/give-up events). The Hub service (`trevor-dashboard.service`) was NOT restarted — only the watchdog's own service reloaded. No DB/TLS/bot touch.
- **Preference (2026-06-03):** the REL-06 health watchdog polls `/api/health` every **`CHECK_INTERVAL=15s`** (REL-12 tighten from 30s; wedge-detection reaction ~45s). `FAIL_THRESHOLD=2` and the windowed give-up (`MAX_RESTARTS_IN_WINDOW=3 / RESTART_WINDOW_SEC=1800` → alert-only + loud `#qa-agent` page) are unchanged. The ~45s primary reaction stays deliberately **below** the 60s systemd `WatchdogSec` subordinate backstop — REL-06-primary → INFRA-02-subordinate ordering is preserved. REL-12 is now **active** (no longer deferred). A future tighten of `FAIL_THRESHOLD`→1 or `MAX_TIME` is NOT sanctioned (removes the transient-blip filter / risks false-fail on a slow-but-recovering loop).

### 2026-06-03 — RHR-02: boot-grace window for the `/api/health` LAG max_ms latch (RM-HUB-R Wave A — SOLO on master)
- **Cosmetic health-body signal fix, no money path, no control-path change.** `src/app/api/health/route.ts` only. The REL-14 `max_ms` latch (`max_ms > LAG_DEGRADED_MAX_MS=1000` → body `status:"degraded"`) was tripped by the routine Next.js cold-start spike and, because `max_ms` is cumulative since process start, stayed latched `degraded` for the entire process life despite a healthy loop. **Zero restart impact** — the REL-06 watchdog reads the HTTP code only (`dashboard_health_watchdog.sh:157/167` — `%{http_code}`, 200/degraded treated as healthy), never the body field; confirmed live in Phase 0.
- **Phase 0 evidence (proven live):** pre-fix body `status:"degraded"` at uptime **7913s (~2.2h)** driven by `max_ms 1677.72 > 1000` while `p99_ms` was a healthy **11.83ms** — textbook boot-spike latch poisoning.
- **Fix — uptime-30s boot-grace + one-time histogram reset (latch logic only).** `LAG_BOOT_GRACE_SEC=30`: while `process.uptime() < 30s` the `max_ms` latch term is suppressed (`!inBootGrace && max_ms > LAG_DEGRADED_MAX_MS`); at the first request past the window the shared perf_hooks histogram is `reset()` **once** (reached via the same `globalThis.__trevorEloopDelay` key `event-loop-lag.ts` stashes it under, optional-chained), clearing the cumulative boot-spike high-water mark so the post-grace latch evaluates only post-warmup lag. **A bare uptime gate alone would NOT fix it** — the stale boot-spike `max_ms` persists in the cumulative histogram and re-trips the latch the instant the window closes; the one-time reset is what actually removes the boot spike. **~16 lines added; `LAG_DEGRADED_MAX_MS`, the p99 latch, the watchdog, the HTTP status code, and `restart_count` are all untouched.**
- **Latch still works post-warmup:** the comparison `eventLoop.max_ms > LAG_DEGRADED_MAX_MS` is byte-identical to before, gated only by `!inBootGrace` (true post-grace) on a now-clean (post-reset) histogram — a genuine post-warmup wedge >1000ms still flips `degraded` exactly as before; only the cold-start spike is suppressed.
- **Verified live:** `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0; `npm run build` exit 0. `sudo systemctl restart trevor-dashboard.service` → `[HUB] ready`, `restart_count:0`, 0 journal errors. Health body **before** restart = `degraded` (max_ms 1677, p99 11.83); **after** restart + warmup window = `status:"healthy"` (max_ms **28.9** post-reset, p99 12.55) — boot spike no longer latched. `curl -o /dev/null -w %{http_code}` = **200** (watchdog dependency intact). Bounded event-driven journal monitor armed across the restart — only `ready` + `watchdog armed`, zero errors.
- **Regression posture:** Hub health-route only, no bot restart → all 5 recurring-bug canaries (signal dedup/cooldown, HOLD card delete, orphaned reminders, reply handler, results auto-delete) are bot-side → trivially untouched. No `auto_config`/flag flips, no DB writes (route is read-only, confirmed — no DB access at all post-REL-09), no TLS/allowlist, no design tokens, no new npm deps, no sacred-file touches, `trevor.service` not restarted.
- **Files (2):** `src/app/api/health/route.ts` (boot-grace window + one-time reset) · `CLAUDE.md` (this entry). `tsconfig.tsbuildinfo` NOT staged. Commit `fix: boot-grace window for /api/health LAG latch (RHR-02)` on `master`. Per the Wave-A split, RHR-02 does NOT touch `## Preferences` (RHR-05 owns CLAUDE.md prefs this wave).

### 2026-06-03 — FIX-CSS: restore `/_next/static/` CSS/JS delivery — **filesystem perm, not nginx config** (SOLO on `master`)
- **Symptom:** trevor-prime.com rendered as raw unstyled HTML — SSR content + API data loaded, but every `/_next/static/` asset (CSS, JS, fonts) failed. **Root cause: filesystem EACCES, NOT an nginx-config break.** PERF-01 (2026-06-02) switched `/_next/static/` from Node-proxy (Node runs as `trevor`) to nginx **disk-`alias`** serving — but nginx workers run as **`www-data`**, and `/home/trevor` was mode **`0750`** (`drwxr-x---`), excluding `www-data` from traversal. The correct alias path was unreachable. Proof: nginx error.log from Ghost's IP (172.56.101.240, 18:52:10) `[crit] stat() ".../static/css/2cbfd02837804931.css" failed (13: Permission denied)` on CSS+JS+woff2 identically; `sudo -u www-data test -r` confirmed CANNOT read. Node direct (:3333) served the same chunk **200 + `text/css`**; live nginx conf was **byte-identical to repo** (`diff` exit 0) and `nginx -t` passed — config was never the issue.
- **Fix (smallest possible):** `sudo chmod o+x /home/trevor` (**0750 → 0751**) — grant "other" *traverse-only* (not list, not read) on the sole blocking node. Everything beneath was already world-rx (`.next/static` dirs `0775`, css `0664`), so one bit restored the whole path. **No nginx config edit, no reload, no rebuild, no restart.** PERF-01 disk-serve intent preserved (no preference conflict).
- **Security:** `o+x` is traverse, not read — only already-world-readable files become reachable; verified `sudo -u www-data test -r .env.local` → still **DENIED** (protected by its own `0600`/`0640` bits). No allowlist / TLS / DB / bot touch.
- **Verified:** `www-data` now reads the css chunk (`stat OK, 77018 bytes`); `.env.local` still denied; Node :3333 still 200+`text/css`; `nginx -t` PASS. VM-through-nginx stays **403 (allowlist)** — expected ceiling from the VM; the `www-data` read-test + cleared error.log EACCES are the VM-side proof. End-to-end browser confirm = Ghost from an allowlisted IP. **Files: `CLAUDE.md` only** (no repo conf changed — fix is a filesystem chmod). git add CLAUDE.md only.

### 2026-06-03 — PERF-07: per-page Suspense streaming — AUDITED, CLOSED NO-CODE (no surgical TTFB win on this architecture)
- **Render-path perf audit, no money path, ZERO code change.** SOLO on `master`. Phase 0 audit (THOUGHTS gate) found the prompt's premise — "a slow flag read / data fetch blocks the entire first byte, wrap it in per-page `Suspense`" — **does not hold on this codebase**. Ghost decision at the gate: **close no-code, document the finding** (don't manufacture a win that isn't there).
- **`server.js` does NOT buffer responses → streaming IS supported at the server layer.** It uses the canonical `handle(req, res, parsedUrl)` pattern — hands the raw Node `res` straight to Next's request handler (no `res.end(buffered)`, no response proxy, no body/compression wrapper). Next streams SSR directly to `res`. (Edge caveat: nginx `proxy_buffering` is ON for normal HTML — REL-07 only disabled it for the SSE route — so the edge mildly buffers, but `server.js` itself does not, and that is not the blocker.)
- **The architecture already streams page content client-side.** Every zone view (`auto-zone-view`, `stocks-zone-view`, `intel-zone-view`, `memory-zone-view`) is a **`"use client"`** component that fetches its data client-side (raw `fetch` + `setInterval`). **No page body awaits a heavy server-side data read** — `grep runPython src/components` → exactly ONE hit: `app-shell.tsx`. So there is no slow server read in any page segment to wrap in `Suspense`.
- **The sole SSR first-byte blocker is ONE flag spawn in the ROOT LAYOUT.** `layout.tsx` → `<AppShell>` (async server component) `await`s `getAllFlagsCached()` → **`query_feature_flags.py`** (one prewarmed [PERF-04], request-deduped [PERF-03] Python subprocess) to choose legacy-vs-new nav, THEN renders `{children}`. Because it sits in the root layout, it gates the entire response (shell + nav + content). The zone pages' own gate flags hit the same React-`cache()` spawn → zero added first-byte cost.
- **Why per-PAGE Suspense gives ~no TTFB gain (load-bearing):** (1) a `loading.tsx` / `<Suspense>` at a page segment is a **child** of the root layout — it physically cannot unblock the layout's own `await`; the ~200ms flag spawn still gates first byte regardless of page-body boundaries. (2) There is no slow server read in any page body to wrap (content already client-streams). (3) The existing `loading.tsx` files (`autotrader`/`stocks`/`memory`/`intel`/`chat`/`login`) **already** provide Suspense streaming for in-app **client navigations** (instant skeleton on route transition) — that part of the win is already shipped.
- **Measured evidence:** `query_feature_flags.py` direct spawn **0.17–0.29 s**; authed live TTFB (`time_starttransfer`) steady-state **/autotrader 0.37s · /memory 0.25s · /stocks 0.26–0.28s · /intel 0.19–0.24s** (one 0.93s cold-render first hit). **TTFB ≈ flag-spawn time + small render** — no second slow read hides in the page body.
- **The only boundary that could move first-byte TTFB = restructuring the root-layout `AppShell`** so the nav chrome streams a fallback before the flag resolves. **Rejected** (not built): the flag picks the ENTIRE nav shell (legacy vs new) that wraps everything, so a fallback→real-shell swap reflows the whole page = the layout shift the prompt forbids; `HUB_REDESIGN_NAV` is effectively static in prod → high blast radius, low reward, not surgical.
- **Flagged for a future wave (RM-HUB-R), NOT built here:** dropping the ~200ms flag spawn off first byte via a short-TTL **in-process cache** of `getAllFlagsCached` would be a real TTFB win — but that's a **PERF-03-style** caching change, NOT the `Suspense` streaming PERF-07 names. Candidate for a dedicated prompt; out of scope for PERF-07.
- **No `BEHAVIOR_RULES.md` in the Hub repo** (the prompt's Phase 0 step 1 references the bot repo's copy; Hub rules live in this `CLAUDE.md`). No prior streaming/rendering preference → no `⚠️ PREFERENCE WARNING`.
- **Regression posture:** zero code change → all 5 recurring-bug canaries (signal dedup/cooldown, HOLD card delete, orphaned reminders, reply handler, results auto-delete) are bot-side → trivially untouched. No build, no service restart, no `auto_config`/flag flips, no DB writes, no design tokens, no new npm deps, no sacred-file touches.
- **Files (1):** `CLAUDE.md` (this entry only). `tsconfig.tsbuildinfo` NOT staged. Commit prefix `docs:` on `master`.
- **Preference (2026-06-03):** Hub SSR streaming verdict — **`server.js` does NOT buffer** (canonical `handle(req,res)`; streaming supported). The Hub's pages already stream content **client-side** (all `*-zone-view` are `"use client"`, data via `fetch`+poll); the **only** SSR first-byte blocker is the ROOT-LAYOUT `AppShell` flag spawn (`query_feature_flags.py`, prewarmed [PERF-04] + request-deduped [PERF-03], ~200ms). **Per-PAGE `Suspense`/`loading.tsx` cannot unblock a root-layout `await`** and there is no slow server read in any page body to wrap → PERF-07 closed no-code. Existing `loading.tsx` files already stream client-nav transitions. A short-TTL in-process flag cache (PERF-03-style, NOT Suspense) is the real first-byte lever — deferred to RM-HUB-R.

### 2026-06-03 — QUAL-05: remove dead trades components + recharts + orphan helpers; untrack helpers from bot TRACKED_HELPERS (RM-HUB Wave 7, prompt 1 — SOLO on master, cross-repo, DEPLOYED)
- **Dead-code removal, no money path.** SOLO on `master` (spans both repos + deletion discipline — not a worktree). Proven-dead-only; all API routes KEPT (none proven dead to bar). Ghost confirmed the removal list at the Phase 0 gate.
- **Hub deletions (7 files, `git rm`):** dead `trades/` components `history-table.tsx` + `journal-tab.tsx` + `trade-form.tsx` (zero importers — no app page/route/symbol/dynamic-import references; the `trades/` dir is now gone); the two `charts/` recharts wrappers `StyledLineChart.tsx` + `StyledBarChart.tsx` (`StyledBarChart` already orphaned; `StyledLineChart`'s only importer was the dead `history-table.tsx`) — required to make recharts truly unused; orphan Python helpers `query_similar_trades.py` + `query_calibration_deep.py` (Hub-repo root; only reference was a now-updated comment in `intel-zone-view.tsx`; nothing in Hub or bot subprocesses them).

### 2026-06-03 — PERF-06 + INFRA-04 + INFRA-07 + QUAL-06: jitter price polls + nginx proxy_connect_timeout + ssl_protocols/gzip + dep/IP/comment cleanup (RM-HUB Wave 6 polish batch — WORKTREE, NOT MERGED)
- **Four low-risk polish/infra quick-wins, no money path.** Worktree `/home/trevor/worktrees/HUB-POLISH-W6` on `feat/HUB-POLISH-W6` (off `6f13b8c`). No restart/reload/push inside the worktree — nginx reload (INFRA-04, INFRA-07) + Hub rebuild/restart (PERF-06, QUAL-06 IP/comment) happen on main at merge-all. No order/size/leverage/cap/killswitch/sacred-file/trading-logic touch.
- **PERF-06 — jitter the two synchronized 2s `/api/prices` polls.** `PriceStrip.tsx:32` and `active-position-card.tsx:123` both ran a bare `setInterval(fetchPrices, 2_000)` started right after a synchronous initial fetch → on any shared mount they fired in lockstep, hitting `/api/prices` in coordinated 2s bursts. Each is now a **self-scheduling `setTimeout` loop at `2_000 + Math.random()*400` ms, re-randomized every tick**, guarded by the existing `cancelled` flag + `clearTimeout` teardown. Effective cadence ~2.2s avg (LP-01 2s preference preserved); the two desync so the burst flattens. Pure burst-flattening — LP-01 already guarantees these hit the WS store / cache, never fresh HL REST, so zero behavior/cost change beyond timing. `active-position-card`'s 15s `fetchTrades` interval left as-is (only the colliding 2s price poll jittered).
- **INFRA-04 — `proxy_connect_timeout 3s` on the Hub `location /`** (staged `deploy/nginx/trevor-hub.conf`). The block set long `proxy_read/send_timeout 86400s` but no connect timeout → the 60s default meant a wedged/restarting Node made nginx hang ~60s before failing. 3s fails fast during a restart (a healthy local connect is sub-ms) while tolerating a brief restart stall. `/ws/terminal` + `/api/chat/stream` connect defaults left alone (per Ghost).
- **INFRA-07 — global `ssl_protocols` correction (merge-step edit) + gzip widening for static; brotli DEFERRED.**
  - **ssl_protocols:** global `/etc/nginx/nginx.conf:32` is `ssl_protocols TLSv1 TLSv1.1 TLSv1.2 TLSv1.3;` — includes deprecated **TLSv1 + TLSv1.1** (RFC 8996). Correction → `ssl_protocols TLSv1.2 TLSv1.3;`. Per Ghost (Decision 1) this global file is **NOT staged into the Hub repo** (committing a full copy invites drift from live) — applied as a one-line edit at merge (see Merge Step). Proposed value validated standalone: `nginx -t` exit 0.
  - **brotli: DEFERRED — module not compiled.** `nginx -V` shows only `--with-http_gzip_static_module` + `--with-http_v2_module`, no brotli. A `brotli` directive would fail `nginx -t` ("unknown directive"), so gzip-only until the module is added.
  - **gzip for static:** the Hub server block already had its own `gzip on; gzip_types … application/javascript text/css …`, and nginx 1.18 `mime.types` maps `.js→application/javascript`/`.css→text/css` — so the disk-served `/_next/static` assets (PERF-01) were **already gzipped**. Widened the Hub-block `gzip_types` defensively with `text/javascript image/svg+xml application/wasm` (additive; the headline INFRA-07 win is the ssl_protocols fix, not this).
- **QUAL-06 — IP externalized + stale comment fixed; @emnapi DEFERRED (premise false).**
  - **@emnapi (Ghost Decision 2 — no-op):** the prompt's "unused dep to `npm prune`" premise is **false**. `@emnapi` is absent from `package.json` deps/devDeps and unimported in `src/`; in the lockfile it's only a **transitive *optional*** dep of `@img/sharp-wasm32` (`^1.7.0`) + bundled in `@tailwindcss/oxide-wasm32-wasi` (wasm fallbacks). `npm prune @emnapi` is a no-op and hand-deleting the lockfile entry would be wrong (re-added on next install, risks the wasm fallback). **package.json + package-lock.json NOT touched.**
  - **Hardcoded VM IP → env (`src/middleware.ts`):** the direct-IP→domain 301 redirect hardcoded `34.28.231.36`. Now reads `process.env.HUB_VM_IP || "34.28.231.36"` (sane default → redirect unchanged if the env var is unset; a VM IP change is now a config flip, not a code edit).
  - **Stale sqlite3 comment (`src/app/api/status/route.ts:60`):** comment claimed `// Query DB via Python (sqlite3 CLI not installed)` — but `which sqlite3` → `/usr/bin/sqlite3` IS installed. Corrected: the real reason is the architectural Python bridge (the Hub has no Node SQLite binding), never a missing CLI.
- **Each item independent.** @emnapi deferred (premise false) without blocking the other three; the other items are orthogonal (2 TSX files, 1 middleware, 1 route comment, 1 staged nginx file, 1 global merge-step edit).
- **Gates:** `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0 (whole project). `npm run build` exit 0 (`/autotrader` 20.3 kB, `/intel` 12.6 kB, middleware 34.4 kB — no drift). Staged Hub block `nginx -t` exit 0 via a transient `/tmp` wrapper that `include`s the worktree conf (live `/etc/nginx/sites-available/trevor-hub` md5 unchanged, no reload). Proposed global `ssl_protocols TLSv1.2 TLSv1.3` `nginx -t` exit 0 standalone. No standalone TS LSP server installed → `tsc` is authoritative per repo convention. (worktree-local `node_modules` symlink to the main repo removed before commit, NOT committed.)
- **Regression posture:** Hub-only (2 client components + 1 middleware + 1 route comment + nginx config), no bot restart → all 5 recurring-bug canaries (signal dedup/cooldown, HOLD card delete, orphaned reminders, reply handler, results auto-delete) are bot-side → trivially untouched. No `auto_config`/flag flips, no DB schema/writes, no design tokens, no chart code, no new npm deps, no sacred-file touches (`.sacred_manifest.sha256` not touched; zero `/home/trevor/trevor/` writes).

(b) **Hub rebuild + restart** — `npm run build` → `sudo systemctl restart trevor-dashboard.service` (picks up PERF-06 jitter + QUAL-06 IP/comment). Then live-verify: `/api/prices` polls from PriceStrip + active-position-card no longer arrive simultaneously; direct-IP hit 301s to the domain (optionally set `HUB_VM_IP` to confirm the env override); `curl -I https://trevor-prime.com/_next/static/<chunk>.js` shows `Content-Encoding: gzip`; an outdated-TLS handshake (`openssl s_client -tls1_1`) is refused. **DEFERRED:** brotli (no nginx module compiled).

Global nginx `ssl_protocols` = **`TLSv1.2 TLSv1.3`** only (TLSv1/1.1 dropped, RFC 8996). **brotli is unavailable** (no module compiled in nginx 1.18.0) — gzip-only; the Hub block gzips static `/_next/static` JS/CSS (already covered, widened defensively). ### 2026-06-03 — QUAL-01: killswitch readout fail-SAFE on DB error (RM-HUB Wave 5, batch 1 — WORKTREE, NOT MERGED)
- **Safety-critical readout hardening, no money path, no control-path change.** `src/app/api/system-health/route.ts` only. Worktree `/home/trevor/worktrees/QUAL-01` on `feat/QUAL-01` (off `d7b30b6`). No restart/reload/push inside the worktree — Hub restart happens on main at merge-all (see Merge Step).
- **⚠️ Prompt-vs-reality correction (Phase 0 gate, Ghost-confirmed).** The prompt's premise — "the readout reads a deleted `.kill_switch` flat file → always reports inactive" — was **already fixed** by RM-AH-P03 (commit `28daed0`): the route reads `auto_config.EMERGENCY_KILLSWITCH` correctly, and the dead flat file does not exist (zero `.kill_switch` refs in the worktree). The CLAUDE.md "Known Issues" entry citing `route.ts:81` was **stale** and is what generated the prompt. Additionally: **no Hub component consumes this route's `kill_switch` field** — `/memory?tab=health` sources the killswitch tile from `/api/killswitch` (`<KillswitchControlCard>`) + `/api/heartbeat` (`<HeartbeatView>`), so the prompt's stated failure mode could not occur via this route. Per Ghost (Option 1): fix the one genuine remaining safety bug — the **fail-OPEN** error path.
- **The fix (surgical, except-branch only).** The DB read of `auto_config.EMERGENCY_KILLSWITCH` (string `"true"/"false"` → `active:true/false`, value format confirmed from the live DB) is **untouched**. Only the `except` branch changed: was `result["kill_switch"] = {"active": False}` (fail-OPEN — a false all-clear on any DB error, the exact anti-pattern a safety readout must avoid); now `{"active": None, "status": "unknown", "error": str(ks_exc)}` (fail-SAFE — a human investigates rather than trusting a stale "inactive"). Comment block updated fail-open → fail-SAFE.
- **Readout-only. Zero control-path / trading-logic touch.** Hub stays read-only on `trevor.db` (`mode=ro`). The killswitch ENGAGE/DISENGAGE path (bot-side, money-path) is not touched. The production killswitch was NOT engaged for testing.
- **Verification.** In-house (exact killswitch block, isolated): real DB → `{active:false}`; fixture engaged (`value='true'`) → `{active:true, reason:...}`; DB read failure (bad path) → `{active:null, status:"unknown", error:...}` — fail-SAFE, NOT false-inactive. Full embedded `pyScript` `py_compile` OK; old fail-open branch asserted absent, new fail-safe branch asserted present. **LIVE route** (`/api/system-health` on running :3333): `kill_switch.active=false` matches the real DB value `false` right now (happy-path readout reflects real state). The fail-safe error-path change is verified live on main after merge+restart (no restart inside worktree). No TS LSP server installed (tsc is authoritative per repo convention); the edit is confined to the embedded Python template-literal string, so no TS surface changed.
- **Regression posture:** Hub-only, single route, readout-only, no bot restart → all 5 recurring-bug canaries (signal dedup/cooldown, HOLD card delete, orphaned reminders, reply handler, results auto-delete) are bot-side → trivially untouched. No `auto_config`/flag flips, no DB writes, no design tokens, no chart code, no sacred-file touches (`.sacred_manifest.sha256` not touched).
- **Files (2):** `src/app/api/system-health/route.ts` (except-branch + comment) · `CLAUDE.md` (this entry + corrected Known Issue). `tsconfig.tsbuildinfo` NOT staged (perpetually-modified). Commit prefix `fix(safety):` on `feat/QUAL-01`; not pushed/merged.
- **⚠️ MERGE STEP (on main, after merging `feat/QUAL-01`):** rebuild + `sudo systemctl restart trevor-dashboard.service`, then re-confirm `/api/system-health` `kill_switch.active` matches the real `auto_config.EMERGENCY_KILLSWITCH` value live. ⚠️ **QUAL-04 (W5-P3) shares the system-health route surface and merges AFTER this** — the QUAL-01 edit is confined to the killswitch `except` branch to keep the merge clean.
- **Preference (2026-06-03):** the Hub killswitch readout source is **`auto_config.EMERGENCY_KILLSWITCH`** (string `"true"/"false"`; engaged = `active:true`) — never a flat file. A safety readout **fails SAFE**: on a DB read error report `{"active": null, "status": "unknown"}`, NEVER a false `active:false` all-clear, so a human investigates. This applies to `/api/system-health`'s `kill_switch` field (and is the standing direction for any future killswitch/safety readout).
### 2026-06-03 — QUAL-02 + QUAL-03: parameterize query_trades.py SQL + feature-flag union/stub hygiene (RM-HUB Wave 5, batch 2 — WORKTREE, NOT MERGED)
- **Two code-correctness fixes, no money path.** Worktree `/home/trevor/worktrees/QUAL-02-03` on `feat/QUAL-02-03` (off `d7b30b6`). No restart/push inside the worktree — Hub restart happens on main at merge-all. No order/size/leverage/cap/killswitch/sacred-file/trading-logic touch.
- **QUAL-02 — parameterize the `history`-scope SELECT in `query_trades.py`.** Two user-influenced values (from `json.loads(sys.argv[4])`) were f-string-interpolated into SQL: `ticker LIKE '%{filters['ticker']}%'` and `direction='{filters['direction']}'`, then joined into `f"SELECT COUNT(*) ... WHERE {where}"` + the `f"""...WHERE {where} ... LIMIT {limit} OFFSET {offset}"""` SELECT — SQL-injectable. Fixed with `?` placeholders + a `params` list: `ticker LIKE ?` (wildcards moved into the bound value — LIKE semantics unchanged), `direction = ?`, and `LIMIT ? OFFSET ?` (limit/offset were already `int()`-cast/non-injectable but parameterized too so the last f-string is gone). The query strings are now plain string-concat of **static** fragments (`status = 'closed'`, `ticker LIKE ?`, `direction = ?`, `pnl_pct > 0`/`<= 0`) — no value interpolation. **Identical results preserved.**
  - **⚠️ Line 362 (`UPDATE ... WHERE rowid IN ({placeholders})`) INTENTIONALLY untouched** — that's the canonical-safe dynamic-`IN` idiom: `placeholders = ",".join("?" for _ in ids)` expands to `?,?,?` (placeholder *marks*, never user values; values bound via `[status] + [int(i) for i in ids]`). Correct as-is; out of scope.
  - **Verified in-house:** `py_compile` OK; old-vs-new id-set comparison over the live DB across 7 representative filter combos ({}, ticker, direction, outcome, and mixes) → **ALL IDENTICAL**; injection input `ticker="BTC'; DROP--"` → parameterized path returns 0 rows (literal LIKE match), `active_trades` intact (73 rows) — the OLD path would have embedded `...LIKE '%BTC'; DROP--%'...` in the SQL text. `grep` confirms zero remaining value-interpolating f-string SQL (scoped to the lines 86/93/98/99 sites; line 362 safe idiom retained). The file's DELETE/UPDATE write paths were NOT touched (read-SELECT change only).
- **QUAL-03 — `feature-flags.ts` union + stub hygiene.** (a) Added `HUB_REDESIGN_DOCS` to the `FlagName` union **and** the `ALL_FLAGS` array (the flag is live per the Feature Flags table but was missing from the typed union — type hygiene). (b) Removed the dead `readFlag()` stub (always returned `{value:false, source:"default"}`) **and** its sole caller `isFlagOn()` — both were unreferenced anywhere in `src/`; the real server-side flag source is `getAllFlagsCached` in `feature-flags-server.ts` (PERF-03). `FlagName`/`ALL_FLAGS`/`FlagState` type surface + the real `readCookieOverride()` util are retained. The whole module is NOT deleted (per Ghost — `readCookieOverride` is a real util + the type surface is wanted).
  - **⚠️ Prompt-vs-reality correction (Phase 0 gate, Ghost-confirmed):** the prompt said to "drop the stale `SCOUT_V3_FEED`" from the union — but `SCOUT_V3_FEED` was **never in `feature-flags.ts`** (the union only ever held the 9 `HUB_REDESIGN_*` flags; the only `src/` reference is a comment in `stock-section.tsx`). No-op. Also: the entire `feature-flags.ts` module had **zero importers** in `src/` (verified), so removing the stub chain cannot break any consumer.
  - **Verified:** union includes `HUB_REDESIGN_DOCS`, excludes `SCOUT_V3_FEED`; the only remaining `readFlag`/`isFlagOn` matches are in the explanatory comment (no code refs) → no dangling references; **`npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0** (whole project, via a temporary worktree-local `node_modules` symlink to the main repo — removed before commit, NOT committed). No standalone TS LSP server installed → `tsc` is the authoritative type check per repo convention.
- **Regression posture:** Hub-only, no bot restart → all 5 recurring-bug canaries (signal dedup/cooldown, HOLD card delete, orphaned reminders, reply handler, results auto-delete) are bot-side → trivially untouched. No `auto_config`/flag flips, no DB schema/writes, no design tokens, no new npm deps, no sacred-file touches (`.sacred_manifest.sha256` not touched).

Commit prefix `fix:` on `feat/QUAL-02-03`; not pushed/merged.

Merge order between the two is free.
