### 2026-05-27 — D4: Shadow page overhaul — compact cards + Active/Dormant tabs + function sections
- **`/intel?tab=shadow` rewritten from long-scroll grid → compact, tabbed, function-grouped layout.** The old `<ShadowSection>` (17 full-size `<ShadowTableCard>` × 4-up grid, ~7-10 mobile screens of scroll) replaced by `<ShadowOverview>` (~3-4 screens) per Ghost's "too big, too much scroll — make it glanceable" brief. All 25 shadows visible across the two tabs (20 ACTIVE + 5 DORMANT, matches A1 §9 inventory exactly; 0 BROKEN at deploy).
- **Active/Dormant `<TabBar>` at top.** Count badges per tab (`Active (N)` includes the Shadow Scoring hero in the N; `Dormant (M)` only shows function sections that have dormant shadows). React state, not URL params — this is a nested tab inside the Intel SHADOW sub-tab; URL `?tab=shadow` continues to route to the SHADOW sub-tab, and the inner Active/Dormant state lives in `useState`.
- **Function sub-sections** via `<CollapsibleSection>` + `rightSlot={<Pill intent="active|warn" size="sm">{count}</Pill>}` — per C1 Ghost ruling 2 (no `<CategorySection>` primitive built; `<CollapsibleSection>` is the documented single source of truth). Order: Entry → Exit → Scoring → Risk → Data. `defaultOpen` heuristic: ≤5 items → open, >5 → collapsed (current data has Entry=6 + Exit=6 active so those start collapsed; everything else open).
- **`<ShadowScoringHero>` at the top of the Active tab.** Dedicated card for the HMM+DS pipeline (the 22 K+ row core scoring system), visually distinct via `card-elevated` + `border-l-4 border-l-accent-cyan-soft-strong`. Shows Method · Rows · Ready · Last Score in a 4-up `<MetricTile>` grid + progress bar (re-uses the existing hero's metric set from `shadow-section.tsx`). To populate Method / Ready / progress, `<ShadowOverview>` fetches `/api/intel/shadow` in parallel with `/api/shadow/registry` (`Promise.all`) — registry alone doesn't carry `model_method` / `rows_required_for_analysis` / `ready_for_analysis`. **`shadow_scoring` is stripped from the Scoring function section** to avoid visual duplication (Ghost decision at D4 Phase 0 gate). Scoring section therefore shows 3 active (`shadow_scorer_v2`, `calibrator_audit`, `calibration_v2_audit`) and 1 dormant (`group_weight_shadow`).
- **`<CompactShadowCard>` (C1 primitive) — adapter, not modification.** Registry fields (`display`, `table_name`, `rows`, `rows_48h`, `latest_write` ISO, `status` `"ACTIVE"|"DORMANT"|"BROKEN"`) map to card props (`name`, `tableName`, `totalRows`, `rows48h`, `latestAge` relative, `status` `"active"|"dormant"`) in a local `adaptCard()` helper. `BROKEN` collapses to `"dormant"` for card visual + gets surfaced via the Dormant tab. `fmtAge()` mirrors the proven UTC-Z normalizer from `shadow-table-card.tsx` (SQLite naive timestamps + ISO-offset tolerated). Each card wrapped in a `border-l-2` div with activity-driven left color (>100 rows/48h → cyan-soft-strong, 10-100 → cyan-soft/50, 1-9 → fg-faint, 0/dormant → border-subtle) — color coding achieved without modifying the C1 shared primitive.
- **Sort:** Active tab sorts each function section by `rows_48h` DESC (most active first); Dormant tab sorts by `rows` DESC (most historical data first). Per Phase 3 spec.
- **Accordion expand intentionally NOT enforced** — Ghost decision at D4 Phase 0 gate. `<CompactShadowCard>`'s built-in self-managed `expanded` state stays. Lifting state would require modifying the C1 shared primitive (`expanded` + `onToggle` props), which Ghost ruled against. Multiple cards can be expanded concurrently — acceptable for a glance-heavy compact layout.
- **`<ShadowSection>` + `<ShadowTableCard>` left on disk, no longer imported.** Per Phase 4 hard constraint ("Don't delete old shadow components. Stop importing them, but leave the files. Cleanup is a separate prompt."). The only import-site, `intel-zone-view.tsx:33`, now points at `<ShadowOverview>`. `/api/intel/shadow` route + `query_shadow_status.py` stay live (the hero uses the route).
- **Files (new, 2):** `src/components/intel/shadow-overview.tsx` (~210 lines), `src/components/intel/shadow-scoring-hero.tsx` (~110 lines). **(modified, 2):** `src/components/intel/intel-zone-view.tsx` (swap `ShadowSection` → `ShadowOverview` import + 1 JSX line; docblock entry for D4), `CLAUDE.md` (this entry).
- **Untouched on purpose:** `src/components/intel/notes-section.tsx`, `LessonsSection`, `JournalSection` — D4 scope is the SHADOW sub-tab only; the other 3 Intel sub-tabs are byte-identical. C1 shared primitives (`CompactShadowCard`, `CollapsibleSection`, `TabBar`, `Pill`) consumed via barrel `@/components/ui` — none modified. No new API routes, no new Python helpers (registry endpoint shipped in B1c). No bot file touched. No service restart. No `auto_config` flag flip. No DB schema change.
- **Verified:** `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0 (clean). `npm run build` clean — `/intel` route **9.33 kB** (was 7.88 kB at 2026-05-26 baseline; +1.45 kB net for the new tab/hero/grouping vs replaced shadow-section grid — old components still on disk but no longer imported, so the +1.45 kB is the marginal cost of the new layout). Sacred 12/12 untouched (Hub-only wave).
- **D4 commit prefix:** `feat(D4-shadow)`. Hub `feat/D4-shadow` worktree at `/home/trevor/worktrees/D4`. NOT merged — awaiting D1/D2/D3 to complete + Ghost's `scripts/worktree_merge_all.sh` orchestration per the wave spec.

### 2026-05-27 — D3: AutoTrader Activity tab — paginated change_log + source filters
- **Wave D3 of the AutoTrader/Shadow overhaul.** The Activity sub-tab on `/autotrader?tab=activity` now ships the paginated `change_log` readout C1's placeholder reserved. Parallel with D1 / D2 / D4 — each in its own worktree off the C1 commit.
- **`src/components/autotrader-v2/activity-tab.tsx` rewritten** (~210 lines; was a 17-line placeholder). Fetches `GET /api/auto/activity?limit=50&offset=<n>&source_type=<v>` (B1c endpoint), newest first. State: `entries[]`, `total`, `loading`, `initialLoad`, `error`, `chipLabel`. `hasMore = entries.length < total`. Defensive force-stop if the API ever returns an empty page while still claiming more rows.
- **Single `<FilterChips>` bar** — 6 chips: **All · UI · Discord · Autonomous · CC Prompt · CLI-Script**. Display label → API `source_type` mapping (verified against `audit_logger.py:59` SOURCE_TYPES enum `{UI, Discord, Autonomous, Direct-Edit, CLI-Script, Trigger, API, unknown}`): `CC Prompt → "API"` (CC sessions write through the Hub API); other chips map 1:1; `All` sends no `source_type` param. `Trigger` rows (close_audit_log fires; 5/9 of today's rows) intentionally not chipped — surface under `All` only, per Ghost ruling at THOUGHTS gate. Switching chips resets `entries`/`total` and refetches from offset 0 (via the `[sourceValue]` effect — cancelled flag protects against late responses).
- **Infinite scroll via `IntersectionObserver`** (per prompt §1.4). A 1px sentinel `<div ref={sentinelRef}/>` sits after the last `<ActivityRow>`. On intersection (`rootMargin: "120px"` so the next page kicks in just before the sentinel is fully in view), `loadMore()` fires with `offset = entries.length`. `loadingRef` guards re-entry; the observer effect re-creates the IO on every `[hasMore, loading, initialLoad, loadMore]` change so the auto-cascade fills tall viewports without a stuck state. Cleanup `observer.disconnect()` in every teardown.
- **Empty / loading / error states.** `initialLoad` → `<Skeleton h-32 w-full/>`. `entries.length === 0 && !filterActive` → `EmptyState` "No activity recorded yet. Changes will appear here once you edit configs or toggles." `entries.length === 0 && filterActive` → `EmptyState "No <Chip> activity found" · "Try a different source filter."` Mid-pagination → `<Skeleton h-4 w-32/>` strip below the sentinel. End-of-feed → `— No more entries —` sans-uppercase divider. API/network errors → red banner above the list (`accent-red/5` bg + `accent-red/40` border).
- **Each entry rendered via C1's `<ActivityRow>` shared primitive** (unmodified, imported from `@/components/ui` barrel). Built-ins reused as-shipped: UTC-Z timestamp parser → relative `Ns/m/h/d ago` with raw stamp in `title` tooltip; expand-to-show actor + source + promptId + notes `<dl>`; source `<Pill intent>` map (UI → blue-chip · Discord → mid-cap · Autonomous → warn · CC|Claude → active · else → neutral default). `Trigger`/`CLI-Script`/`API` source-types render with the default neutral pill — per Ghost ruling at THOUGHTS gate, acceptable for D3; extending the intent map is a future surgical edit to ActivityRow, not D3 scope. Null-`key` rows fall back to `<table_name>#<row_id>` for display; null `new_value` shows `—`.
- **State preservation across tab switches** — `<AutoZoneView subtab="activity">` mounts/unmounts on tab changes, so component remount refetches from offset 0 and resets the filter chip to "All". Acceptable per prompt §2.1 ("preserves state OR refetches — either acceptable").
- **Verified.** `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0. `npx next build` clean — `/autotrader` route **13.2 kB** (was 12.3 kB at C1; +0.9 kB for the new tab). Live `query_activity.py 5 0` against production `trevor.db` returned 5 of 9 rows (4 `Autonomous` from `DISCOVERED_TICKERS` updates + 5 `Trigger` from close_audit_log fires) — UI handles both source-types and the null-`old_value` shape gracefully. **Service restart deferred** — never restarted from inside a worktree workflow per established Hub-wave pattern; D1 / D2 / D4 will report and the bundle picks up at the next coordinated restart.
- **Untouched on purpose:** B1c `/api/auto/activity` route + `query_activity.py` helper (consumed as-shipped — they were built for D3). `<ActivityRow>` + `<FilterChips>` shared primitives (C1 infra; D3 imports from `@/components/ui` — zero duplication). `<AutoZoneView>` dispatcher + the other 4 tab files. Bot files, sacred files, `trevor.db` schema, design tokens. No new npm deps. No flag flips. No DB schema changes.
- **Path note** (carry-over from C1's D-wave note): the prompt brief referred to `src/components/auto/ActivityTab.tsx` and a `shared` dir — actual paths are `src/components/autotrader-v2/activity-tab.tsx` (kebab-case) and `src/components/ui/` (barrel `index.ts`). Already correct at the worktree from C1's commit.
- **Hub `master` commit prefix:** `feat(D3-activity)` — committed in `/home/trevor/worktrees/D3` on branch `feat/D3-activity`, **not pushed, not merged this prompt** (D1 / D2 / D4 still in flight; merge wave follows).

### 2026-05-27 — D2: Control Center sub-tab — 85 toggles grouped by category + Tier 1/2/3 confirm tiers (worktree)

- **D2 wave of the AutoTrader/Shadow overhaul.** Replaces the C1 `<ControlTab>` placeholder with a live-editable interface for every `auto_config` boolean. Pause/Resume at the top, 7 collapsible category sections in the middle, recent toggle changes from B1b `change_log` at the bottom.
- **Filter discipline.** The 4 dedicated-endpoint keys (`AUTO_TRADER_ENABLED`, `EMERGENCY_KILLSWITCH`, `LIVE_PARTIALS_ENABLED`, `CONFIRM_CYCLES_PROMOTED`) are filtered OUT of the toggle list — each already has its own dedicated card / pill / page surface. The control-full PATCH refuses them with 400; rendering them here would be misleading duplication. Footer advisory documents which keys are hidden and where they live. 89 − 4 = **85 visible toggles**.
- **Layout (top → bottom):**
  1. `<AutoTraderToggleCard />` — reused as-is from the Dashboard tab (Rule 32 carve-out, owns its own 2-tap BottomSheet + audit + 30s poll). Per Ghost decision: don't extract a separate PauseResumeSection — the card is already perfect.
  2. LIVE_EDIT_ENABLED state banner — UNLOCKED (mint, write-mode advisory) vs LOCKED (gold, with copy-paste SQL to enable). Banner is non-interactive; the gate flip path is a deliberate Ghost decision via SQL or CLI.
  3. Toast strip — `{ok|warn|err}` 4s lifetime, surfaces the PATCH result.
  4. 7 `<CollapsibleSection>`s in A1 §3 order: **Execution (10) · Signal Gates (28) · Critic Stack (8) · Calibration (5) · Risk (16) · Experimental (6) · Misc (16)** — actual visible counts after dedicated-key filter: **8 · 26 · 8 · 5 · 16 · 6 · 16 = 85** (4 keys filtered out: 2 from Execution — `AUTO_TRADER_ENABLED`, `EMERGENCY_KILLSWITCH`; 2 from Signal Gates — `LIVE_PARTIALS_ENABLED`, `CONFIRM_CYCLES_PROMOTED`). Default-open: Execution + Signal Gates (highest impact, scanned first); the rest collapsed. `rightSlot` shows a red `<Pill intent="error">{N} T1</Pill>` when the category has Tier-1 keys, then a blue-chip count.
  5. Hidden-keys advisory card.
  6. `<RecentTogglesSection />` — bottom card reading `/api/auto/activity?limit=50&table_name=auto_config`, client-filters to `new_value ∈ {true, false}` (drops non-bool flips like `DISCOVERED_TICKERS` JSON), renders top 10 via `<ActivityRow>`. "View all activity →" link routes to `/autotrader?tab=activity` (D3 placeholder for now). 30s poll.
- **Sensitivity tier behavior (C1 `<ToggleSwitch>` carries the burden):**
  - **Tier 1 (destructive, A1 §9):** tap → `<ConfirmModal requireReason destructive>` opens. Confirm disabled until non-empty reason. Reason flows into the PATCH body and lands in `change_log.notes` via the new helper extension.
  - **Tier 2 (sensitive):** tap → in-row 2s gold warning string `Toggling "X" — sensitive flag.` then PATCH fires.
  - **Tier 3 (free):** tap → PATCH fires immediately.
- **B1c PATCH extension (additive, 3 lines TS + 2 lines Python):** `PATCH /api/auto/control-full/[key]` now accepts optional `reason` in body, passed as 5th argv to `write_control_value.py`. The helper threads it to `audit_config_write(notes=reason or "D2 toggle flip")`. Backward compatible — the 4-argv signature still works for any caller that doesn't pass reason. Per Ghost ruling 1 at the THOUGHTS gate: extend B1c, don't rewrite or stash reason in author.
- **423 handling:** when `LIVE_EDIT_ENABLED=false`, PATCH returns 423 (not 403 as the prompt brief said — B1c uses 423 Locked). UI catches it in `handleToggle`, surfaces a gold warn toast `"Editing locked — set LIVE_EDIT_ENABLED=true in auto_config to enable toggle writes."` and throws so the ToggleSwitch optimistic state rolls back. Toggle switches themselves render `disabled` when `liveEdit === false` for visual confirmation that taps are non-productive.
- **No `<CategorySection>` primitive created** — per C1 ruling, `<CollapsibleSection title=... rightSlot={<Pill>{count}</Pill>}>` is the chosen pattern; D2 follows it.
- **Files (new, 1):** `src/components/autotrader-v2/recent-toggles-section.tsx`. **(modified, 3):** `src/components/autotrader-v2/control-tab.tsx` (placeholder → real D2 component, ~310 LOC) · `src/app/api/auto/control-full/[key]/route.ts` (+5 lines: read optional `reason` from body, conditional argv append) · `write_control_value.py` (+3 lines: read argv[4], thread to audit `notes`; docstring updated). No bot files, no DB writes, no sacred file touches.
- **Verified:** `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0 (zero new errors). `python3 write_control_value.py SHADOW_LOG_NEUTRAL false ghost "test reason"` with `LIVE_EDIT_ENABLED=false` returns exit 3 → JSON `{ok:false, gate_locked:true, status:423}` (gate path still fires correctly with the new arg). Live API verified: `/api/auto/control-full` returns 89 toggles across 7 categories with tier distribution 7/25/57. `/api/auto/activity?table_name=auto_config` returns the 16 current `change_log` rows (post-filter to bool yields the toggle flips).
- **Activation path:** merge `feat/D2-control` into master (Ghost runs `bash scripts/worktree_merge_all.sh --restart trevor-dashboard.service` after all D1-D4 wrap). Then `UPDATE auto_config SET value='true' WHERE key='LIVE_EDIT_ENABLED';` (no service restart needed; the helper reads it per-request). To disable: same SQL with `value='false'`.
- **Hub `master` commit prefix:** `feat(D2-control)`. Bot side untouched. Worktree: `/home/trevor/worktrees/D2` on branch `feat/D2-control`. **Sacred 9/9 untouched.** No flag flips. No new npm deps. No DB schema changes.

### 2026-05-27 — D1: Config sub-tab — editable thresholds + per-ticker panel
- **Config tab is real.** Replaces the C1 placeholder at `/autotrader?tab=config` with a fully-wired editor for the 60 non-boolean `auto_config` rows surfaced by `/api/auto/config-full`. Fetch + 60 s auto-refresh, debounced search (200 ms), grouped by A1 §2 category (Capital → Signal → Exit → Risk → Per-Ticker → Calibration → Execution → Misc), one `<EditableField>` per row with type-correct validation (int / float / text + JSON-parse for `type=json`). Calibration section auto-hides when empty (currently 0 rows). Honest about its own write-gate: when `LIVE_EDIT_ENABLED=false` a top gold banner reads "Live edit disabled — flip LIVE_EDIT_ENABLED first"; PATCH attempts still return HTTP 423 from the server, and the shared `handleSave` closure surfaces that as the inline EditableField error.
- **PerTickerPanel is the Per-Ticker category's renderer.** Replaces the flat key-value list with a ticker-centric view (`<FilterChips>` for BTC / ETH / SOL / HYPE / FARTCOIN). For the selected ticker it shows: confidence thresholds (quiet/normal/active from `TICKER_THRESHOLDS`); shadow thresholds V2 (`RECALIBRATED_THRESHOLDS_V2`); direction penalty; momentum floor; trail floor; max leverage (🔒 immutable per Ghost ruling 4). Those six values are **source-only** — they live in Python source (`ticker_thresholds.py` / `auto_trader/config.py` / `auto_trader/monitor.py` / `auto_trader/exit_helpers.py`), not `auto_config`, so the Hub mirrors them as read-only with the `Source-code config — edit via CC prompt` tooltip. Below them, an "Auto-Config Overrides" group surfaces the three editable DB-side per-ticker rows: **ALO Disabled** (`HapticButton` that toggles the ticker in/out of `ALO_DISABLED_TICKERS` JSON array), **Hard Stop PCT** (`<EditableField type="float">` that round-trips through `TICKER_HARD_STOP_PCT_JSON` dict), **Leverage V2 Seed** (same pattern for `LEVERAGE_V2_INTERMEDIATE_JSON`). Bottom of the panel lists `DISCOVERED_TICKERS` (67-row autonomous list) as read-only with a warn pill.
- **Shared primitives only** — D1 imports `<EditableField>`, `<CollapsibleSection>` (the `<CategorySection>` replacement per the C1 Ghost ruling — `rightSlot={<Pill size="sm">{count}</Pill>}` for category badges), `<FilterChips>`, `<Pill>`, `<Card>`/`<CardHeader>`/`<CardTitle>`, `<Skeleton>`, `<EmptyState>`, `<HapticButton>` — every primitive came from the C1 shared set. **No new ui/ primitives**, no design-token additions, no new npm deps.
- **Single save closure.** Both the per-category EditableFields and the PerTickerPanel funnel through the same `handleSave(key, newValue)` callback in `ConfigTab`. Maps PATCH responses: 200 → refetch (so `updated_at` stays fresh); 400 → `throw new Error(payload.error)` → EditableField inline error; 423 → `throw new Error("Editing disabled — flip LIVE_EDIT_ENABLED first.")`; other non-2xx → throw `payload.error ?? HTTP {status}`. JSON dict round-trip: `saveDictEntry()` in PerTickerPanel mutates the selected ticker's entry (or `delete`s it on empty input) and writes back the full serialized dict via the same closure.
- **Path discrepancies in the D1 brief — corrected per C1 carry-forward:** brief said `src/components/auto/ConfigTab.tsx` (PascalCase) → real path `src/components/autotrader-v2/config-tab.tsx` (kebab-case, matches every other tab + the auto-zone-view dispatcher). Brief said `CategorySection` exists as a shared primitive → it does NOT (C1 Ghost ruling — reuse `<CollapsibleSection rightSlot={<Pill>…</Pill>}>` instead). Brief said gate-locked is HTTP 403 → actual is HTTP 423 (matches the B1c spec + write_config_value.py exit-3 mapping).
- **Files (new, 1):** `src/components/autotrader-v2/per-ticker-panel.tsx` (~270 lines; source-of-truth dicts hardcoded from the verified bot-side files, JSON round-trip handlers, ALO toggle with inline pending/error). **(modified, 1):** `src/components/autotrader-v2/config-tab.tsx` — full overwrite of the C1 placeholder (~270 lines: fetch + poll + debounced search + grouped CollapsibleSection render + gate banner + skeleton + empty state). **`auto-zone-view.tsx` untouched** — already routes `case "config"` → `<ConfigTab />`, no wiring change needed.
- **Verified:** `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0 (clean). `next build` clean — `/autotrader` route **15.8 kB** (was 12.3 kB at C1, +3.5 kB for the two new components). Zero warnings / errors during build. Component-map row `autotrader-v2/` count 14 → 15 files (per-ticker-panel.tsx added).
- **Verification gaps (honest):** **No service restart** (per worktree-isolated wave pattern — restart deferred to `worktree_merge_all.sh` post-merge). **No live API smoke** against the running Hub (the live server still serves the C1 placeholder until merge + restart). **No 423/400/200 path exercise against the production endpoint** — that happens post-merge with `LIVE_EDIT_ENABLED` flipped briefly by Ghost. Static + build verification only inside the worktree.
- **Hardcoded source-of-truth note.** The six per-ticker scalar/dict values surfaced as read-only in `<PerTickerPanel>` were copied from `/home/trevor/trevor/ticker_thresholds.py` / `auto_trader/config.py` / `auto_trader/monitor.py` / `auto_trader/exit_helpers.py` at audit time (2026-05-27). If the bot-side files are edited via a CC prompt without paired D1 updates, the Hub display will drift. Acceptable trade-off vs. building a Python-source-parser endpoint: these dicts change rarely + observability is worth the staleness risk + alternatives (server-side import or AST parse) cross the Hub→bot read boundary and add fragility.
- **Untouched on purpose:** every file under `/home/trevor/trevor/` (Hub never modifies bot code). The 4 B1c API route files (`/api/auto/config-full` GET + `[key]` PATCH consumed as-is; no widening). Other autotrader-v2 components (Dashboard, Recent stay byte-identical; Control/Activity remain placeholders for D2/D3; Per-Ticker rendering on the existing `<ConfigCard>` dashboard tile is unchanged). All ui/ primitives. No new design tokens. No flag flips. No DB schema changes. No service restart. No `auto_config` writes during testing (validation done via tsc + build only — no test rows to clean up).
- **Hub commit scope (worktree `feat/D1-config`):** `src/components/autotrader-v2/config-tab.tsx` (overwrite) · `src/components/autotrader-v2/per-ticker-panel.tsx` (new) · `CLAUDE.md` (this entry + component-map row count). Awaiting merge into `master` via `worktree_merge_all.sh --restart trevor-dashboard.service` paired with D2/D3/D4.

### 2026-05-27 — C1: AutoTrader 5-tab shell + Dashboard + Recent + 6 shared D1-D4 primitives
- **`/autotrader` restructured from single-view → 5-tab shell.** Tabs (in order, Dashboard default): **Dashboard · Recent · Config · Control · Activity**. State via existing `?tab=` URL search params (deep-linkable, browser back/forward, sticky). The page is now a server component reading `searchParams` and rendering `<AutoZoneView subtab={...}>`. Config / Control / Activity ship as placeholder shells in C1; Wave D1 / D2 / D3 fill them via the B1c API endpoints (`/api/auto/config-full`, `/api/auto/control-full`, `/api/auto/activity`).
- **No `AutoTabNav` component built.** The existing `<ZoneSubTabs>` infrastructure (`src/components/navigation/zone-sub-tabs.tsx`) auto-renders the strip for any zone with `subTabs` defined in `src/lib/navigation.ts` — extending the AUTO zone with `subTabs` + `defaultSubTab: "dashboard"` was the only nav-side change. Matches the Intel/Stocks/Memory pattern exactly: same 28px edge-fade, same cyan-soft underline + glow, same 44px `tap-target`, same `role="tablist"` a11y, same back/forward navigation. Per Ghost ruling at the THOUGHTS gate: extend infra, do NOT duplicate.
- **Dashboard tab** ports every card from the deleted `<ScalperViewV2>` _minus_ `<RecentTradesCard>` — per Ghost ruling option (a), all toggle / shadow / config cards stay on Dashboard during the C1→D# gap so users retain zero-regression access: `<ScalperHeader>` + `<CapitalHero>` + `<ActivePositionCard>` + `<ConfigCard>` + `<WatchlistGrid>` + `<AutoTraderToggleCard>` + `<ExitControlsCard>` (B3) + `<PartialsToggleCard>` (B4) + `<PartialShadowCard>` (B4). D1 will replace `<ConfigCard>` with the new `<EditableField>` grid in the Config tab; D2 will carve toggle cards into the Control tab.
- **Recent tab** owns its own fetch (`/api/auto/trades?type=closed&limit=50`, 30 s refresh) and filtering. Three `<FilterChips>` bars: **Ticker** (ALL + 5 sacred {BTC, ETH, SOL, HYPE, FARTCOIN} + any extras discovered in the loaded set), **Direction** (ALL · LONG · SHORT), **Outcome** (ALL · PROFITABLE · LOSING — `pnl_pct > 0` vs `<= 0`). Filters combine; client-side. Row rendering matches the deleted `<RecentTradesCard>` layout (LIVE pill, ticker · direction, hold, timestamp, exit reason, % P&L) plus a `filtered/total` counter in the card header.
- **6 shared primitives** added to `src/components/ui/` for Wave D1-D4 consumption (barrel-exported from `@/components/ui`). Each generic, mobile-first (375 px verified), 44 px tap targets, refined A4 v1.1 styling (cyan-soft / mint / plum / gold; mono for data, sans for labels):
  - `filter-chips.tsx` — `<FilterChips options selected onChange ariaLabel? />`; horizontal scroll on mobile (`-mx-4` bleed, scrollbar-hidden), `tap-target`, cyan-soft border + subtle glow when selected, dimmed border + muted text when unselected. Already used by C1 Recent tab; D3 Activity + D4 Shadow consume it for source/function filtering.
  - `confirm-modal.tsx` — `<ConfirmModal isOpen title message requireReason? confirmLabel? cancelLabel? onConfirm onCancel destructive? />`. **Wraps `<BottomSheet>`** (inherits scrim + ESC + body-scroll-lock + sticky header + slide-up-mobile / centered-modal-desktop). Optional reason `<textarea>` (required → confirm disabled until non-empty). Destructive variant overrides confirm button to red. Pending state during async `onConfirm`. Foundational primitive for D2 ToggleSwitch tier 1.
  - `editable-field.tsx` — `<EditableField label value type={"text"|"number"|"float"} immutable? onSave validate? category? />`. Display mode renders label + mono value with hover `<Pencil>` affordance. Tap → input field appears with `<Check>` save + `<X>` cancel (Enter / Escape keyboard shortcuts). Validation runs `validate` first then a built-in type check (integer / decimal regex). `immutable` shows a `<Lock>` icon and disables the affordance. Errors render below the input. Optimistic update via the caller's `onSave` promise.
  - `toggle-switch.tsx` — `<ToggleSwitch label description? value sensitivity={1|2|3} onToggle disabled? />`. Switch visual: track + thumb, mint when ON (with `shadow-glow-subtle-mint`), neutral when OFF. **Sensitivity tiers per A1 §9**: tier 3 (free) → single tap toggles immediately; tier 2 (sensitive) → single tap + transient gold warning toast (2 s) before toggle fires; tier 1 (destructive) → tap opens `<ConfirmModal requireReason destructive>` and waits for confirmation. `onToggle(newValue, reason?)` signature so callers route the optional reason into the audit envelope.
  - `compact-shadow-card.tsx` — `<CompactShadowCard name tableName totalRows rows48h latestAge status={"active"|"dormant"} function extraMetrics? />`. **Collapsed (default):** one-line row with mint/faint status dot, name, rows count, 48h count, relative age — fits ~8-10 per mobile screen. **Expanded (on tap):** full metrics `<dl>` grid including table name, function group, status, plus arbitrary `extraMetrics`. Dormant cards render at `opacity-75`. The `function` prop name kept literal (D4's bot data shape uses it); component destructures with rename to dodge the JS keyword.
  - `activity-row.tsx` — `<ActivityRow timestamp keyName oldValue newValue actor sourceType promptId? notes? />`. Single row: relative-age timestamp · key · `old → new` mono · source `<Pill intent>` (UI → blue-chip / Discord → mid-cap / Autonomous → warn / CC|Claude → active / else → neutral). Tap to expand → actor + source + promptId (plum) + notes. UTC parser matches the `shadow-table-card.tsx` convention (appends `Z` to naive SQLite stamps so the browser reads UTC).
- **`<CategorySection>` intentionally NOT created** (per Ghost ruling 2 at THOUGHTS gate). The existing `<CollapsibleSection>` already covers the spec — D1 / D2 / D4 pass `rightSlot={<Pill size="sm">{count}</Pill>}` for the count badge. Single source of truth; no duplicate primitive.
- **Files (new, 11):** `src/components/autotrader-v2/auto-zone-view.tsx` · `dashboard-tab.tsx` · `recent-tab.tsx` · `config-tab.tsx` · `control-tab.tsx` · `activity-tab.tsx` · `src/components/ui/filter-chips.tsx` · `confirm-modal.tsx` · `editable-field.tsx` · `toggle-switch.tsx` · `compact-shadow-card.tsx` · `activity-row.tsx`. **(modified, 3):** `src/lib/navigation.ts` (+11 lines: AUTO `subTabs` array + `defaultSubTab: "dashboard"`) · `src/app/autotrader/page.tsx` (8 → 12 lines: `searchParams` reader → `<AutoZoneView subtab={tab ?? "dashboard"}>`) · `src/components/ui/index.ts` (+18 lines: 6 new barrel exports). **(deleted, 2):** `src/components/autotrader-v2/scalper-view.tsx` (Dashboard tab supersedes; zero remaining consumers) · `src/components/autotrader-v2/recent-trades-card.tsx` (Recent tab absorbs its rendering; zero remaining consumers).
- **Verified:** `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0. `npm run build` clean — `/autotrader` route **12.3 kB** (was 7.98 kB at B4 + B1c; +4.3 kB for the 11 new component files balanced against the 2 deletions). Service restart + journal monitor + live smoke happen below at Phase 5. Sacred 9/9 untouched (Hub never touches bot files; only `src/components/` + `src/lib/` + `src/app/autotrader/page.tsx` modified).

Bot files / brain / sacred / Python helpers. Other zones / global nav chrome / chat surface. No new npm deps. No new design tokens. No flag flips.

### 2026-05-27 — B1c: 6 new API routes for config/control/activity/shadow + LIVE_EDIT_ENABLED gate (cross-repo with bot main)
- **Cross-repo wave with bot `main`.** Bot side adds `LIVE_EDIT_ENABLED='false'` to `auto_trader/config.py` DEFAULTS (Phase 3) + 2 documentation files. Hub side ships all 6 new endpoints + 6 Python helpers + the new `LIVE_EDIT_ENABLED` write-gate.
- **6 new endpoints (4 GET / 2 PATCH).** All auth-gated by the existing middleware cookie session. PATCH endpoints additionally gated by `auto_config.LIVE_EDIT_ENABLED` → HTTP 423 Locked when false. Default ships `false`.
  - `GET /api/auto/config-full` → `query_config_full.py`. Returns the 60 non-boolean `auto_config` rows categorized per A1 §2 (Capital · Signal · Exit · Risk · Per-Ticker · Calibration · Execution · Misc) with type inference (int/float/json/str). 7 keys flagged `immutable=true`: `LIVE_HARD_CAPITAL_CAP_USD` (Ghost ruling — inviolable $50 cap), `EMERGENCY_KILLSWITCH_LAST_TOGGLE/AUTHOR/REASON` (system counters), `ANTHROPIC_API_DAILY_RESET_DATE/TOKENS_USED` (budget tracker counters), `DISCOVERED_TICKERS` (autonomous discovery output). These are read-only from the UI even when LIVE_EDIT_ENABLED=true.
  - `PATCH /api/auto/config-full/[key]` → `write_config_value.py`. Body `{value, author?}`. Helper exit codes map to HTTP 0→200 (success or idempotent no-op), 1→400 (immutable / boolean-shape / dedicated / typo), 3→423 (gate locked). Writes via `audit_config_write()` (B1b helper) BEFORE the INSERT OR REPLACE so the change_log captures the transition even if the write fails. Refuses boolean-shaped values + boolean-shaped existing rows (caller mis-routing a toggle through the config endpoint).
  - `GET /api/auto/control-full` → `query_control_full.py`. Returns the 89 boolean-valued `auto_config` rows categorized per A1 §3 (Execution · Signal Gates · Critic Stack · Calibration · Risk · Experimental · Misc) with sensitivity tier per A1 §9. Current tier counts: Tier 1 (destructive, 2-tap+reason in UI) = 7 incl. `LIVE_EDIT_ENABLED` (the toggle that opens the write surface itself); Tier 2 (sensitive, 1-tap+warning) = 25; Tier 3 (free toggle) = 57.
  - `PATCH /api/auto/control-full/[key]` → `write_control_value.py`. Body `{value, author?, reason?}`. Validates `value ∈ {true,false}`. **Refuses 4 dedicated-endpoint keys** with a 400 pointing to the correct endpoint: `AUTO_TRADER_ENABLED` → `/api/memory/autotrader-toggle`, `EMERGENCY_KILLSWITCH` → `/api/killswitch` (cache bust required), `LIVE_PARTIALS_ENABLED` → `/api/auto/partials-toggle`, `CONFIRM_CYCLES_PROMOTED` → `/api/auto/exit-controls`. The aggressive toggle (`aggressive_mode_config.enabled`) lives in a separate table and is also routed via its own `/api/memory/aggressive` endpoint.
  - `GET /api/auto/activity?limit=&offset=&actor=&source_type=&key=&table_name=` → `query_activity.py`. Paginated B1b change_log readout, newest first. limit clamped to [1, 500] (default 50). All filters optional (use empty string or `*` as wildcard). At deploy the table has 1 row — the autonomous `DISCOVERED_TICKERS` update captured between B1b commit and B1c deploy, proving the audit logger is live in production without a service restart.
  - `GET /api/shadow/registry` → `query_shadow_registry.py`. 25-table inventory grouped by function (Entry 7 · Exit 8 · Scoring 5 · Risk 3 · Data 2) with the same ACTIVE/DORMANT/BROKEN classifier `query_shadow_status.py` uses (rows==0+expected_active → BROKEN; latest_write < 7d → ACTIVE; else DORMANT). Current state: 20 ACTIVE / 5 DORMANT / 0 BROKEN. **TABLE_DEFS re-declared independently** (no import dependency on `query_shadow_status.py`) — the existing Intel-tab grid stays the deep-dive surface with per-table key_stat helpers; this endpoint is the lighter, function-grouped roll-up for D4.
- **Helper script conventions.** All 6 helpers live in `/home/trevor/trevor-dashboard/` (per `DASHBOARD_DIR=process.cwd()` in `api-helpers.ts:6`). `runPython` runs them with `cwd=TREVOR_DIR=/home/trevor/trevor` so `audit_logger` resolves. The 2 PATCH helpers add `sys.path.insert(0, "/home/trevor/trevor")` at the top (mirrors `set_killswitch.py:38`) for explicit safety on direct invocation. Read helpers use SQLite `mode=ro` URI. PATCH helpers wrap audit calls in try/except for fail-open per B1b doctrine.
- **Route file conventions.** Match the B3 `exit-controls/route.ts` gold standard: GET via `runPython` + try/catch returning 200-with-error-in-payload (fail-soft for read endpoints); PATCH via `spawnSync` direct (NOT `runPython`) so non-zero exit codes don't throw — lets the helper signal gate-locked (exit 3 → 423) and bad-input (exit 1 → 400) via exit code. Argv-only invocation per Rule 26 (no shell, no interpolation). `runtime = "nodejs"` + `dynamic = "force-dynamic"` on all 6.
- **`feature-flags.ts` NOT modified.** Per Ghost ruling at Phase 0 gate: the `FlagName` union is scoped to `HUB_REDESIGN_*` UI rollout flags; `LIVE_EDIT_ENABLED` is a backend write-gate and is read directly from `auto_config` by the PATCH helpers. Cleaner separation than overloading the redesign-flag enum.
- **Smoke verified end-to-end:** all 6 read helpers emit valid JSON; both PATCH helpers correctly return exit 3 → 423 when LIVE_EDIT_ENABLED=false; immutable + dedicated key paths return exit 1 → 400 with correct error messages; valid edits + flips (with the gate flipped on briefly) returned exit 0 → 200 plus change_log rows. All test rows cleaned. `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0. Build + service restart deferred to Phase 5 (paired with `trevor.service` restart — first Wave-B service restart, picks up B1a + B1b + B1c together).
- **Hub `master` commit scope:** 6 new Python helpers · 6 new `src/app/api/.../route.ts` files · `CLAUDE.md` (this entry). **Bot `main` commit (paired):** `auto_trader/config.py` (+13 lines DEFAULTS), `CLAUDE.md`, `BEHAVIOR_RULES.md`.

### 2026-05-27 — Exit Engine Controls card on /autotrader (B3: Momentum Confirm Cycles toggle)
- **New "Exit Engine Controls" card at the bottom of `/autotrader`.** Single Hub write surface for `auto_config.CONFIRM_CYCLES_PROMOTED` — the bot-side B3 flag that gates Layer 6 momentum EXIT on a confirmation window (require N consecutive sub-threshold readings, with N read from the existing `MOMENTUM_EXIT_CONFIRM_CYCLES` setting, default 2). Single-tap toggle (no BottomSheet — not destructive, just a flag flip with hot-reload; cfg_bool on the bot side has no cache so changes apply at the next monitor cycle ~30s without a service restart). Status pill ARMED (mint, `intent="active"`) when ON, OFF (gold, `intent="warn"`) when off. Helper text "When ARMED, Layer 6 momentum EXIT requires N consecutive sub-threshold readings before firing — gives other exit layers (stale, timeout) time to activate."
- **3 new files + 2 surgical edits.** New `src/components/autotrader-v2/exit-controls-card.tsx` (~180 lines; refined A4 v1.1 styling — `card-elevated`, `Sliders` icon, font-sans labels, `Pill intent="active|warn"`, `HapticButton variant="primary|secondary"`, success/error result banner with `border-accent-mint/40` / `border-accent-red/40`). New `src/app/api/auto/exit-controls/route.ts` (GET + POST; mirrors `/api/memory/autotrader-toggle` line-for-line — `spawnSync` directly on POST so exit code 3 surfaces as HTTP 423 for the gate-locked case). Two new repo-root Python helpers: `query_confirm_cycles.py` (cloned from `query_autotrader_enabled.py`; reads `CONFIRM_CYCLES_PROMOTED` + `MOMENTUM_EXIT_CONFIRM_CYCLES` + gate flag + last 5 `confirm_cycles_*` audit rows; emits one JSON object on stdout) and `set_confirm_cycles_promoted.py` (cloned from `set_autotrader_enabled.py`; gates on `HUB_CONFIRM_CYCLES_TOGGLE_ENABLED`, exit codes 0/1/2/3 → HTTP 200/400/500/423). Mounted via one new import + one new JSX line at the bottom of `src/components/autotrader-v2/scalper-view.tsx` after `<AutoTraderToggleCard />`. `src/components/autotrader-v2/autotrader-toggle-card.tsx` `TOGGLE_DESCRIPTIONS` map extended with `confirm_cycles_on` + `confirm_cycles_off` entries so the description line on the existing card's "Recent Toggles" list renders properly when the new toggle fires (audit table is shared — per Ghost ruling at B3 Phase 0).
- **Audit table reuse.** Per Ghost ruling, the new setter writes to the existing `autotrader_state_audit` table with `action='confirm_cycles_on|off'` and `source='hub:<author>'`. Fewer moving parts than a new table, and the flip surfaces in `AutoTraderToggleCard`'s "Recent Toggles" list (which queries the same table unfiltered) for free with no extra UI code. The new card's "Last change" line filters to `confirm_cycles_*` events only via the query script's `WHERE action IN (...)` clause.
- **Gate-locked path.** `HUB_CONFIRM_CYCLES_TOGGLE_ENABLED` was seeded `'true'` by the bot-side Phase 1 commit (mirrors the `HUB_AUTOTRADER_TOGGLE_ENABLED` precedent so the toggle isn't locked on first use). If the flag is ever flipped `false`, the Hub surface degrades gracefully: `<Pill intent="warn">OFF</Pill>` stays, "Toggle locked" gold banner appears, the Enable/Disable button disables, and any POST that slips through returns 423 with a clear message.
- **`runPython` call site.** `query_confirm_cycles.py` runs through the standard `runPython` helper (`spawnSync`, argv-only, no shell — Rule 26 safe). The POST path uses `spawnSync` directly (not `runPython`) so non-zero exit codes don't throw — gate-lock exit 3 needs to surface its JSON payload while mapping to HTTP 423.
- **Verification.** `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0. `next build` clean. New route `/api/auto/exit-controls` 321 B in the build output. `/autotrader` route 7.14 → 7.98 kB (+0.84 kB for the new card + the 4-key `TOGGLE_DESCRIPTIONS` additions). Live `query_confirm_cycles.py` smoke (via bot venv direct invocation) returns `{promoted:false, confirm_cycles:2, toggle_enabled:true, shadow_enabled:true, audit:[]}` — initial state correct. **Service restart deferred** — never restarted from inside the worktree workflow; defer to Ghost approval as is the standard pattern for Hub waves.
- **Untouched on purpose:** `src/app/api/auto/config/route.ts` (read-only by design — the new toggle gets its own scoped endpoint instead of widening that route's contract). `set_autotrader_enabled.py` / `query_autotrader_enabled.py` (the new helpers are clones, not extensions). Existing `AutoTraderToggleCard` POST path / GET path / BottomSheet (only the `TOGGLE_DESCRIPTIONS` map was widened; toggle behavior byte-identical). No bot file under `/home/trevor/trevor/` touched FROM the Hub repo — bot-side B3 Phase 1 landed independently on the `feat/B3-confirm-cycles` worktree branch.
- **Hub `master` commit scope:** `query_confirm_cycles.py` (new) · `set_confirm_cycles_promoted.py` (new) · `src/app/api/auto/exit-controls/route.ts` (new) · `src/components/autotrader-v2/exit-controls-card.tsx` (new) · `src/components/autotrader-v2/scalper-view.tsx` (+2 lines) · `src/components/autotrader-v2/autotrader-toggle-card.tsx` (+6 lines TOGGLE_DESCRIPTIONS) · `CLAUDE.md` (this entry).

### 2026-05-26 — Intel Shadow tab expanded — inventory cards for all shadow tables
- **`/intel?tab=shadow` now shows the existing Shadow Scoring card plus per-table cards for every other shadow table in `trevor.db`.** Sections grouped by status — Active (17) at top, Broken (red, hidden when empty), Dormant (6) in a `<CollapsibleSection>` collapsed by default. 4-up grid on `xl`, 3-up on `lg`, 2-up on `sm`, 1-up on mobile. Auto-refresh every 60 s via `setInterval` inside the existing fetch effect.
- **Status is data-driven, not hard-coded.** `rows == 0 && expected_active → BROKEN`; `rows == 0 && !expected_active → DORMANT`; `latest_write < 7d → ACTIVE`; else `DORMANT`. The classifier auto-promoted `shadow_scorer_v2` to ACTIVE (4046 rows, latest write < 1 min ago) — the P01 fix has landed, so the prompt's stale "BROKEN" assumption is no longer true. Currently 17 ACTIVE / 0 BROKEN / 6 DORMANT.
- **`query_shadow_status.py` extended (+393 lines).** The original `shadow` payload is byte-identical (still drives the detailed Shadow Scoring card). New `tables: ShadowTableInfo[]` array added alongside. Each of the 23 inventoried tables has its own `key_stat_fn` — ALO fill/timeout/reject split, calibrator_v2 divergence %, calibrator_v1 avg/min/max delta, double-filter pass/block %, funding total USD, gap-watchdog cycles/alerts, leverage-v2 gates-all-pass %, meme/whale rows/24h, momentum-exit V2 hold/exit/confirm, per-ticker-cap divergence, regime-gate blocked/divergent, sizing v1-vs-v2 avg, slippage avg bps, threshold-recalibration `N/30` gate progress, time-gate shadow blocks/passes, scorer-v2 total. `shadow_scoring` itself is intentionally excluded from the grid (the dedicated card above already covers it).
- **Timestamp handling — three SQLite shapes supported.** `ts_kind="iso"` covers naive `YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS` (most tables, treated as UTC) AND offset-aware ISO `YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SS.fff±HH:MM` (calibrator_audit + calibration_v2_audit emit this); `_parse_ts()` routes both through `datetime.fromisoformat()` and normalizes to naive UTC. `ts_kind="unix"` (meme_onchain_shadow_log + whale_source_shadow_log) reads `ts` as integer epoch seconds — `latest_write` rendered via `datetime(ts, 'unixepoch')` and the 48 h window filtered via `CAST(strftime('%s', 'now', '-2 days') AS INTEGER)`. `datetime.now(timezone.utc).replace(tzinfo=None)` used instead of deprecated `.utcnow()`.
- **Defensive per-table.** Each table's inspection runs inside its own try/except — missing tables surface as `{status: "BROKEN" if expected else "DORMANT", error: "table not found"}` instead of crashing the API. key_stat failures degrade to `key_stat={}` + an `error` string on the card. The API route's outer try/catch (unchanged) still returns `{shadow:{}, tables:[], error}` with 200 on a top-level failure.
- **New component `src/components/intel/shadow-table-card.tsx`** (~130 lines, `"use client"`). One reusable `<ShadowTableCard info={ShadowTableInfo}/>` that takes a single inventory row and renders display name + status pill in the header, big rows count, 48 h count + relative `latest_write` (`fmtAge` returns `s/m/h/d ago`), then up to 4 key-value pairs from `key_stat`. Status drives both the card class (`card-elevated` / `card-warn` / `card-base opacity-75`) and the `<Pill>` (`intent="active"` / `intent="error"` / `intent="warn"` / `tone="neutral"`). `fmtAge` appends `Z` to naive SQLite timestamps so the browser parses them as UTC, not local; raw value in `title` tooltip.
- **`src/components/intel/shadow-section.tsx` extended (+~80 lines, existing Shadow Scoring card byte-identical).** Added the active/broken/dormant section render below the existing card, plus the 60 s auto-refresh. New `ShadowResponse` shape adds optional `tables?: ShadowTableInfo[]`. Loading state: dedicated Skeleton for the grid while the first fetch completes (the original card has its own loading branch unchanged). Broken section conditionally renders only when non-empty; Dormant uses the existing `<CollapsibleSection>` primitive with `defaultOpen={false}`.
- **Verified live post-restart.** `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0; `next build` clean — `/intel` route 7.82 → 7.88 kB (+60 B for the wiring). `trevor-dashboard.service` restarted 2026-05-26 14:10:58 UTC PID 584096 → `[HUB] TREVOR Hub ready on http://127.0.0.1:3333` in 4 s, 0 boot errors / warnings (`journalctl -u trevor-dashboard.service --since "2 minutes ago" | grep -iE 'error|warning|fail|fatal|exception'` returned empty). Cookie-auth probes: `GET /api/intel/shadow` 200 → `shadow.rows=21695`, `tables=23`, `{ACTIVE: 17, DORMANT: 6}`. `GET /intel?tab=shadow` 200 (27185 B; up from 27145 — new component bytes; section headings hydrate client-side). Sample tables — momentum_exit_shadow `{v2_hold:20, v2_exit:147, confirm_exit:10}`, meme_onchain_shadow_log `{total:30599, rows_24h:969}`, shadow_scorer_v2 `{total:4066}` — all key_stat shapes correct end-to-end.
- **Untouched on purpose:** existing Shadow Scoring card, `src/app/api/intel/shadow/route.ts` (the new payload rides through the existing pass-through), every bot file under `/home/trevor/trevor/`, `trevor.db` schema (read-only), no new npm deps. The existing Optuna A/B removal (commit `b45c00e`, also 2026-05-26) stays — Shadow Scoring + the new inventory grid are the only Intel-shadow surfaces now.
- **Hub `master` commit `1d3ac9e` scope:** `query_shadow_status.py` · `src/components/intel/shadow-section.tsx` · `src/components/intel/shadow-table-card.tsx` (new) · `CLAUDE.md` (this entry + component-map row updated to `intel/ × 4` files). Bot side untouched.

### 2026-05-22 — Docs category reorder (UP/DOWN arrows) + Uncategorized moved to LAST
- **Phase 0 finding:** the reorder backend was fully shipped in Wave A1 (2026-05-19) — `categories.json` carries `sort_order` per row, `download_manager.reorder_categories()` exists, `PUT /api/docs/categories/reorder` accepts `{category_ids: [...]}` and persists, `category-tabs.tsx` already sorts by `sort_order`. Only the UI control was missing (Wave B2 explicitly deferred "drag-reorder intentionally NOT implemented — no DnD library installed"). Sacred 12/12 OK at entry + exit.
- **Approach: arrow buttons, not drag.** Hub `package.json` has no DnD library and the QUICK time budget (5–10 min) doesn't justify adding one. UP/DOWN arrows work natively on iOS Safari touch, are keyboard- and screen-reader-accessible, and don't risk the HTML5-drag-on-mobile failure mode (mobile browsers ignore `draggable` outside of long-press-to-drag-on-text contexts). Honesty: the prompt's commit-message template says "draggable" — shipped as arrows; actual commit message reflects what shipped.
- **`src/components/docs/category-settings-sheet.tsx` (+~95 lines).** Adds 4 new pieces of state to the existing CRUD sheet: `optimisticOrder` (the in-flight id list during a reorder round-trip), `reordering` (in-flight flag), `reorderError` (top-of-modal error banner). New `displayCategories` memo applies the optimistic order over the server-sorted list and falls back to the server order if the id-set diverges (e.g. add/delete completes during a reorder). New `submitReorder(catId, delta: -1 | 1)` swaps adjacent rows, sets optimistic state, PUTs `/api/docs/categories/reorder` with `{category_ids: [...]}`, calls `onCategoriesChanged()` on success, snaps back + surfaces error on failure. New effect clears the optimistic snapshot once the server's `sort_order` order matches the optimistic order (avoids stale state). Per-row UI adds a vertical `[↑]/[↓]` pair (lucide `ArrowUp`/`ArrowDown` at size=12) before the rename button — disabled at boundaries and during any reorder round-trip. Hidden in editing mode (no overlap with the rename input). Uncategorized's locked system row never gets arrows.
- **`src/components/docs/category-tabs.tsx` (3-line behavior change).** Uncategorized moved from FIRST to LAST in the tabs `items` array. Updates the inline comment to reflect the 2026-05-22 supersede of the 2026-05-20 first-tab preference. No primitive change, no prop change, no design-token change — `<TabBar>` consumes the new order transparently. `displayCategories` (in the settings sheet) continues to render the API categories only — Uncategorized is locked-and-rendered-last in the settings UI by the existing manual `<li>` block at the bottom.
- **B2 wave entry's deferral note is now obsolete.** The line "Drag-reorder intentionally NOT implemented this wave — no drag-and-drop library is installed" in `category-settings-sheet.tsx`'s top-of-file docblock has been rewritten to record the 2026-05-22 reorder addition.
- **Smoke verified live:** authed `PUT /api/docs/categories/reorder` with `{category_ids: ["audits","reports","deep-for-rms","monitor-center"]}` → `{success: true}`; GET reflects new `sort_order` (1,2,3,4 — sparse 1,2,4,5 normalized to dense by the Python layer). Restored original Ghost-set order with a second PUT. `npx tsc --noEmit` clean, `npm run build` clean (`/docs` route 6.78 → 7.29 kB, +0.51 kB for optimistic state + arrows + reorder fetch), `trevor-dashboard.service` restarted 2026-05-22 20:51:25 UTC → `[HUB] TREVOR Hub ready` in 2 s, 0 boot errors / warnings. SSR for /docs returns 200; category labels load client-side after hydration (per Wave B1 design — `useEffect` fetch of `/api/docs/categories`), so SSR HTML scan for label order is expected to be empty — verified API+sort logic instead.
- **Sacred 9/9 byte-identical** to entry baseline (manifest unchanged: `download_manager.py` not touched — the existing `reorder_categories()` API was sufficient).
- **Files modified:** `src/components/docs/category-settings-sheet.tsx`, `src/components/docs/category-tabs.tsx`, `CLAUDE.md` (this entry). Bot-side: a one-line `## Preferences` flip ("Uncategorized FIRST" → "Uncategorized LAST") in `/home/trevor/trevor/CLAUDE.md`. No `download_manager.py` change, no DB migration, no API-route change.

### 2026-05-21 — Hub Design Foundation (refined tokens + Geist + tab fade + card/pill primitives)
- **Foundation-only wave.** No page-level redesigns; only tokens, primitives, and the tab overflow fix land here. B1/B2/C1 prompts adopt the refined family page-by-page. Additive across the board — every existing A4 v1 token (`--color-accent-cyan` and the rest) and every legacy `--neon-*`/`--glow-*` alias keeps working; this wave adds parallel siblings.
- **Phase 1 — Refined color tokens + glow system (done).** `src/app/globals.css @theme inline` extended with an A4 v1.1 block (sanctioned A4-revision): refined accents `--color-accent-cyan-soft` `#5fb4cc`, `--color-accent-plum` `#a067c4`, `--color-accent-mint` `#5fcc99`, `--color-accent-gold` `#d4a64a` (red intentionally unchanged — warnings stay clear) — each with `*-strong` (focus/active brightness) and `*-subtle` (rgba ~18-20% alpha for borders) siblings. Refined borders `--color-border-cyan-soft / -plum / -mint / -gold`. Subtle glow utilities `--shadow-glow-subtle / -active / -focus` (default cyan-soft) at ~30-40% of the original neon glow intensity, plus per-color subtle/active glows for each refined accent + red. Type token `--font-sans: var(--font-geist-sans), 'Geist', 'Inter', system-ui, sans-serif` wired so the Tailwind `font-sans` utility resolves to Geist once Phase 2 mounts it via `next/font`. New `src/styles/design-tokens.md` reference doc (palette + glow filled; type / cards / pills sections held open for Phase 2 / 4). `npm run build` clean; route sizes unchanged (no consumer adopts the new tokens this phase).
- **Phase 2 — Sans-serif type system (done).** `src/app/layout.tsx` imports `Geist` from `next/font/google` (`variable: "--font-geist-sans"`, `display: "swap"`) and hangs `geistSans.variable` on `<html>`. The `@theme inline` token `--font-sans` resolves through `var(--font-geist-sans)` → `'Geist'` → `'Inter'` → `system-ui`, so the auto-generated `font-sans` Tailwind utility renders Geist as soon as it's applied. **Body default stays mono** — Geist is opt-in. Refined type utilities added to `globals.css` (next to the existing A4 v1 type scale): `.text-label-ui` (11px / 500 / 0.075em uppercase — nav/tab/button), `.text-heading-ui` / `.text-h1-ui` / `.text-h2-ui` (semibold 600, tightened tracking — refined-heading set), `.text-prose-ui` (15px / line-height 1.6 — comfortable body prose), `.text-caption-ui`. Each forces `font-family: var(--font-sans)` so it overrides body's mono regardless of nesting. `design-tokens.md` Type system section filled (mono-vs-sans table + utility reference + conventions). `npm run build` clean; route sizes unchanged (no consumer adopts the new utilities yet).
- **Phase 3 — Tab primitive overflow fix (done).** `src/components/ui/tab-bar-v2.tsx` rewritten to attach a `scrollRef` to the scrollable container, watch `scrollLeft / scrollWidth / clientWidth` with a passive scroll listener + `ResizeObserver`, and apply a 28px **mask-image fade** on the leading/trailing edge whenever there's more content to scroll. Surface-agnostic — works inside `ZoneSubTabs` (sticky on `--color-bg-sidebar`) AND inside a `Card` (e.g. `CategoryTabs` in `/docs`) without needing a hardcoded bg color. The active tab indicator now uses **`accent-cyan-soft-strong`** (refined steel cyan) with `shadow-glow-subtle-cyan` instead of the original `accent-cyan` + heavy `shadow-glow-cyan` — ~30% of the previous glow intensity. Tap targets preserved via the existing `tap-target` (44px) + `px-3` (12px) padding. Prop API **unchanged** (`items`/`active`/`onChange`/`className?`), so `ZoneSubTabs` + `CategoryTabs` + `design-system` showcase consume the upgraded behavior with zero call-site edits. ### 2026-05-21 — Docs PDF download option (DOWNLOAD AS sheet)
- **`/docs` Downloads file cards: the Download button now opens a `<BottomSheet>` titled `DOWNLOAD AS`** with two rows — `Download .md` (cyan, the existing direct-link flow against `/api/intel/downloads/[filename]`) and `Download PDF` (red, on-demand server-side conversion via the new `/api/docs/downloads/[filename]/pdf` route). One tap on the cyan Download button opens the sheet; one more tap picks the format. The cyan/red accents match the existing `fileTypePillTone` convention (`.md` cyan, `.pdf` red) so the visual hint is consistent with the file-type pills on each card.
- **New `src/components/docs/download-format-sheet.tsx`** (~140 lines) — wraps the shared `<BottomSheet>` primitive (slide-up on mobile, centered modal on desktop, scrim + Escape + body-scroll-lock + sticky header ✕). Two `<HapticButton>` rows; per-row state mirrors the `MoveToSheet` pattern (idle → pending → error → 2 s `Failed` badge then revert; sheet stays open on PDF failure, auto-closes on success). PDF row pending state surfaces `"Converting… (up to ~60 s for large files)"` as the right-side hint — honest about worst-case latency.
- **New `src/app/api/docs/downloads/[filename]/pdf/route.ts`** (~75 lines) — `GET`; runs `runPython("convert_md_to_pdf.py", [filename], { timeout: 90_000 })`; parses JSON `{path, size, cached}` / `{error}`; serves PDF bytes via `readFileSync(result.path)` + `Content-Disposition: attachment; filename="<name>.pdf"` (`.md → .pdf` suffix swap). Status codes: 200 success · 400 invalid filename / non-`.md` / path-traversal · 404 not-found · 500 conversion error. Auth: middleware-global (no per-route code). Timeout bumped to 90 s (default 15 s) to absorb the worst-case 263 KB / 2400-table-row real document.
- **New Hub script `convert_md_to_pdf.py`** (~165 lines) — Python-Markdown → HTML → WeasyPrint pipeline. Extensions: `tables`, `fenced_code`, `codehilite` (Pygments `default` style), `sane_lists`, `attr_list`, `def_list`. CSS: DejaVu Serif body + DejaVu Sans Mono code, letter @page with 1″ margins, `@bottom-center` page numbers, filename + generation-date strip at the top, scaled heading hierarchy with rules under H1/H2, bordered + zebra-striped tables, light-gray-on-white code blocks. Content-addressed cache: `downloads/.pdf_cache/<sha256(filename + source mtime_ns)>.pdf` (atomic `.tmp` → rename). Always emits a single JSON object to stdout + exits 0; the route handler distinguishes `error: "not found"` → 404 from any other `error` → 500.
- **`src/components/docs/downloads-section.tsx`** — surgical additions: import `DownloadFormatSheet`; add `downloadSheetFile: string | null` state; change `handleDownload(filename)` to `setDownloadSheetFile(filename)` (was the direct `<a download>` link click); mount `<DownloadFormatSheet open={downloadSheetFile !== null} onClose={() => setDownloadSheetFile(null)} filename={downloadSheetFile}/>` at section root. The .md download flow is preserved verbatim — just moved inside the sheet's `handleMd` so the sheet hosts both formats. Download / Move / Delete card buttons + Move-to + category-settings sheets unchanged. `aria-label` on the now-multi-format button kept as the original "Download {filename}".
- **System deps installed once** (apt; one-time, root): `libpango-1.0-0`, `libpangoft2-1.0-0`, `libcairo2`, `libgdk-pixbuf-2.0-0`, `libharfbuzz0b`, `shared-mime-info`, `fonts-liberation`, `fonts-dejavu-core`. Bot venv (`/home/trevor/trevor/venv/bin/pip`): added `markdown 3.10.2` + `weasyprint 68.1` and their transitive deps (`Pillow`, `cssselect2`, `pydyf`, `pyphen`, `tinycss2`, `tinyhtml5`, `fonttools`, `brotli`, `zopfli`, `webencodings`). Pygments + Jinja2 already present.
- **Script-location note:** the prompt specified `/home/trevor/trevor/convert_md_to_pdf.py` (bot repo). Real path is `/home/trevor/trevor-dashboard/convert_md_to_pdf.py` (Hub repo) because `api-helpers.ts:23` resolves `scriptPath = join(DASHBOARD_DIR, script)` — a bot-side script can't be found by `runPython`. The script does run with `cwd = TREVOR_DIR = /home/trevor/trevor`, so `downloads/active/` and `downloads/.pdf_cache/` resolve to the bot's downloads tree. `download_manager.py` is not imported or modified.
- **Performance verified live** (authed cookie, restarted at 12:42:50 ET): cached small file 0.93 s · uncached 14 KB 2.7 s · 94 KB exhaustive research 9.4 s · 263 KB monitor report 42 s first render (worst real document on disk — 2400 table rows, 198 PDF pages) → sub-second cache hit re-fetch. The 42 s worst case is well under the 90 s route timeout; the UI surfaces honest "Converting… up to ~60 s for large files" copy while the request is in flight. CSS-optimization experiments (drop zebra-striping) saved only ~2 s on the worst case — the bottleneck is weasyprint laying out the tables, not CSS-selector cost.
- **Negative paths verified live:** 404 (`no_such_file.md`) · 400 path-traversal (`..%2Fsecret.md`) · 400 non-.md (`foo.txt`) · 401 unauth (no cookie) · `.md` download still 200 + `text/markdown; charset=utf-8` (regression check on `/api/intel/downloads/[filename]`).
- **Build + restart:** `npx tsc --noEmit` clean (0 errors); `next build` clean (`/docs` route 6.17 → 6.78 kB, +0.61 kB for the new sheet; new `/api/docs/downloads/[filename]/pdf` route in build output at 330 B). `trevor-dashboard.service` restarted 2026-05-21 12:42:50 UTC PID 2318730 → `[HUB] TREVOR Hub ready on http://127.0.0.1:3333` in 1 s, 0 boot errors / warnings. Service monitor armed throughout the smoke window — zero error/warning/fail/exception events fired.
- **Sacred 9/9 byte-identical to Phase 0 baseline** (`brain/IDENTITY.md` 62ee61b6… · `brain/BRAIN.md` d35df71e… · `brain/SOUL.md` 785274ba… · `brain/AGENTS.md` a881531f… · `swarms_brain.py` 8841496a… · `training_bridge.py` 584f32c1… · `signal_guard.py` 58b4789f… · `signal_cooldown.py` c3c77715… · `signal_cleanup.py` d5c2db77…). `hooks/guard_recurring_bugs.sh` 15/15 PASS (incl. Bug #15 downloads auto-delete still disabled).
- **`.gitignore` deviation:** the prompt directed adding `downloads/.pdf_cache/` to the bot repo `.gitignore`. The bot repo already gitignores the whole `downloads/` tree at line 69, so the cache subfolder is covered without further edit. No `.gitignore` change made.

State shape `interface Note { id: string; text: string; createdAt: string }`; persistence keyed `localStorage["trevor-hub-notes"]`; SSR-safe (loads in a hydration `useEffect`, never writes until after the bootstrap read). No API call, no Python bridge, no DB. UI: a `Card`-wrapped 4-row textarea with cyan-focus border (placeholder `"jot something down..."`) + a terminal-green `+ ADD NOTE` `tap-target` button (44×44 enforced) using the `bg-accent-green/10 / text-accent-green / border-accent-green/40` token pattern; Ctrl/⌘+Enter submits. Below: an `<EmptyState title="No notes yet" …/>` when empty, else a newest-first `<ul>` of `Card`-wrapped note rows — `whitespace-pre-wrap` text, relative timestamp (`just now` / `Ns/m/h/d ago` / `Mon D` / `Mon D, YYYY` cross-year) and a small ✕ delete button (`tap-target -m-2`, red on hover, no confirm). `crypto.randomUUID()` IDs with a `Date.now()` fallback; `JSON.parse` wrapped in try/catch (corrupt → `[]`); `localStorage.setItem` wrapped in try/catch (quota / private-mode → in-memory only). No new design tokens, no new npm deps.

### 2026-05-19 — Docs category tabs frontend (Wave B1)
- **Frontend for the A1 category system.** The `/docs?tab=downloads` view now renders a category tab strip (**Reports · Audits · Monitor · Uncategorized**, categories in `sort_order` then Uncategorized last) with per-tab count badges, and the file list filters to the selected tab. The global zone strip (DOWNLOADS / LESSONS / JOURNAL) is unchanged — Lessons and Journal are unaffected.
- **New `src/components/docs/category-tabs.tsx`** — presentational; wraps the shared `<TabBar>` primitive (the same component `<ZoneSubTabs/>` uses), so the strip matches the Similar / Calibration / Shadow styling exactly: horizontal scroll, 44 px tap targets, cyan active underline, muted count-pill badges. Responsive negative margins extend `TabBar`'s built-in `-mx-4` bleed to the section's `md:p-6` / `lg:px-8` padding so the strip stays edge-to-edge at every breakpoint.
- **`src/components/docs/downloads-section.tsx`** — adds `categories` + `activeCategory` state, fetches `GET /api/docs/categories` once on mount (degrades to a lone Uncategorized tab if the route 404/500s — same defensive shape `LessonsSection` uses), derives per-tab file counts via `useMemo` over the existing listing, and filters the displayed list client-side. The status `SegmentedToggle` (All / Active / Archived) and every action handler (Download / Archive / Delete) are byte-identical to pre-B1. `DownloadFile.category_id` added to the interface (was already returned by the API since A1).
- **Single-fetch design** — one `/api/intel/downloads?status=…` call holds every file with its `category_id`, so tab switches filter in-memory with 0 network and no stale-response risk. The `?category=` server filter A1 added stays available (verified live below) but is unused by the UI — count badges already force a full client-side dataset, which would make a per-tab fetch redundant. Recommended over the prompt's per-tab `?category=` and Ghost-confirmed at the Phase 0 gate.
- **Default tab on first load** — the first category (by `sort_order`) that has files, else Uncategorized. Picked exactly once (a `categoryInitRef` guard) so later 60 s polls and status-toggle changes never yank the user off their selected tab. With every file currently `category_id=null`, the live default resolves to Uncategorized.
- **Untouched:** `src/lib/navigation.ts`, `<ZoneSubTabs/>`, `docs-zone-view.tsx`, `LessonsSection`, `JournalSection`, the Intel zone, every Python module under `/home/trevor/trevor/`. The category tabs are a second-level strip inside the Downloads sub-tab; the global zone tabs are unaffected.
- ⚠️ **Path note** (carry-over from A1): the prompt's stated component path (`src/components/intel/downloads-section.tsx`) is stale — the component moved to `src/components/docs/` in Wave D2. Real `docs/` path used.
- **Verified live post-deploy:** `npx tsc --noEmit` clean, `next build` clean, `trevor-dashboard.service` restarted 2026-05-19 21:19:55 UTC PID 1921309 → `[HUB] TREVOR Hub ready on http://127.0.0.1:3333` in 1 s, 0 boot errors / warnings. `GET /api/docs/categories` 200 (3 categories + `uncategorized_count=19`); `GET /api/intel/downloads?category=reports` 200 with `files: []`; `GET /api/intel/downloads` 200 returning 19 files all `category_id=null` (matches `manifest.json` state); `GET /docs?tab=downloads` 200 (cookie-auth). Sacred 9/9 byte-identical to Phase 0; `discord_bot.py` regression check unchanged (`signal_guard|cooldown|cleanup` refs = 20).
- Commit `ab6fc03` (Hub, `master`); this CLAUDE.md entry is a separate `docs:` commit. Bot side has no commit this wave (no Python touched).

### 2026-05-19 — Docs category system: backend + API routes (frontend deferred to B1)
- **Bot side** (`download_manager.py`, repo `trevor` / `main`) — new `downloads/categories.json` (folder defs: `id` slug · `name` · `sort_order` · `created_at`) and a `category_id` field on every `manifest.json` entry (`null` = Uncategorized; 19 existing entries backfilled). 8 new functions — `load_categories` / `save_categories` / `create_category` / `rename_category` / `delete_category` / `reorder_categories` / `move_file_to_category` / `list_downloads_by_category` — atomic tmp-rename writes + the existing module lock.
- **New dispatcher `query_docs_categories.py`** at the Hub repo root (beside `query_downloads.py`) — actions `categories-list` / `category-create` / `category-rename` / `category-delete` / `category-reorder` / `file-move` / `list-by-category`; always emits one JSON object and exits 0.
- **4 new API routes** under `src/app/api/docs/` — `categories/route.ts` (GET list + uncategorized_count, POST create), `categories/[id]/route.ts` (PUT rename, DELETE), `categories/reorder/route.ts` (PUT), `downloads/[filename]/move/route.ts` (PUT). Auth is `middleware.ts`-global — no per-route auth code, matching the existing downloads routes. Status codes 400 / 404 / 500.
- **`src/app/api/intel/downloads/route.ts`** extended — optional `?category=<id>` / `?category=uncategorized` filter (via `list-by-category`); no param → unchanged behavior. Every file in the response now carries `category_id`.
- Verified: `npx tsc --noEmit` clean; `query_docs_categories.py` exercised for all 7 actions + negative cases. **Live `curl` checks + the Hub build/restart are deferred to Wave B1** — this wave ships no frontend and triggers no restart. Bot `main` carries the `download_manager.py` / `categories.json` / `manifest.json` commit; Hub `master` carries the routes + dispatcher + this entry.

### 2026-05-19 — Downloads: delete button (confirm-to-delete + Discord cleanup)
- **New API route `src/app/api/intel/downloads/delete/route.ts`** — `POST {filename}`, mirrors `archive/route.ts` exactly: filename validation (rejects empty / `/` / `..`), `runPython("query_downloads.py", ["delete", filename])`, returns the result JSON — `200` success, `404` not-found, `400` bad input, `500` on a Python error.
- **`query_downloads.py`** — new `delete` action alongside `list` / `archive` / `unarchive` / `path`; calls bot-side `download_manager.delete_download()` and emits `{success, filename, discord_deleted, error}`. Touching `query_downloads.py` was required to mirror the archive route — the archive route routes through this dispatcher, it does not import `download_manager` directly; the change is purely additive (one `elif` branch).
- **`src/components/docs/downloads-section.tsx`** — each file card gains a red (`accent-red` / `#ff3366`) DELETE button beside ARCHIVE. Two-tap confirm: first tap → "CONFIRM DELETE" (pulsing, brighter red) armed for 3 s; a second tap within the window fires the delete; otherwise it reverts to "DELETE". A failure shows "FAILED" for 2 s, then reverts. On success the list refetches — the card disappears and the header stats (active / archived counts + total MB) refresh from server truth. Works for active and archived files. `HapticButton` supplies the 44 px tap target + `uppercase` rendering.
- ⚠️ The prompt's stated component path (`src/components/intel/downloads-section.tsx`) was stale — the component moved to `src/components/docs/` in Wave D2. The real `docs/` path was used.
- Verified: `tsc --noEmit` clean, `next build` clean; live (cookie-auth) — `GET /api/intel/downloads` 200, `POST …/delete` nonexistent → `404 {"success":false,"error":"not found"}`, missing / `..` filename → `400`. `trevor-dashboard.service` restarted 2026-05-19 12:30 UTC — `[HUB] ready on :3333`, 0 boot errors.
